    <script>
        // ================================================================

        const state = {
            pages: [],

            pageName: '我的主页',

            dialogCallback: null,

            defaultGlobalStyles: '',
            defaultGlobalScripts: '',
            templates: [],
            templateStorageKey: 'wochuangye_templates_v1',
            importCancel: false,
            taskName: '测试',
        };

        const canvas = document.getElementById('canvas');
        const propContent = document.getElementById('propContent');
        const propTypeLabel = document.getElementById('propTypeLabel');
        const codeArea = document.getElementById('codeArea');
        const codePanel = document.getElementById('code-panel');
        const codeEmpty = document.getElementById('codeEmpty');
        const codePanelTitle = document.getElementById('codePanelTitle');
        const pageTabs = document.getElementById('pageTabs');
        const toastContainer = document.getElementById('toast-container');
        const dialogOverlay = document.getElementById('dialog-overlay');
        const dialogTitle = document.getElementById('dialogTitle');
        const dialogBody = document.getElementById('dialogBody');
        const dialogCancel = document.getElementById('dialogCancel');
        const dialogConfirm = document.getElementById('dialogConfirm');
        const codeApplyBtn = document.getElementById('codeApplyBtn');
        const codeGlobalBtn = document.getElementById('codeGlobalBtn');
        const addPageBtn = document.getElementById('addPageBtn');
        const fileInput = document.getElementById('fileInput');
        const dataFileInput = document.getElementById('dataFileInput');
        const imageFileInput = document.getElementById('imageFileInput');
        const templateFileInput = document.getElementById('templateFileInput');

        const DEFAULT_GLOBAL_STYLES = '/* 在此添加全局 CSS */';
        const DEFAULT_GLOBAL_SCRIPTS = '// 在此添加全局 JavaScript';

        function escapeHtml(str) {
            if (!str) return '';
            const d = document.createElement('div');
            d.textContent = str;
            return d.innerHTML;
        }

        function uid() { return state.nextId++; }

        function getPage(id) { return state.pages.find(p => p.id === id); }

        function getCurrentPage() { return getPage(state.currentPageId); }

        function getComponent(id) {
            const page = getCurrentPage();
            if (!page) return null;
            let comp = page.components.find(c => c.id === id);
            if (comp) return comp;
            for (const c of page.components) {
                if (c.type === 'container' && c.children) {
                    const child = c.children.find(ch => ch.id === id);
                    if (child) return child;
                }
                if (c.type === 'pager' && c.props.pages) {
                    for (const pg of c.props.pages) {
                        if (pg.children) {
                            const child = pg.children.find(ch => ch.id === id);
                            if (child) return child;
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            return null;
        }

        function getTopLevelComponents() {
            const page = getCurrentPage();
            if (!page) return [];
            return page.components.filter(c => c.parentId === undefined || c.parentId === null);
        }

        function getContainerChildren(containerId) {
            const page = getCurrentPage();
            if (!page) return [];
            const container = page.components.find(c => c.id === containerId && c.type === 'container');
            if (!container) return [];
            return container.children || [];
        }

        function getTypeLabel(type, subtype) {
            const map = {
                'button': { default: '按钮' },
                'input': { text: '文本输入' },
                'message': { info: '消息触发器' },
                'effect': { fade: '淡入效果', slide: '滑动效果', bounce: '弹跳效果', rotate: '旋转效果' },
                'appbar': { top: '顶部应用栏' },
                'image': { default: '图片' },
                'fileinput': { default: '导入数据' },
                'filedownload': { default: '下载数据' },
                'custom': { default: '自定义组件' },
                'container': { default: '容器' },
                'counter': { default: '计数器' },
                'quiz': { default: '答题' },
                'textlabel': { default: '文字说明' },
                'pager': { default: '分页' },
                'radio': { default: '单选框' },
                'checkbox': { default: '多选框' },
                'select': { default: '下拉框' },
                'switch': { default: '转换开关' },
                'autostart': { default: '启动按钮' },
                'taskstatus': { default: '任务状态' }
            };
            return (map[type] && map[type][subtype]) || type;
        }

        function getTypeNames() {
            return {
                'button': { default: '按钮' },
                'input': { default: '输入框' },
                'message': { default: '消息' },
                'effect': { default: '效果' },
                'appbar': { default: '顶栏' },
                'image': { default: '图片' },
                'fileinput': { default: '导入数据' },
                'filedownload': { default: '下载数据' },

                'container': { default: '容器' },

                'radio': { default: '单选框' },

                'autostart': { default: '启动按钮' },

        function getActionLabel(type) {
            const map = {
                'none': '无动作',
                'dialog': '弹窗',
                'save': '保存',
                'download': '下载',
                'message': '消息弹出',
                'switchPage': '切换页面',
                'triggerButton': '触发按钮',
                'navigate': '跳转链接',
                'runPreview': '预览运行',

                'toggleState': '切换状态',
                'hideShow': '隐藏/显示',

                'replaceComponent': '替换组件',
                'quiz_submit': '交卷',
                'autoConfigFinish': '提交配置并启动',
                'autoSaveTaskConfig': '保存任务配置',
                'autoStartTask': '启动任务',
                'autoStopTask': '停止任务'
            };
            return map[type] || type;
        }

        function showToast(msg, duration = 1800) {
            const el = document.createElement('div');
            el.className = 'toast';
            el.textContent = msg;
            toastContainer.appendChild(el);
            requestAnimationFrame(() => el.classList.add('show'));
            setTimeout(() => {
                el.classList.remove('show');
                setTimeout(() => el.remove(), 400);
            }, duration);
        }
        window._showToast = showToast;

        function openDialog(config) {
            return new Promise((resolve) => {
                state.dialogCallback = config.onConfirm || null;
                dialogTitle.textContent = config.title || '提示';
                dialogBody.innerHTML = '';
                const content = config.content || '';
                if (typeof content === 'string') {
                    dialogBody.innerHTML = content;
                } else if (Array.isArray(content)) {
                    const type = config.type || 'radio';
                    const name = 'dialog_option_' + Date.now();
                    content.forEach((opt, idx) => {
                        const div = document.createElement('div');
                        div.className = 'option-item';
                        const input = document.createElement('input');
                        input.type = type;
                        input.name = name;
                        input.value = opt.value || opt;
                        input.id = 'opt_' + idx;
                        const label = document.createElement('label');
                        label.htmlFor = 'opt_' + idx;
                        label.textContent = opt.label || opt;
                        div.appendChild(input);
                        div.appendChild(label);
                        dialogBody.appendChild(div);
                    });
                } else if (content.type === 'input') {
                    const inp = document.createElement('input');
                    inp.type = content.inputType || 'text';
                    inp.placeholder = content.placeholder || '';
                    inp.className = 'dialog-input';
                    inp.style.cssText =
                        'width:100%;padding:5px 10px;border:1px solid #dadce0;border-radius:5px;font-size:13px;margin-top:4px;font-family:inherit;';
                    if (content.value) inp.value = content.value;
                    dialogBody.appendChild(inp);
                    dialogBody._input = inp;
                } else if (content.type === 'nameSettings') {
                    const taskLabel = document.createElement('label');
                    taskLabel.textContent = '任务名称';
                    taskLabel.style.cssText = 'display:block;font-weight:600;margin-top:6px;font-size:13px;';
                    const taskInput = document.createElement('input');
                    taskInput.type = 'text';
                    taskInput.placeholder = '例如：测试';
                    taskInput.className = 'dialog-input';
                    taskInput.style.cssText =
                        'width:100%;padding:5px 10px;border:1px solid #dadce0;border-radius:5px;font-size:13px;margin-top:4px;font-family:inherit;';
                    taskInput.value = content.taskName || '测试';
                    dialogBody.appendChild(taskLabel);
                    dialogBody.appendChild(taskInput);
                    dialogBody._taskInput = taskInput;

                    const fileLabel = document.createElement('label');
                    fileLabel.textContent = '文件名称';
                    fileLabel.style.cssText = 'display:block;font-weight:600;margin-top:10px;font-size:13px;';
                    const fileNameInput = document.createElement('input');
                    fileNameInput.type = 'text';
                    fileNameInput.placeholder = '例如：我的主页';
                    fileNameInput.className = 'dialog-input';
                    fileNameInput.style.cssText =
                        'width:100%;padding:5px 10px;border:1px solid #dadce0;border-radius:5px;font-size:13px;margin-top:4px;font-family:inherit;';
                    fileNameInput.value = content.fileName || '我的主页';
                    dialogBody.appendChild(fileLabel);
                    dialogBody.appendChild(fileNameInput);
                    dialogBody._fileNameInput = fileNameInput;

                    const pathHint = document.createElement('div');
                    pathHint.style.cssText = 'font-size:11px;color:#5f6368;margin-top:8px;line-height:1.5;word-break:break-all;';
                    pathHint.textContent = '保存路径：/storage/emulated/0/auto-editor/local/任务名称/assets/文件名称.htm';
                    dialogBody.appendChild(pathHint);
                } else if (content.type === 'customEditor') {
                    const nameLabel = document.createElement('label');
                    nameLabel.textContent = '组件名称';
                    nameLabel.style.cssText = 'display:block;font-weight:500;margin-top:6px;font-size:13px;';
                    const nameInput = document.createElement('input');
                    nameInput.type = 'text';
                    nameInput.placeholder = '输入组件名称';
                    nameInput.className = 'dialog-input';
                    nameInput.style.cssText =
                        'width:100%;padding:5px 10px;border:1px solid #dadce0;border-radius:5px;font-size:13px;margin-top:2px;font-family:inherit;';
                    if (content.name) nameInput.value = content.name;
                    dialogBody.appendChild(nameLabel);
                    dialogBody.appendChild(nameInput);
                    dialogBody._nameInput = nameInput;

                    const cssLabel = document.createElement('label');
                    cssLabel.textContent = 'CSS 样式';
                    cssLabel.style.cssText = 'display:block;font-weight:500;margin-top:8px;font-size:13px;';
                    const cssText = document.createElement('textarea');
                    cssText.placeholder = '/* 自定义 CSS */';
                    cssText.style.cssText =
                        'width:100%;padding:5px 10px;border:1px solid #dadce0;border-radius:5px;font-size:12px;font-family:monospace;margin-top:2px;height:60px;resize:vertical;';
                    if (content.css) cssText.value = content.css;
                    dialogBody.appendChild(cssLabel);
                    dialogBody.appendChild(cssText);
                    dialogBody._cssInput = cssText;

                    const jsLabel = document.createElement('label');
                    jsLabel.textContent = 'JavaScript 逻辑';
                    jsLabel.style.cssText = 'display:block;font-weight:500;margin-top:8px;font-size:13px;';
                    const jsText = document.createElement('textarea');
                    jsText.placeholder = '// 自定义 JS (点击时执行)';
                    jsText.style.cssText =
                        'width:100%;padding:5px 10px;border:1px solid #dadce0;border-radius:5px;font-size:12px;font-family:monospace;margin-top:2px;height:60px;resize:vertical;';
                    if (content.js) jsText.value = content.js;
                    dialogBody.appendChild(jsLabel);
                    dialogBody.appendChild(jsText);
                    dialogBody._jsInput = jsText;
                } else if (content.type === 'templateMain') {
                    dialogBody.innerHTML = `
                        <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:8px;">
                            <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;">
                                <button class="btn-confirm" id="templateSaveBtn" style="padding:4px 16px;border:none;border-radius:5px;font-size:13px;cursor:pointer;background:var(--primary);color:#fff;">💾 保存当前页面为模板</button>
                                <button class="btn-primary" id="templateManageBtn" style="padding:4px 16px;border:none;border-radius:5px;font-size:13px;cursor:pointer;background:#1a73e8;color:#fff;">📂 管理模板</button>
                                <button class="btn-warning" id="templateExportBtn" style="padding:4px 16px;border:none;border-radius:5px;font-size:13px;cursor:pointer;background:#fbbc04;color:#202124;">📤 导出全部模板</button>
                                <button class="btn-primary" id="templateImportBtn" style="padding:4px 16px;border:none;border-radius:5px;font-size:13px;cursor:pointer;background:#8e44ad;color:#fff;">📥 导入模板</button>
                            </div>
                            <div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--text-secondary);margin-top:4px;">当前已存储 <strong id="templateCountDisplay">0</strong> 个模板</div>
                        </div>
                    `;
                    setTimeout(() => {
                        const saveBtn = document.getElementById('templateSaveBtn');
                        const manageBtn = document.getElementById('templateManageBtn');
                        const exportBtn = document.getElementById('templateExportBtn');
                        const importBtn = document.getElementById('templateImportBtn');
                        const countDisplay = document.getElementById('templateCountDisplay');
                        if (countDisplay) countDisplay.textContent = state.templates.length;
                        if (saveBtn) saveBtn.addEventListener('click', () => { dialogOverlay.classList.remove(
                                'active');
                            showSaveTemplateDialog(); });
                        if (manageBtn) manageBtn.addEventListener('click', () => { dialogOverlay.classList.remove(
                                'active');
                            showManageTemplatesDialog(); });
                        if (exportBtn) exportBtn.addEventListener('click', () => { dialogOverlay.classList.remove(
                                'active');
                            exportAllTemplates(); });
                        if (importBtn) importBtn.addEventListener('click', () => { dialogOverlay.classList.remove(
                                'active');
                            triggerTemplateImport(); });
                    }, 50);
                } else if (content.type === 'templateManage') {
                    // 由函数动态渲染
                } else if (content.type === 'saveTemplate') {
                    // 由函数动态渲染
                } else if (content.type === 'quizEditor') {
                    // 答题编辑器
                    renderQuizEditor(content.comp);
                }
                const inp = dialogBody.querySelector('.dialog-input');
                if (inp) setTimeout(() => inp.focus(), 100);
                dialogOverlay.classList.add('active');
                const onCancel = () => {
                    dialogOverlay.classList.remove('active');
                    dialogCancel.removeEventListener('click', onCancel);
                    dialogConfirm.removeEventListener('click', onConfirm);
                    resolve(null);
                };
                const onConfirm = () => {
                    dialogOverlay.classList.remove('active');
                    dialogCancel.removeEventListener('click', onCancel);
                    dialogConfirm.removeEventListener('click', onConfirm);
                    const result = {};
                    const checked = dialogBody.querySelectorAll('input:checked');
                    if (checked.length > 0) {
                        result.values = Array.from(checked).map(el => el.value);
                        if (checked.length === 1) result.value = checked[0].value;
                    }
                    const inputEl = dialogBody._input || dialogBody.querySelector('.dialog-input');
                    if (inputEl) result.input = inputEl.value;
                    const nameInput2 = dialogBody._nameInput;
                    const cssInput2 = dialogBody._cssInput;
                    const jsInput2 = dialogBody._jsInput;
                    if (nameInput2) result.name = nameInput2.value;
                    if (cssInput2) result.css = cssInput2.value;
                    if (jsInput2) result.js = jsInput2.value;
                    if (dialogBody._taskInput) result.taskName = dialogBody._taskInput.value;
                    if (dialogBody._fileNameInput) result.fileName = dialogBody._fileNameInput.value;
                    resolve(result);
                    if (state.dialogCallback) {
                        state.dialogCallback(result);
                        state.dialogCallback = null;
                    }
                };
                dialogCancel.addEventListener('click', onCancel);
                dialogConfirm.addEventListener('click', onConfirm);
                const keyHandler = (e) => {
                    if (e.key === 'Escape') { onCancel();
                        document.removeEventListener('keydown', keyHandler); }
                    if (e.key === 'Enter') {
                        const inp2 = dialogBody.querySelector('.dialog-input');
                        if (inp2 && document.activeElement === inp2) { onConfirm();
                            document.removeEventListener('keydown', keyHandler); }
                    }
                };
                document.addEventListener('keydown', keyHandler);
            });
        }

        // ================================================================
        //  答题编辑器
        // ================================================================
        function renderQuizEditor(comp) {
            const p = comp.props;
            const questions = p.questions || [];
            let html = `
                <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:8px;">
                    <div class="prop-group">
                        <label>📝 标题</label>
                        <input type="text" id="quizTitle" value="${escapeHtml(p.title || '选择题')}" />
                    </div>
                    <div class="prop-group">
                        <label>📊 题目数量</label>
                        <input type="number" id="quizCount" value="${questions.length}" min="1" max="20" />
                        <button id="quizUpdateCount" style="margin-top:4px;padding:2px 12px;border:none;border-radius:4px;background:var(--primary);color:#fff;cursor:pointer;font-size:12px;">更新题目数量</button>
                    </div>
                    <div style="max-height:300px;overflow:auto;border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:5px;padding:6px;">
                        <div id="quizQuestionsList">
                            ${questions.map((q, idx) => `
                                <div style="border-bottom:1px solid #f0f0f0;padding:6px 0;">
                                    <div style="display:flex;gap:4px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap;">
                                        <span style="font-weight:600;font-size:13px;min-width:30px;">${idx+1}.</span>
                                        <input type="text" class="q-text-input" value="${escapeHtml(q.text)}" style="flex:1;padding:2px 6px;border:1px solid #dadce0;border-radius:4px;font-size:12px;" />
                                        <select class="q-type-select" style="padding:2px 6px;border:1px solid #dadce0;border-radius:4px;font-size:11px;">
                                            <option value="single" ${q.type==='single'?'selected':''}>单选</option>
                                            <option value="multi" ${q.type==='multi'?'selected':''}>多选</option>
                                        </select>
                                        <button class="q-del-btn" style="padding:1px 8px;border:none;border-radius:4px;background:#ea4335;color:#fff;cursor:pointer;font-size:11px;">✕</button>
                                    </div>
                                    <div style="padding-left:30px;margin-top:4px;">
                                        ${q.options.map((opt, oi) => `
                                            <div style="display:flex;gap:4px;align-items:center;padding:2px 0;">
                                                <span style="font-size:12px;min-width:20px;">${String.fromCharCode(65+oi)}.</span>
                                                <input type="text" class="q-opt-input" value="${escapeHtml(opt)}" style="flex:1;padding:2px 6px;border:1px solid #dadce0;border-radius:4px;font-size:12px;" />
                                                <label style="font-size:11px;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:2px;">
                                                    <input type="checkbox" class="q-correct-check" ${q.correct.includes(oi)?'checked':''} />
                                                    正确
                                                </label>
                                            </div>
                                        `).join('')}
                                        <button class="q-add-opt-btn" style="margin-top:2px;padding:1px 10px;border:1px dashed #dadce0;border-radius:4px;background:transparent;cursor:pointer;font-size:11px;color:var(--text-secondary);">+ 添加选项</button>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                            `).join('')}
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    <button id="quizAddQuestion" style="padding:4px 12px;border:1px dashed var(--primary);border-radius:4px;background:transparent;cursor:pointer;color:var(--primary);font-size:12px;">➕ 添加题目</button>
                    <div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--text-secondary);">💡 单选题默认仅一个正确选项，多选题默认为两个正确选项</div>
                </div>
            `;
            dialogBody.innerHTML = html;

            // 绑定事件
            setTimeout(() => {
                // 更新题目数量
                const countBtn = document.getElementById('quizUpdateCount');
                if (countBtn) {
                    countBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
                        const count = parseInt(document.getElementById('quizCount').value) || 1;
                        updateQuizQuestions(comp, count);
                        renderQuizEditor(comp);
                    });
                }

                // 添加题目
                const addBtn = document.getElementById('quizAddQuestion');
                if (addBtn) {
                    addBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
                        const qs = comp.props.questions || [];
                        const newId = qs.length > 0 ? Math.max(...qs.map(q => q.id)) + 1 : 1;
                        qs.push({
                            id: newId,
                            text: '新题目',
                            options: ['选项A', '选项B', '选项C', '选项D'],
                            correct: [0],
                            type: 'single'
                        });
                        comp.props.questions = qs;
                        renderQuizEditor(comp);
                    });
                }

                // 删除题目
                document.querySelectorAll('.q-del-btn').forEach((btn, idx) => {
                    btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
                        const qs = comp.props.questions || [];
                        qs.splice(idx, 1);
                        comp.props.questions = qs;
                        renderQuizEditor(comp);
                    });
                });

                // 添加选项
                document.querySelectorAll('.q-add-opt-btn').forEach((btn, idx) => {
                    btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
                        const qs = comp.props.questions || [];
                        if (qs[idx]) {
                            const newOpt = '选项' + String.fromCharCode(65 + qs[idx].options.length);
                            qs[idx].options.push(newOpt);
                            renderQuizEditor(comp);
                        }
                    });
                });

                // 保存标题
                const titleInput = document.getElementById('quizTitle');
                if (titleInput) {
                    titleInput.addEventListener('input', () => {
                        comp.props.title = titleInput.value;
                    });
                }

                // 保存题目文本、类型、选项、正确标记
                const qItems = document.querySelectorAll('#quizQuestionsList > div');
                qItems.forEach((item, idx) => {
                    const qs = comp.props.questions || [];
                    if (!qs[idx]) return;
                    const q = qs[idx];

                    const textInput = item.querySelector('.q-text-input');
                    if (textInput) {
                        textInput.addEventListener('input', () => {
                            q.text = textInput.value;
                        });
                    }

                    const typeSelect = item.querySelector('.q-type-select');
                    if (typeSelect) {
                        typeSelect.addEventListener('change', () => {
                            q.type = typeSelect.value;
                            // 如果是单选，确保只有一个正确选项
                            if (q.type === 'single') {
                                const corrects = [];
                                const checkboxes = item.querySelectorAll('.q-correct-check');
                                checkboxes.forEach((cb, ci) => {
                                    if (ci === 0) cb.checked = true;
                                    else cb.checked = false;
                                    if (cb.checked) corrects.push(ci);
                                });
                                q.correct = corrects.length > 0 ? [0] : [0];
                            }
                            renderQuizEditor(comp);
                        });
                    }

                    const optInputs = item.querySelectorAll('.q-opt-input');
                    optInputs.forEach((inp, oi) => {
                        inp.addEventListener('input', () => {
                            if (q.options[oi] !== undefined) {
                                q.options[oi] = inp.value;
                            }
                        });
                    });

                    const correctChecks = item.querySelectorAll('.q-correct-check');
                    correctChecks.forEach((cb, oi) => {
                        cb.addEventListener('change', () => {
                            if (q.type === 'single') {
                                // 单选：清除其他选项的正确标记
                                correctChecks.forEach((c, ci) => {
                                    if (ci !== oi) c.checked = false;
                                });
                                q.correct = [oi];
                            } else {
                                // 多选：收集所有选中的
                                const checked = [];
                                correctChecks.forEach((c, ci) => {
                                    if (c.checked) checked.push(ci);
                                });
                                q.correct = checked;
                            }
                        });
                    });
                });

                // 确认按钮保存所有更改
                const confirmBtn = document.getElementById('dialogConfirm');
                if (confirmBtn) {
                    confirmBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
                        comp.props.questions = comp.props.questions || [];
                        refreshComponent(comp);
                        updatePropertiesPanel();
                        autoSave();
                        showToast('✅ 答题已更新');
                    });
                }

            }, 50);
        }

        function updateQuizQuestions(comp, count) {
            const qs = comp.props.questions || [];
            while (qs.length < count) {
                const newId = qs.length > 0 ? Math.max(...qs.map(q => q.id)) + 1 : 1;
                qs.push({
                    id: newId,
                    text: `题目 ${qs.length + 1}`,
                    options: ['选项A', '选项B', '选项C', '选项D'],
                    correct: [0],
                    type: 'single'
                });
            }
            while (qs.length > count) {
                qs.pop();
            }
            comp.props.questions = qs;
        }

        // ================================================================
        //  答题结果弹窗
        // ================================================================
        function showQuizResult(comp) {
            const p = comp.props;
            const questions = p.questions || [];
            const userAnswers = comp.userAnswers || {};

            let totalCorrect = 0;
            let details = '';

            questions.forEach((q, idx) => {
                const userAns = userAnswers[q.id] || [];
                const sortedUser = [...userAns].sort();
                const sortedCorrect = [...q.correct].sort();
                const isCorrect = JSON.stringify(sortedUser) === JSON.stringify(sortedCorrect);
                if (isCorrect) totalCorrect++;

                const correctText = q.correct.map(i => String.fromCharCode(65 + i)).join(', ');
                const userText = userAns.length ? userAns.map(i => String.fromCharCode(65 + i)).join(', ') : '未作答';

                details += `
                    <div class="q-result">
                        <strong>${idx+1}.</strong> ${escapeHtml(q.text)}
                        <br>
                        <span style="color:${isCorrect ? '#34a853' : '#ea4335'};">
                            ${isCorrect ? '✅' : '❌'} 你的答案: ${userText}
                        </span>
                        <br>
                        <span class="q-answer">✅ 正确答案: ${correctText}</span>
                    </div>
                `;
            });

            const score = Math.round((totalCorrect / questions.length) * 100);

            const content = `
                <div class="quiz-result-score">
                    🎯 得分: ${totalCorrect} / ${questions.length} (${score}分)
                </div>
                <div class="quiz-result-detail">
                    ${details}
                </div>
                <div style="text-align:center;margin-top:12px;font-size:12px;color:#5f6368;">
                    ${p.title || '选择题'}
                </div>
                <div style="text-align:center;margin-top:8px;">
                    <button class="quiz-close-btn" id="closeResultBtn">关闭</button>
                </div>
            `;

            openDialog({
                title: '📝 交卷',
                content: content,
                type: 'custom'
            }).then(() => {
                p.showResult = true;
                refreshComponent(comp);
                autoSave();
            });

            setTimeout(() => {
                const closeBtn = document.getElementById('closeResultBtn');
                if (closeBtn) {
                    closeBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
                        const overlay = document.getElementById('dialog-overlay');
                        if (overlay) overlay.classList.remove('active');
                    });
                }
            }, 100);
        }

        // ================================================================
        //  持久化
        // ================================================================
        function saveStateToStorage() {
            try {
                const data = {
                    pages: state.pages.map(p => ({
                        ...p,
                        components: p.components.map(c => {
                            const base = {
                                id: c.id,
                                type: c.type,
                                subtype: c.subtype,
                                x: c.x,
                                y: c.y,
                                width: c.width,
                                height: c.height,
                                baseWidth: c.baseWidth,
                                baseHeight: c.baseHeight,
                                scale: c.scale || 1,
                                contentAlignX: c.contentAlignX || 'center',
                                contentAlignY: c.contentAlignY || 'center',
                                props: c.props,
                                action: c.action,
                                toggleState: c.toggleState || 0,
                                customCss: c.customCss || '',
                                customJs: c.customJs || '',
                                parentId: c.parentId,
                                userAnswers: c.userAnswers || {},
                            };
                            if (c.type === 'container') {
                                base.children = c.children ? c.children.map(ch => ({
                                    id: ch.id,
                                    type: ch.type,
                                    subtype: ch.subtype,
                                    x: ch.x,
                                    y: ch.y,
                                    width: ch.width,
                                    height: ch.height,
                                    baseWidth: ch.baseWidth,
                                    baseHeight: ch.baseHeight,
                                    scale: ch.scale || 1,
                                    contentAlignX: ch.contentAlignX || 'center',
                                    contentAlignY: ch.contentAlignY || 'center',
                                    props: ch.props,
                                    action: ch.action,
                                    toggleState: ch.toggleState || 0,
                                    customCss: ch.customCss || '',
                                    customJs: ch.customJs || '',
                                    parentId: ch.parentId,
                                    userAnswers: ch.userAnswers || {},
                                })) : [];
                            }
                            return base;
                        })
                    })),
                    currentPageId: state.currentPageId,
                    inputValues: state.inputValues,
                    globalStyles: state.globalStyles,
                    globalScripts: state.globalScripts,
                    pageName: state.pageName,
                    taskName: state.taskName,
                    customComponents: state.customComponents,
                };
                localStorage.setItem(state.storageKey, JSON.stringify(data));
            } catch (e) { console.warn('保存失败', e); }
        }

        function loadStateFromStorage() {
            try {
                const raw = localStorage.getItem(state.storageKey);
                if (!raw) return false;
                const data = JSON.parse(raw);
                if (!data.pages || data.pages.length === 0) return false;
                state.pages = data.pages;
                state.currentPageId = data.currentPageId;
                state.inputValues = data.inputValues || {};
                state.globalStyles = data.globalStyles || '';
                state.globalScripts = data.globalScripts || '';
                state.pageName = data.pageName || '我的主页';
                state.taskName = data.taskName || '测试';
                state.customComponents = data.customComponents || [];
                for (const page of state.pages) {
                    for (const comp of page.components) {
                        if (comp.type === 'container' && comp.children) {
                            for (const child of comp.children) {
                                child.element = null;
                            }
                        }
                        comp.element = null;
                    }
                }
                let maxId = 1000;
                for (const page of state.pages) {
                    for (const comp of page.components) {
                        if (comp.id && comp.id > maxId) maxId = comp.id;
                        if (comp.children) {
                            for (const child of comp.children) {
                                if (child.id && child.id > maxId) maxId = child.id;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
                state.nextId = maxId + 1;
                return true;
            } catch (e) { console.warn('加载失败', e); return false; }
        }

        function autoSave() {
            saveStateToStorage();
            saveTemplatesToStorage();
        }

        // ================================================================
        //  模板存储
        // ================================================================
        function loadTemplatesFromStorage() {
            try {
                const raw = localStorage.getItem(state.templateStorageKey);
                if (!raw) return;
                const data = JSON.parse(raw);
                if (Array.isArray(data)) {
                    state.templates = data;
                }
            } catch (e) { console.warn('模板加载失败', e); }
        }

        function saveTemplatesToStorage() {
            try {
                localStorage.setItem(state.templateStorageKey, JSON.stringify(state.templates));
            } catch (e) { console.warn('模板保存失败', e); }
        }

        function getDefaultSizes(type, subtype) {
            const map = {
                'button': { w: 100, h: 38 },
                'input': { w: 180, h: 34 },
                'message': { w: 220, h: 40 },
                'effect': { w: 160, h: 42 },
                'appbar': { w: 280, h: 40 },
                'image': { w: 160, h: 120 },
                'fileinput': { w: 140, h: 38 },
                'filedownload': { w: 140, h: 38 },
                'custom': { w: 120, h: 40 },
                'container': { w: 220, h: 180 },
                'counter': { w: 140, h: 90 },
                'quiz': { w: 320, h: 280 },
                'textlabel': { w: 120, h: 32 },
                'radio': { w: 220, h: 110 },
                'checkbox': { w: 220, h: 120 },
                'select': { w: 220, h: 76 },
                'switch': { w: 220, h: 54 },
                'autostart': { w: 160, h: 42 },
                'taskstatus': { w: 180, h: 46 }
            };
            return map[type] || { w: 120, h: 40 };
        }

        // ================================================================
        //  规整功能
        // ================================================================
        function arrangeComponents() {
            const page = getCurrentPage();
            if (!page) { showToast('没有页面可规整'); return; }
            const topLevel = getTopLevelComponents();
            if (topLevel.length === 0) { showToast('页面为空，无需规整'); return; }

            const margin = 12;
            const canvasWidth = canvas.clientWidth - 40;

            const sorted = [...topLevel].sort((a, b) => (a.y || 0) - (b.y || 0));
            const placed = [];
            for (const comp of sorted) {
                const w = typeof comp.width === 'number' ? comp.width : 100;
                const h = typeof comp.height === 'number' ? comp.height : 40;
                let x = comp.x || 0;
                let y = comp.y || 0;
                let attempts = 0;
                const maxAttempts = 100;
                while (attempts < maxAttempts) {
                    let overlap = false;
                    for (const p of placed) {
                        const pw = typeof p.width === 'number' ? p.width : 100;
                        const ph = typeof p.height === 'number' ? p.height : 40;
                        const horizontalOverlap = x < p.x + pw + margin && x + w + margin > p.x;
                        const verticalOverlap = y < p.y + ph + margin && y + h + margin > p.y;
                        if (horizontalOverlap && verticalOverlap) {
                            overlap = true;
                            break;
                        }
                    }
                    if (!overlap) break;
                    x += 20;
                    if (x + w > canvasWidth) {
                        x = 20;
                        y += h + margin + 10;
                    }
                    attempts++;
                }
                if (attempts >= maxAttempts) {
                    y += h + margin;
                    x = 20;
                }
                comp.x = x;
                comp.y = y;
                refreshComponent(comp);
                placed.push(comp);
            }

            if (state.selectedId !== null) {
                const old = page.components.find(c => c.id === state.selectedId);
                if (old && old.element) old.element.classList.remove('selected');
                state.selectedId = null;
            }

            ensureCanvasSize();
            updatePropertiesPanel();
            updateCodePanel();
            autoSave();
            showToast('📐 组件已规整 (防重叠)');
        }

        // ================================================================
        //  模板功能
        // ================================================================
        function showTemplateDialog() {
            openDialog({
                title: '📋 模板',
                content: { type: 'templateMain' },
                onConfirm: () => {},
            }).then(() => {});
        }

        function showSaveTemplateDialog() {
            const nextNum = state.templates.length + 1;
            openDialog({
                title: `💾 保存当前页面为模板 (序号 ${nextNum})`,
                content: {
                    type: 'input',
                    inputType: 'text',
                    placeholder: '请输入模板名称',
                    value: `模板${nextNum}`
                }
            }).then(result => {
                if (result && result.input && result.input.trim()) {
                    saveCurrentPageAsTemplate(result.input.trim());
                } else if (result && result.input !== undefined && !result.input.trim()) {
                    showToast('⚠️ 模板名称不能为空');
                }
            });
        }

        function saveCurrentPageAsTemplate(name) {
            const page = getCurrentPage();
            if (!page) { showToast('没有页面可保存'); return; }
            if (page.components.length === 0) { showToast('页面为空，无法保存为模板'); return; }

            const pageData = {
                id: page.id,
                name: page.name,
                components: page.components.map(c => {
                    const base = {
                        id: c.id,
                        type: c.type,
                        subtype: c.subtype,
                        x: c.x,
                        y: c.y,
                        width: c.width,
                        height: c.height,
                        baseWidth: c.baseWidth,
                        baseHeight: c.baseHeight,
                        scale: c.scale || 1,
                        contentAlignX: c.contentAlignX || 'center',
                        contentAlignY: c.contentAlignY || 'center',
                        props: { ...c.props },
                        action: c.action ? { ...c.action, params: { ...c.action.params } } : { type: 'none',
                            params: {} },
                        toggleState: c.toggleState || 0,
                        customCss: c.customCss || '',
                        customJs: c.customJs || '',
                        parentId: c.parentId,
                        userAnswers: c.userAnswers || {},
                    };
                    if (c.type === 'container' && c.children) {
                        base.children = c.children.map(ch => ({
                            id: ch.id,
                            type: ch.type,
                            subtype: ch.subtype,
                            x: ch.x,
                            y: ch.y,
                            width: ch.width,
                            height: ch.height,
                            baseWidth: ch.baseWidth,
                            baseHeight: ch.baseHeight,
                            scale: ch.scale || 1,
                            contentAlignX: ch.contentAlignX || 'center',
                            contentAlignY: ch.contentAlignY || 'center',
                            props: { ...ch.props },
                            action: ch.action ? { ...ch.action, params: { ...ch.action.params } } : { type: 'none',
                                params: {} },
                            toggleState: ch.toggleState || 0,
                            customCss: ch.customCss || '',
                            customJs: ch.customJs || '',
                            parentId: ch.parentId,
                            userAnswers: ch.userAnswers || {},
                        }));
                    }
                    return base;
                })
            };

            const template = {
                id: Date.now(),
                name: name.trim(),
                pages: [pageData],
                createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
                componentCount: pageData.components.length,
            };

            state.templates.push(template);
            saveTemplatesToStorage();
            showToast(`✅ 已保存当前页面为模板: "${name}" (${pageData.components.length} 个组件)`);
        }

        function showManageTemplatesDialog() {
            renderManageTemplates();
        }

        function renderManageTemplates(searchKeyword = '') {
            const filtered = searchKeyword ?
                state.templates.filter(t => t.name.toLowerCase().includes(searchKeyword.toLowerCase())) :
                state.templates;

            const count = state.templates.length;

            let listHtml = '';
            if (filtered.length === 0) {
                listHtml = `<div class="template-empty">${count === 0 ? '📭 暂无模板，请先保存' : '🔍 没有匹配的模板'}</div>`;
            } else {
                listHtml = `<div class="template-list">`;
                for (const t of filtered) {
                    listHtml += `
                        <div class="template-item" data-id="${t.id}">
                            <span class="tname">📄 ${escapeHtml(t.name)}</span>
                            <span class="tinfo">${t.componentCount || 0} 个组件 · ${new Date(t.createdAt).toLocaleDateString()}</span>
                            <div class="tbtns">
                                <button class="t-apply" data-id="${t.id}">应用模板</button>
                                <button class="t-edit" data-id="${t.id}">编辑模板</button>
                                <button class="t-del" data-id="${t.id}">删除</button>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    `;
                }
                listHtml += `</div>`;
            }

            const bodyHtml = `
                <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:6px;">
                    <div class="template-toolbar">
                        <input type="text" class="template-search" id="templateSearchInput" placeholder="🔍 输入关键字搜索模板..." value="${escapeHtml(searchKeyword)}" />
                        <button class="clear-all-btn" id="templateClearAllBtn">🗑 清空全部模板</button>
                        <span style="font-size:12px;color:var(--text-secondary);margin-left:auto;">共 ${count} 个模板</span>
                    </div>
                    ${listHtml}
                </div>
            `;

            openDialog({
                title: '📂 管理模板',
                content: bodyHtml,
            }).then(() => {});

            setTimeout(() => {
                const searchInput = document.getElementById('templateSearchInput');
                if (searchInput) {
                    searchInput.addEventListener('input', () => {
                        const keyword = searchInput.value;
                        dialogOverlay.classList.remove('active');
                        renderManageTemplates(keyword);
                    });
                }

                const clearBtn = document.getElementById('templateClearAllBtn');
                if (clearBtn) {
                    clearBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
                        if (state.templates.length === 0) {
                            showToast('没有模板可清空');
                            return;
                        }
                        openDialog({
                            title: '⚠️ 清空全部模板',
                            content: '确定要清空全部模板吗？此操作不可撤销！',
                        }).then(result => {
                            if (result !== null) {
                                state.templates = [];
                                saveTemplatesToStorage();
                                dialogOverlay.classList.remove('active');
                                showToast('🗑 已清空全部模板');
                                setTimeout(() => renderManageTemplates(), 300);
                            }
                        });
                    });
                }

                document.querySelectorAll('.template-item .t-apply').forEach(btn => {
                    btn.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                        e.stopPropagation();
                        const id = parseInt(btn.dataset.id);
                        applyTemplate(id);
                    });
                });
                document.querySelectorAll('.template-item .t-edit').forEach(btn => {
                    btn.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                        e.stopPropagation();
                        const id = parseInt(btn.dataset.id);
                        editTemplate(id);
                    });
                });
                document.querySelectorAll('.template-item .t-del').forEach(btn => {
                    btn.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                        e.stopPropagation();
                        const id = parseInt(btn.dataset.id);
                        const template = state.templates.find(t => t.id === id);
                        if (!template) return;
                        openDialog({
                            title: `⚠️ 删除模板`,
                            content: `确定要删除模板 "${template.name}" 吗？`,
                        }).then(result => {
                            if (result !== null) {
                                deleteTemplate(id);
                            }
                        });
                    });
                });
            }, 100);
        }

        // ================================================================
        //  【修复】应用模板 - 使用ID映射避免冲突
        // ================================================================
        function applyTemplate(templateId) {
            const template = state.templates.find(t => t.id === templateId);
            if (!template) { showToast('模板不存在'); return; }
            if (!template.pages || template.pages.length === 0) { showToast('模板数据为空'); return; }

            const pageData = template.pages[0];
            const newPage = createPage(template.name + ' (副本)');

            // 🔑 建立旧ID到新ID的映射表
            const idMap = {};

            // 递归处理动作参数中的targetId
            function fixActionParams(action, map) {
                if (!action || !action.params) return action;
                const params = action.params;
                // 处理 targetId
                if (params.targetId && map[params.targetId]) {
                    params.targetId = map[params.targetId];
                }
                // 处理嵌套动作 (如 dialog 的 nextAction)
                if (params.nextParams && params.nextParams.targetId && map[params.nextParams.targetId]) {
                    params.nextParams.targetId = map[params.nextParams.targetId];
                }
                // 处理 replaceType 的 targetId
                if (params.targetId && map[params.targetId]) {
                    params.targetId = map[params.targetId];
                }
                return action;
            }

            for (const compData of pageData.components) {
                const oldId = compData.id;
                const newId = uid();
                idMap[oldId] = newId;

                // 修复动作参数中的targetId
                const fixedAction = compData.action ? fixActionParams({ ...compData.action }, idMap) : { type: 'none',
                    params: {} };

                const newComp = {
                    id: newId,
                    type: compData.type,
                    subtype: compData.subtype,
                    x: compData.x || 50,
                    y: compData.y || 50,
                    width: compData.width || 'auto',
                    height: compData.height || 'auto',
                    props: { ...compData.props },
                    action: fixedAction,
                    toggleState: compData.toggleState || 0,
                    customCss: compData.customCss || '',
                    customJs: compData.customJs || '',
                    parentId: compData.parentId ? idMap[compData.parentId] : undefined,
                    element: null,
                    children: [],
                    userAnswers: compData.userAnswers || {},
                };

                if (compData.type === 'container' && compData.children) {
                    newComp.children = compData.children.map(ch => {
                        const childOldId = ch.id;
                        const childNewId = uid();
                        idMap[childOldId] = childNewId;
                        // 修复子组件的动作参数
                        const childFixedAction = ch.action ? fixActionParams({ ...ch.action }, idMap) : { type: 'none',
                            params: {} };
                        return {
                            id: childNewId,
                            type: ch.type,
                            subtype: ch.subtype,
                            x: ch.x || 10,
                            y: ch.y || 10,
                            width: ch.width || 'auto',
                            height: ch.height || 'auto',
                            props: { ...ch.props },
                            action: childFixedAction,
                            toggleState: ch.toggleState || 0,
                            customCss: ch.customCss || '',
                            customJs: ch.customJs || '',
                            parentId: newId,
                            element: null,
                            userAnswers: ch.userAnswers || {},
                        };
                    });
                    // 更新子组件的parentId引用
                    for (const child of newComp.children) {
                        child.parentId = newId;
                    }
                }
                newPage.components.push(newComp);
                const el = renderComponent(newComp);
                newComp.element = el;
                canvas.appendChild(el);
            }

            // 修复所有组件的parentId（确保映射正确）
            for (const comp of newPage.components) {
                if (comp.parentId && !idMap[comp.parentId]) {
                    comp.parentId = undefined;
                }
            }

            switchPage(newPage.id);
            ensureCanvasSize();
            autoSave();
            showToast(`✅ 已应用模板: "${template.name}" (${newPage.components.length} 个组件)`);
        }

        function editTemplate(templateId) {
            const template = state.templates.find(t => t.id === templateId);
            if (!template) { showToast('模板不存在'); return; }
            if (!template.pages || template.pages.length === 0) { showToast('模板数据为空'); return; }

            dialogOverlay.classList.remove('active');
            applyTemplate(templateId);
            showToast(`✏️ 已应用模板 "${template.name}" 并进入编辑`);
        }

        function deleteTemplate(templateId) {
            const idx = state.templates.findIndex(t => t.id === templateId);
            if (idx === -1) { showToast('模板不存在'); return; }
            const name = state.templates[idx].name;
            state.templates.splice(idx, 1);
            saveTemplatesToStorage();
            dialogOverlay.classList.remove('active');
            showToast(`🗑 已删除模板: "${name}"`);
            setTimeout(() => renderManageTemplates(), 300);
        }

        function exportAllTemplates() {
            if (state.templates.length === 0) {
                showToast('没有模板可导出');
                return;
            }
            const data = JSON.stringify(state.templates, null, 2);
            const blob = new Blob([data], { type: 'application/json' });
            const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
            const a = document.createElement('a');
            a.href = url;
            const dateStr = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
            a.download = `逆风UI导出模板_${dateStr}.json`;
            document.body.appendChild(a);
            a.click();
            document.body.removeChild(a);
            URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
            showToast(`📤 已导出 ${state.templates.length} 个模板`);
        }

        function triggerTemplateImport() {
            templateFileInput.click();
        }

        templateFileInput.addEventListener('change', function(e) {
            const file = this.files[0];
            if (!file) return;
            this.value = '';
            importTemplatesWithProgress(file);
        });

        function importTemplatesWithProgress(file) {
            const reader = new FileReader();
            let progress = 0;
            const totalSteps = 10;
            let cancelled = false;

            const overlay = document.createElement('div');
            overlay.className = 'progress-overlay';
            overlay.id = 'importProgressOverlay';
            overlay.innerHTML = `
                <div class="progress-box">
                    <h3>📥 导入模板中</h3>
                    <div class="progress-bar-bg">
                        <div class="progress-bar-fill" id="importProgressFill" style="width:0%"></div>
                    </div>
                    <div class="progress-text" id="importProgressText">0%</div>
                    <div class="progress-actions">
                        <button id="importCancelBtn">取消</button>
                    </div>
                </div>
            `;
            document.body.appendChild(overlay);

            const fill = document.getElementById('importProgressFill');
            const text = document.getElementById('importProgressText');
            const cancelBtn = document.getElementById('importCancelBtn');

            const updateProgress = (pct) => {
                if (cancelled) return;
                fill.style.width = pct + '%';
                text.textContent = pct + '%';
            };

            cancelBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
                cancelled = true;
                reader.abort();
                overlay.remove();
                showToast('❌ 导入已取消');
            });

            reader.onprogress = (e) => {
                if (e.lengthComputable) {
                    const pct = Math.round((e.loaded / e.total) * 100);
                    const displayPct = Math.min(90, pct);
                    updateProgress(displayPct);
                } else {
                    let step = 0;
                    const interval = setInterval(() => {
                        if (cancelled) { clearInterval(interval); return; }
                        step++;
                        const pct = Math.min(90, Math.round((step / totalSteps) * 90));
                        updateProgress(pct);
                        if (step >= totalSteps) clearInterval(interval);
                    }, 100);
                }
            };

            reader.onload = function(ev) {
                if (cancelled) return;
                try {
                    updateProgress(95);
                    const data = JSON.parse(ev.target.result);
                    if (!Array.isArray(data)) {
                        throw new Error('数据格式错误，需要模板数组');
                    }
                    const before = state.templates.length;
                    for (const t of data) {
                        if (!state.templates.some(ex => ex.name === t.name)) {
                            state.templates.push(t);
                        }
                    }
                    saveTemplatesToStorage();
                    updateProgress(100);
                    setTimeout(() => {
                        overlay.remove();
                        showToast(`✅ 导入成功！新增 ${state.templates.length - before} 个模板`);
                        if (document.querySelector('#templateSearchInput')) {
                            renderManageTemplates();
                        }
                    }, 300);
                } catch (err) {
                    overlay.remove();
                    showToast('❌ 导入失败: ' + err.message);
                }
            };

            reader.onerror = function() {
                if (!cancelled) {
                    overlay.remove();
                    showToast('❌ 读取文件失败');
                }
            };

            reader.readAsText(file);
        }

        // ================================================================
        //  组件动作系统
        // ================================================================
        function executeComponentAction(compId, event = 'click') {
            const comp = getComponent(compId);
            if (!comp) return;

            // 检查是否被禁用
            if (comp.element && comp.element.dataset.disabled === 'true') {
                showToast('⛔ 此组件已被禁用');
                return;
            }

            // 自定义JS优先
            if (comp.customJs && comp.customJs.trim()) {
                try {
                    const fn = new Function('comp', 'event', 'showToast', 'openDialog', 'executeAction', comp.customJs);
                    fn(comp, event, showToast, openDialog, executeAction);
                    return;
                } catch (e) {
                    console.warn('自定义JS执行错误', e);
                    showToast('❌ 自定义JS错误: ' + e.message);
                }
            }

            const action = comp.action;
            if (!action || !action.type || action.type === 'none') {
                handleSaveCondition(compId);
                return;
            }

            // 特殊处理交卷动作
            if (action.type === 'quiz_submit') {
                showQuizResult(comp);
                return;
            }

            executeAction(action.type, action.params || {});
        }

        function executeAction(actionType, params) {
            params = params || {};
            const page = getCurrentPage();

            switch (actionType) {
                case 'dialog': {
                    const title = params.title || '提示';
                    let content = params.content || '这是弹窗内容';
                    const options = params.options ? params.options.split(',').map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean) : [];
                    let dialogConfig = { title, content, type: 'custom' };
                    if (options.length > 0) {
                        dialogConfig.content = options.map(opt => ({ label: opt, value: opt }));
                        dialogConfig.type = options.length > 2 ? 'checkbox' : 'radio';
                    }
                    if (params.nextAction && params.nextAction !== 'none') {
                        dialogConfig.onConfirm = (result) => {
                            const nextType = params.nextAction;
                            const nextParams = params.nextParams || {};
                            executeAction(nextType, nextParams);
                        };
                    }
                    openDialog(dialogConfig).then(result => {
                        if (result) showToast('📋 结果: ' + JSON.stringify(result));
                        else showToast('已取消');
                    });
                    break;
                }
                case 'save': {
                    const msg = params.message || '✅ 已保存';
                    const dur = (params.duration || 1) * 1000;
                    document.querySelectorAll('.input-field').forEach(el => {
                        const id = el.dataset.id;
                        if (id) {
                            state.inputValues[id] = el.value;
                        }
                    });
                    if (page) {
                        page.components.forEach(c => {
                            if (c.type === 'input') {
                                const el = c.element;
                                if (el) {
                                    const inputEl = el.querySelector('.input-field');
                                    if (inputEl) {
                                        c.props.defaultValue = inputEl.value;
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        });
                    }
                    autoSave();
                    showToast('💾 ' + msg, dur);
                    break;
                }
                case 'download': {
                    downloadCurrentHTML();
                    break;
                }
                case 'message': {
                    const msg = params.text || '📢 这是一条消息';
                    const dur = (params.duration || 1.5) * 1000;
                    showToast(msg, dur);
                    break;
                }
                case 'switchPage': {
                    const targetName = params.targetPage || '';
                    if (!targetName) { showToast('请指定目标页面名称'); break; }
                    const targetPage = state.pages.find(p => p.name === targetName);
                    if (!targetPage) { showToast('未找到页面: ' + targetName); break; }
                    if (targetPage.id === state.currentPageId) { showToast('已在当前页面'); break; }
                    switchPage(targetPage.id);
                    showToast('📄 已切换到: ' + targetPage.name);
                    break;
                }
                case 'triggerButton': {
                    const targetId = params.targetId || '';
                    if (!targetId) { showToast('请指定目标按钮ID'); break; }
                    const targetComp = getComponent(parseInt(targetId));
                    if (!targetComp) { showToast('未找到目标组件'); break; }
                    if (targetComp.type !== 'button') { showToast('目标不是按钮'); break; }
                    executeComponentAction(targetComp.id);
                    showToast('⚡ 已触发: ' + (targetComp.props.text || '按钮'));
                    break;
                }
                case 'navigate': {
                    const url = params.url || '';
                    if (!url) { showToast('请输入链接地址'); break; }
                    const target = params.target || '_blank';
                    window.open(url, target);
                    showToast('🔗 跳转: ' + url);
                    break;
                }
                case 'runPreview': {
                    runPreview();
                    break;
                }
                case 'importData': {
                    showImportPathDialog();
                    break;
                }
                case 'exportData': {
                    exportInputData();
                    break;
                }
                case 'toggleState': {
                    toggleButtonState(comp);
                    break;
                }
                case 'hideShow': {
                    const targetId = params.targetId || '';
                    if (!targetId) { showToast('请指定目标组件ID'); break; }
                    const targetComp = getComponent(parseInt(targetId));
                    if (!targetComp) { showToast('未找到目标组件'); break; }
                    toggleHideShow(targetComp);
                    break;
                }
                case 'disableEnable': {
                    const targetId = params.targetId || '';
                    if (!targetId) { showToast('请指定目标组件ID'); break; }
                    const targetComp = getComponent(parseInt(targetId));
                    if (!targetComp) { showToast('未找到目标组件'); break; }
                    toggleDisableEnable(targetComp);
                    break;
                }
                case 'replaceComponent': {
                    const targetId = params.targetId || '';
                    if (!targetId) { showToast('请指定目标组件ID'); break; }
                    const targetComp = getComponent(parseInt(targetId));
                    if (!targetComp) { showToast('未找到目标组件'); break; }
                    const replaceType = params.replaceType || 'button';
                    replaceComponentType(targetComp, replaceType);
                    break;
                }
                default: {
                    showToast('未知动作: ' + actionType);
                }
            }
        }

        // ================================================================
        //  动作实现函数
        // ================================================================
        function toggleHideShow(comp) {
            if (!comp || !comp.element) return;
            const el = comp.element;
            const isHidden = el.style.display === 'none';
            el.style.display = isHidden ? '' : 'none';
            showToast(isHidden ? '👁️ 显示: ' + (comp.props.text || comp.type) : '🙈 隐藏: ' + (comp.props.text || comp.type));
            if (comp.type === 'container' && comp.children) {
                for (const child of comp.children) {
                    if (child.element) {
                        child.element.style.display = isHidden ? '' : 'none';
                    }
                }
            }
            autoSave();
        }

        function toggleDisableEnable(comp) {
            if (!comp || !comp.element) return;
            const el = comp.element;
            const clickables = el.querySelectorAll('.btn, .msg-box, .img-display, [data-type="custom"]');
            const isDisabled = el.dataset.disabled === 'true';
            const newState = isDisabled ? 'false' : 'true';
            el.dataset.disabled = newState;
            clickables.forEach(btn => {
                btn.style.pointerEvents = isDisabled ? 'auto' : 'none';
                btn.style.opacity = isDisabled ? '1' : '0.5';
            });
            showToast(isDisabled ? '✅ 允许点击: ' + (comp.props.text || comp.type) : '🚫 禁止点击: ' + (comp.props.text || comp
                .type));
            autoSave();
        }

        function replaceComponentType(comp, newType) {
            if (!comp) return;
            const oldType = comp.type;
            const oldProps = { ...comp.props };
            const oldAction = comp.action ? { ...comp.action } : { type: 'none', params: {} };
            const oldCss = comp.customCss || '';
            const oldJs = comp.customJs || '';
            const oldUserAnswers = comp.userAnswers || {};

            const x = comp.x || 50;
            const y = comp.y || 50;

            const newCompData = createComponentData(newType, 'default', {});
            newCompData.x = x;
            newCompData.y = y;
            newCompData.id = comp.id;
            newCompData.customCss = oldCss;
            newCompData.customJs = oldJs;
            newCompData.userAnswers = oldUserAnswers;

            if (oldProps.color) newCompData.props.color = oldProps.color;
            if (oldProps.bgColor) newCompData.props.bgColor = oldProps.bgColor;
            if (oldProps.fontSize) newCompData.props.fontSize = oldProps.fontSize;

            if (comp.type === 'container' && comp.children) {
                newCompData.children = comp.children.map(ch => ({
                    ...ch,
                    parentId: newCompData.id,
                }));
            }

            const page = getCurrentPage();
            if (!page) return;
            const idx = page.components.findIndex(c => c.id === comp.id);
            if (idx === -1) return;

            if (comp.element && comp.element.parentNode) {
                comp.element.parentNode.removeChild(comp.element);
            }

            page.components[idx] = newCompData;
            const el = renderComponent(newCompData);
            newCompData.element = el;
            canvas.appendChild(el);
            selectComponent(newCompData.id);
            ensureCanvasSize();
            updateCodePanel();
            autoSave();
            showToast('🔄 已替换为: ' + getTypeLabel(newType, 'default'));
        }

        function toggleButtonState(comp) {
            if (comp.type !== 'button') return;
            const states = comp.props.toggleStates || ['编辑', '完成', '已保存', '发布'];
            const colors = ['#34a853', '#fbbc04', '#1a73e8', '#ea4335'];
            const current = comp.toggleState || 0;
            const next = (current + 1) % states.length;
            comp.toggleState = next;
            comp.props.text = states[next];
            comp.props.bgColor = colors[next % colors.length];
            comp.props.color = '#fff';
            refreshComponent(comp);
            updatePropertiesPanel();
            updateCodePanel();
            autoSave();
            showToast('🔄 状态: ' + states[next]);
        }

        // ================================================================
        //  复制组件
        // ================================================================
        function copyComponent(compId) {
            const page = getCurrentPage();
            if (!page) return;
            const source = page.components.find(c => c.id === compId);
            if (!source) return;

            const newComp = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(source));
            newComp.id = uid();
            newComp.x = (source.x || 50) + 30;
            newComp.y = (source.y || 50) + 30;
            newComp.element = null;
            newComp.userAnswers = {};

            if (newComp.type === 'container' && newComp.children) {
                for (const child of newComp.children) {
                    child.id = uid();
                    child.parentId = newComp.id;
                    child.element = null;
                    child.userAnswers = {};
                }
                let counter = 1;
                for (const c of page.components) {
                    if (c.type === 'container' && c.props.text && c.props.text.startsWith('容器')) {
                        counter++;
                    }
                }
                newComp.props.text = '容器 ' + counter;
            }

            if (newComp.type === 'quiz') {
                // 重置答题状态
                newComp.userAnswers = {};
                newComp.props.showResult = false;
                // 更新题目ID避免冲突
                if (newComp.props.questions) {
                    newComp.props.questions = newComp.props.questions.map(q => ({
                        ...q,
                        id: uid()
                    }));
                }
            }

            page.components.push(newComp);
            const el = renderComponent(newComp);
            newComp.element = el;
            canvas.appendChild(el);
            ensureCanvasSize();
            selectComponent(newComp.id);
            updateCodePanel();
            autoSave();
            showToast('📋 已复制组件' + (newComp.type === 'container' ? ' (含子组件)' : ''));
        }

        // ================================================================
        //  导出 / 导入 数据
        // ================================================================
        function exportInputData() {
