            const page = getCurrentPage();
            const data = {};
            if (page) {
                page.components.forEach(c => {
                    if (c.type === 'input') {
                        const el = c.element;
                        if (el) {
                            const inputEl = el.querySelector('.input-field');
                            if (inputEl) {
                                data[c.id] = inputEl.value;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    if (c.type === 'container' && c.children) {
                        for (const child of c.children) {
                            if (child.type === 'input') {
                                const el = child.element;
                                if (el) {
                                    const inputEl = el.querySelector('.input-field');
                                    if (inputEl) {
                                        data[child.id] = inputEl.value;
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                });
            }
            Object.keys(state.inputValues).forEach(key => {
                data[key] = state.inputValues[key];
            });
            if (Object.keys(data).length === 0) {
                showToast('⚠️ 没有输入框数据可导出');
                return;
            }
            const json = JSON.stringify(data, null, 2);
            const blob = new Blob([json], { type: 'application/json' });
            const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
            const a = document.createElement('a');
            a.href = url;
            a.download = `input_data_${new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10)}.json`;
            document.body.appendChild(a);
            a.click();
            document.body.removeChild(a);
            URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
            showToast('✅ 数据已导出 (' + Object.keys(data).length + ' 项)');
        }

        function importInputData(file) {
            const reader = new FileReader();
            reader.onload = function(ev) {
                importInputDataFromText(ev.target.result);
            };
            reader.readAsText(file);
        }

        function importInputDataFromText(text) {
            try {
                const data = JSON.parse(text);
                const page = getCurrentPage();
                if (!page) { showToast('没有页面'); return; }
                let count = 0;
                page.components.forEach(c => {
                    if (c.type === 'input' && data[c.id] !== undefined) {
                        state.inputValues[c.id] = data[c.id];
                        c.props.defaultValue = data[c.id];
                        const inputEl = c.element && c.element.querySelector('.input-field');
                        if (inputEl) inputEl.value = data[c.id];
                        count++;
                    }
                    if (c.type === 'container' && c.children) {
                        for (const child of c.children) {
                            if (child.type === 'input' && data[child.id] !== undefined) {
                                state.inputValues[child.id] = data[child.id];
                                child.props.defaultValue = data[child.id];
                                const inputEl = child.element && child.element.querySelector('.input-field');
                                if (inputEl) inputEl.value = data[child.id];
                                count++;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                });
                autoSave();
                showToast('✅ 已导入 ' + count + ' 个输入框数据');
            } catch (e) {
                showToast('❌ 数据格式错误: ' + e.message);
            }
        }

        function importInputDataByPath(path) {
            const filePath = String(path || '').trim();
            if (!filePath) { showToast('请输入文件路径'); return; }
            try {
                if (window.file && typeof window.file.read === 'function') {
                    const content = window.file.read(filePath);
                    if (content === null || content === undefined || content === '') {
                        showToast('❌ 读取失败：文件不存在、无权限或内容为空');
                        return;
                    }
                    importInputDataFromText(content);
                    return;
                }
                showToast('当前环境不支持 file.read，可尝试文件选择');
                dataFileInput.click();
            } catch (e) {
                showToast('❌ 读取失败: ' + e.message);
            }
        }

        function showContainerAddMenu(containerData) {
            const typeNames = getTypeNames();
            let menuHtml = '<div style="max-height:400px;overflow:auto;padding:8px;">';
            menuHtml += '<div style="font-size:13px;font-weight:600;color:#333;margin-bottom:8px;">添加组件到容器</div>';
            menuHtml += '<div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(3,1fr);gap:6px;">';
            const types = ['button','input','radio','checkbox','select','switch','textlabel','fileinput','filedownload','counter'];
            types.forEach(t => {
                const label = typeNames[t] ? typeNames[t].default : t;
                menuHtml += '<div class="container-add-item" data-type="' + t + '" style="padding:8px 4px;text-align:center;border:1px solid #e0e0e0;border-radius:6px;cursor:pointer;font-size:11px;color:#333;transition:all 0.15s;user-select:none;">' + label + '</div>';
            });
            menuHtml += '</div></div>';
            openDialog({
                title: '添加组件到容器',
                content: menuHtml,
            }).then(() => {});
            setTimeout(() => {
                document.querySelectorAll('.container-add-item').forEach(item => {
                    item.addEventListener('click', () => {
                        const overlay = document.querySelector('div[style*="z-index:10000"]');
                        if (overlay && overlay.parentNode) overlay.parentNode.removeChild(overlay);
                        const type = item.dataset.type;
                        if (!containerData.children) containerData.children = [];
                        const newComp = createComponentData(type);
                        newComp.x = 5 + Math.random() * 20;
                        newComp.y = 5 + Math.random() * 20;
                        newComp.parentId = containerData.id;
                        containerData.children.push(newComp);
                        refreshComponent(containerData);
                        selectComponent(newComp.id);
                        autoSave();
                        showToast('✅ 已添加到容器');
                    });
                    item.addEventListener('mouseenter', () => { item.style.background = '#e8f0fe'; item.style.borderColor = '#4285f4'; });
                    item.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => { item.style.background = ''; item.style.borderColor = '#e0e0e0'; });
                });
            }, 100);
        }

        function showPagerAddMenu(pagerData, pageIndex) {
            const pages = pagerData.props.pages || [];
            const pg = pages[pageIndex];
            if (!pg) return;
            const typeNames = getTypeNames();
            let menuHtml = '<div style="max-height:400px;overflow:auto;padding:8px;">';
            menuHtml += '<div style="font-size:13px;font-weight:600;color:#333;margin-bottom:8px;">添加组件到: ' + escapeHtml(pg.name || '子页面') + '</div>';
            menuHtml += '<div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(3,1fr);gap:6px;">';
            const types = ['button','input','radio','checkbox','select','switch','textlabel','fileinput','filedownload','counter'];
            types.forEach(t => {
                const label = typeNames[t] ? typeNames[t].default : t;
                menuHtml += '<div class="pager-add-item" data-type="' + t + '" style="padding:8px 4px;text-align:center;border:1px solid #e0e0e0;border-radius:6px;cursor:pointer;font-size:11px;color:#333;transition:all 0.15s;user-select:none;">' + label + '</div>';
            });
            menuHtml += '</div></div>';
            openDialog({
                title: '添加组件',
                content: menuHtml,
            }).then(() => {});
            // 绑定点击
            setTimeout(() => {
                document.querySelectorAll('.pager-add-item').forEach(item => {
                    item.addEventListener('click', () => {
                        const overlay = document.querySelector('div[style*="z-index:10000"]');
                        if (overlay && overlay.parentNode) overlay.parentNode.removeChild(overlay);
                        const type = item.dataset.type;
                        if (!pg.children) pg.children = [];
                        const newComp = createComponentData(type);
                        newComp.x = 5 + Math.random() * 20;
                        newComp.y = 5 + Math.random() * 20;
                        newComp.parentId = pagerData.id;
                        pg.children.push(newComp);
                        refreshComponent(pagerData);
                        selectComponent(newComp.id);
                        autoSave();
                        showToast('✅ 已添加到: ' + (pg.name || '子页面'));
                    });
                    item.addEventListener('mouseenter', () => { item.style.background = '#e8f0fe'; item.style.borderColor = '#4285f4'; });
                    item.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => { item.style.background = ''; item.style.borderColor = '#e0e0e0'; });
                });
            }, 100);
        }

        function showImportPathDialog() {
            openDialog({
                title: '📂 导入数据',
                content: {
                    type: 'input',
                    inputType: 'text',
                    placeholder: '/storage/emulated/0/Download/input_data.json',
                    value: '/storage/emulated/0/Download/input_data.json'
                }
            }).then(result => {
                if (result && result.input !== undefined) {
                    importInputDataByPath(result.input);
                }
            });
        }

        // ================================================================
        //  组件工厂
        // ================================================================
        function createComponentData(type, subtype, props = {}) {
            const base = {
                id: uid(),
                type,
                subtype,
                x: 50 + Math.random() * 60,
                y: 50 + Math.random() * 60,
                width: 'auto',
                height: 'auto',
                baseWidth: null,
                baseHeight: null,
                scale: 1,
                contentAlignX: props.contentAlignX || 'center',
                contentAlignY: props.contentAlignY || 'center',
                props: {
                    text: '',
                    color: '#202124',
                    bgColor: '',
                    fontSize: 14,
                    width: 'auto',
                    placeholder: '',
                    duration: 2,
                    ...props
                },
                action: {
                    type: 'none',
                    params: {}
                },
                toggleState: 0,
                customCss: '',
                customJs: '',
                parentId: undefined,
                userAnswers: {},
            };

            switch (type) {
                case 'button': {
                    base.props.text = props.text || '按钮';
                    base.props.bgColor = props.bgColor || '#FF6B00';
                    base.props.color = props.color || '#ffffff';
                    base.width = 100;
                    base.height = 38;
                    base.action = {
                        type: 'dialog',
                        params: {
                            title: '提示',
                            content: '这是弹窗内容',
                            options: '确定, 取消',
                            nextAction: 'none',
                            nextParams: {}
                        }
                    };
                    break;
                }
                case 'textlabel': {
                    base.props.text = props.text || '文字说明';
                    base.props.bgColor = props.bgColor || '';
                    base.props.color = props.color || '#202124';
                    base.props.fontSize = props.fontSize || 14;
                    base.props.width = props.width || 'auto';
                    base.width = 120;
                    base.height = 32;
                    base.action = { type: 'none', params: {} };
                    break;
                }
                case 'pager': {
                    base.props.text = props.text || '分页';
                    base.props.pages = props.pages || [
                        { name: '第1页' },
                        { name: '第2页' }
                    ];
                    base.props.activeIndex = props.activeIndex || 0;
                    base.props.bgColor = props.bgColor || '#fff';
                    base.props.color = props.color || '#333';
                    base.props.tabBgColor = props.tabBgColor || '#f1f3f4';
                    base.props.tabActiveColor = props.tabActiveColor || '#FF6B00';
                    base.props.tabHeight = props.tabHeight || 36;
                    base.width = 260;
                    base.height = 200;
                    base.action = { type: 'none', params: {} };
                    break;
                }
                case 'input': {
                    base.props.placeholder = props.placeholder || '请输入内容';
                    base.props.configName = props.configName || 'input_value';
                    base.props.text = props.text || '输入框';
                    base.props.showLabel = props.showLabel !== undefined ? props.showLabel : true;
                    base.props.labelPosition = props.labelPosition || 'top';
                    base.props.labelWidth = props.labelWidth || 52;
                    base.props.labelGap = props.labelGap || 4;
                    base.props.width = '100%';
                    base.width = 180;
                    base.height = 62;
                    break;
                }
                case 'message': {
                    base.props.text = props.text || '💡 这是一条消息';
                    base.props.width = '100%';
                    base.props.duration = 2;
                    base.width = 220;
                    base.height = 40;
                    break;
                }
                case 'effect': {
                    base.props.text = props.text || '✨ 淡入效果';
                    base.props.width = '100%';
                    base.width = 160;
                    base.height = 42;
                    if (!['fade', 'slide', 'bounce', 'rotate'].includes(subtype)) subtype = 'fade';
                    base.subtype = subtype;
                    break;
                }
                case 'appbar': {
                    base.props.text = props.text || '📱 顶部导航';
                    base.props.width = '100%';
                    base.width = 280;
                    base.height = 40;
                    break;
                }
                case 'image': {
                    base.props.text = props.text || '🖼️ 图片';
                    base.props.src = props.src || 'https://via.placeholder.com/300x200/FF6B00/fff?text=图片';
                    base.props.alt = props.alt || '图片';
                    base.width = 160;
                    base.height = 120;
                    break;
                }
                case 'fileinput': {
                    base.props.text = props.text || '📂 导入数据';
                    base.width = 140;
                    base.height = 38;
                    base.action = { type: 'importData', params: {} };
                    break;
                }
                case 'filedownload': {
                    base.props.text = props.text || '💾 下载数据';
                    base.width = 140;
                    base.height = 38;
                    base.action = { type: 'exportData', params: {} };
                    break;
                }
                case 'custom': {
                    base.props.text = props.text || '🧩 自定义';
                    base.width = 120;
                    base.height = 40;
                    base.customCss = props.customCss || '';
                    base.customJs = props.customJs || '';
                    base.action = { type: 'none', params: {} };
                    break;
                }
                case 'container': {
                    base.props.text = props.text || '容器';
                    base.props.bgColor = props.bgColor || '#f5f0e8';
                    base.width = 220;
                    base.height = 180;
                    base.children = [];
                    base.action = { type: 'none', params: {} };
                    break;
                }
                case 'counter': {
                    base.props.text = props.text || '计数';
                    base.props.value = props.value || 0;
                    base.props.bgColor = props.bgColor || '#e8e8e8';
                    base.width = 140;
                    base.height = 90;
                    base.action = { type: 'none', params: {} };
                    break;
                }
                case 'quiz': {
                    base.props.text = props.text || '答题';
                    base.props.title = props.title || '选择题';
                    base.props.questions = props.questions || [
                        { id: 1, text: '1 + 1 = ?', options: ['1', '2', '3', '4'], correct: [1], type: 'single' },
                        { id: 2, text: '以下哪些是水果？', options: ['苹果', '香蕉', '白菜', '萝卜'], correct: [0, 1],
                        type: 'multi' }
                    ];
                    base.props.showResult = props.showResult || false;
                    base.width = 320;
                    base.height = 280;
                    base.action = { type: 'quiz_submit', params: {} };
                    base.userAnswers = {};
                    break;
                }
                case 'radio': {
                    base.props.text = props.text || '单选配置';
                    base.props.configName = props.configName || 'radio_option';
                    base.props.options = props.options || '选项1|选项2|选项3';
                    base.props.defaultValue = props.defaultValue || '选项1';
                    base.props.optionLayout = props.optionLayout || 'vertical';
                    base.props.showLabel = props.showLabel !== undefined ? props.showLabel : true;
                    base.props.labelPosition = props.labelPosition || 'top';
                    base.props.labelWidth = props.labelWidth || 52;
                    base.props.labelGap = props.labelGap || 4;
                    base.props.width = '100%';
                    base.width = 220;
                    base.height = 110;
                    break;
                }
                case 'checkbox': {
                    base.props.text = props.text || '多选配置';
                    base.props.configName = props.configName || 'multi_option';
                    base.props.options = props.options || '选项1|选项2|选项3';
                    base.props.defaultValue = props.defaultValue || '选项1|选项2';
                    base.props.optionLayout = props.optionLayout || 'vertical';
                    base.props.showLabel = props.showLabel !== undefined ? props.showLabel : true;
                    base.props.labelPosition = props.labelPosition || 'top';
                    base.props.labelWidth = props.labelWidth || 52;
                    base.props.labelGap = props.labelGap || 4;
                    base.props.width = '100%';
                    base.width = 220;
                    base.height = 120;
                    break;
                }
                case 'select': {
                    base.props.text = props.text || '下拉配置';
                    base.props.configName = props.configName || 'select_option';
                    base.props.options = props.options || '选项1|选项2|选项3';
                    base.props.defaultValue = props.defaultValue || '选项1';
                    base.props.showLabel = props.showLabel !== undefined ? props.showLabel : true;
                    base.props.labelPosition = props.labelPosition || 'top';
                    base.props.labelWidth = props.labelWidth || 52;
                    base.props.labelGap = props.labelGap || 4;
                    base.props.width = '100%';
                    base.width = 220;
                    base.height = 76;
                    break;
                }
                case 'switch': {
                    base.props.text = props.text || '转换开关';
                    base.props.configName = props.configName || 'switch_option';
                    base.props.options = props.options || '开|关';
                    base.props.defaultValue = props.defaultValue || '开';
                    base.props.showLabel = props.showLabel !== undefined ? props.showLabel : true;
                    base.props.labelPosition = props.labelPosition || 'left';
                    base.props.labelWidth = props.labelWidth || 52;
                    base.props.labelGap = props.labelGap || 4;
                    base.props.width = '100%';
                    base.width = 150;
                    base.height = 54;
                    break;
                }
                case 'autostart': {
                    base.props.text = props.text || '🚀 启动任务';
                    base.props.taskName = props.taskName || '第一个任务';
                    base.props.loopMode = props.loopMode || '1';
                    base.props.width = '100%';
                    base.width = 160;
                    base.height = 42;
                    base.action = { type: 'autoConfigFinish', params: { taskName: base.props.taskName, loopMode: base.props.loopMode } };
                    break;
                }
                case 'taskstatus': {
                    base.props.text = props.text || '任务状态';
                    base.props.defaultValue = props.defaultValue || '未运行';
                    base.width = 180;
                    base.height = 46;
                    base.action = { type: 'none', params: {} };
                    break;
                }
                default:
                    base.props.text = '组件';
                    base.width = 100;
                    base.height = 34;
            }
            if (!base.baseWidth && typeof base.width === 'number') base.baseWidth = base.width;
            if (!base.baseHeight && typeof base.height === 'number') base.baseHeight = base.height;
            base.scale = props.scale || base.scale || 1;
            return base;
        }

        // ================================================================
        //  长按拖动：手机上按住组件本体即可移动
        // ================================================================
        const LONG_PRESS_DRAG_DELAY = 450;
        const LONG_PRESS_MOVE_TOLERANCE = 10;
        let suppressComponentClickUntil = 0;

        function getPointerPoint(ev) {
            const touch = ev.touches?.[0] || ev.changedTouches?.[0];
            return {
                x: ev.clientX ?? touch?.clientX ?? 0,
                y: ev.clientY ?? touch?.clientY ?? 0
            };
        }

        function parseOptionList(value) {
            return String(value || '')
                .split('|')
                .map(s => s.trim())
                .filter(Boolean);
        }

        function applyVisualProps(node, props, options = {}) {
            if (!node || !props) return;
            if (props.fontSize) node.style.fontSize = (parseInt(props.fontSize) || 14) + 'px';
            if (props.color) node.style.color = props.color;
            if (props.bgColor) node.style.backgroundColor = props.bgColor;
            if (options.applyWidth !== false && props.width && props.width !== 'auto') {
                node.style.width = props.width;
            }
        }

        function createConfigControlWrap(props, defaultTitle) {
            const wrap = document.createElement('div');
            wrap.className = 'config-control ' + ((props.labelPosition || 'top') === 'left' ? 'label-left' : 'label-top');
            wrap.style.gap = (parseInt(props.labelGap) || 4) + 'px';
            if (props.showLabel !== false) {
                const title = document.createElement('div');
                title.className = 'form-title';
                title.textContent = props.text || defaultTitle || '配置';
                if ((props.labelPosition || 'top') === 'left') {
                    title.style.minWidth = (parseInt(props.labelWidth) || 52) + 'px';
                    title.style.maxWidth = (parseInt(props.labelWidth) || 52) + 'px';
                }
                applyVisualProps(title, props, { applyWidth: false });
                wrap.appendChild(title);
            }
            return wrap;
        }

        function createEditorCustomSelect(data, props, options) {
            const current = props.defaultValue || options[0] || '';
            const box = document.createElement('div');
            box.className = 'custom-select';
            box.innerHTML = `<div class="custom-select-display"><span>${escapeHtml(current)}</span><span>▾</span></div><div class="custom-select-menu"></div>`;
            box.dataset.value = current;
            const menu = box.querySelector('.custom-select-menu');
            options.forEach(opt => {
                const item = document.createElement('div');
                item.className = 'custom-select-option' + (opt === current ? ' active' : '');
                item.textContent = opt;
                item.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                    e.stopPropagation();
                    props.defaultValue = opt;
                    box.dataset.value = opt;
                    box.querySelector('.custom-select-display span').textContent = opt;
                    box.classList.remove('open');
                    selectComponent(data.id);
                    autoSave();
                });
                menu.appendChild(item);
            });
            box.querySelector('.custom-select-display').addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                e.stopPropagation();
                document.querySelectorAll('.custom-select.open').forEach(el => { if (el !== box) el.classList.remove('open'); });
                box.classList.toggle('open');
                selectComponent(data.id);
            });
            return box;
        }

        function isInteractiveTarget(target) {
            return !!(target && target.closest(
                '.btn, .msg-box, .input-field, .form-widget, .form-option, .custom-select, .custom-select-menu, .custom-select-option, .switch-widget, input, textarea, select, label, .img-display, .custom-css, .counter-controls button'
            ));
        }

        function syncComponentWidthFromCss(comp, value) {
            const width = String(value || '').trim();
            comp.props.width = width || 'auto';
            if (!width || width === 'auto') return;
            const pxMatch = width.match(/^(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)px$/i);
            if (pxMatch) {
                comp.width = parseFloat(pxMatch[1]);
            } else if (/^\d+(?:\.\d+)?$/.test(width)) {
                comp.width = parseFloat(width);
                comp.props.width = width + 'px';
            } else {
                comp.width = width;
            }
        }

        function updateComponentScaleFromSize(comp) {
            if (!comp) return;
            if (!comp.baseWidth && typeof comp.width === 'number') comp.baseWidth = comp.width;
            if (!comp.baseHeight && typeof comp.height === 'number') comp.baseHeight = comp.height;
            if (typeof comp.width !== 'number' || typeof comp.height !== 'number') return;
            const bw = comp.baseWidth || comp.width || 1;
            const bh = comp.baseHeight || comp.height || 1;
            comp.scale = Math.max(0.2, Math.min(6, Math.min(comp.width / bw, comp.height / bh)));
            if (comp.element) {
                const content = comp.element.querySelector('.comp-content');
                if (content) content.style.transform = `scale(${comp.scale})`;
            }
        }

        function setComponentScale(comp, scale) {
            if (!comp) return;
            comp.scale = Math.max(0.2, Math.min(6, Number(scale) || 1));
            if (!comp.baseWidth && typeof comp.width === 'number') comp.baseWidth = comp.width / comp.scale;
            if (!comp.baseHeight && typeof comp.height === 'number') comp.baseHeight = comp.height / comp.scale;
            if (typeof comp.baseWidth === 'number') comp.width = comp.baseWidth * comp.scale;
            if (typeof comp.baseHeight === 'number') comp.height = comp.baseHeight * comp.scale;
            if (comp.element) {
                if (typeof comp.width === 'number') comp.element.style.width = comp.width + 'px';
                if (typeof comp.height === 'number') comp.element.style.height = comp.height + 'px';
                const content = comp.element.querySelector('.comp-content');
                if (content) content.style.transform = `scale(${comp.scale})`;
            }
        }

        function clamp(num, min, max) {
            return Math.min(max, Math.max(min, num));
        }

        function hexToRgb(hex) {
            let value = String(hex || '').trim();
            if (!value.startsWith('#')) value = '#' + value;
            if (/^#[0-9a-fA-F]{3}$/.test(value)) {
                value = '#' + value.slice(1).split('').map(ch => ch + ch).join('');
            }
            if (!/^#[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$/.test(value)) return { r: 255, g: 107, b: 0 };
            return {
                r: parseInt(value.slice(1, 3), 16),
                g: parseInt(value.slice(3, 5), 16),
                b: parseInt(value.slice(5, 7), 16)
            };
        }

        function rgbToHex(r, g, b) {
            return '#' + [r, g, b].map(v => clamp(Math.round(v), 0, 255).toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('').toUpperCase();
        }

        function rgbToHsv(r, g, b) {
            r /= 255; g /= 255; b /= 255;
            const max = Math.max(r, g, b);
            const min = Math.min(r, g, b);
            const d = max - min;
            let h = 0;
            if (d !== 0) {
                if (max === r) h = ((g - b) / d) % 6;
                else if (max === g) h = (b - r) / d + 2;
                else h = (r - g) / d + 4;
                h *= 60;
                if (h < 0) h += 360;
            }
            return { h, s: max === 0 ? 0 : d / max, v: max };
        }

        function hsvToRgb(h, s, v) {
            const c = v * s;
            const x = c * (1 - Math.abs((h / 60) % 2 - 1));
            const m = v - c;
            let r = 0, g = 0, b = 0;
            if (h < 60) { r = c; g = x; }
            else if (h < 120) { r = x; g = c; }
            else if (h < 180) { g = c; b = x; }
            else if (h < 240) { g = x; b = c; }
            else if (h < 300) { r = x; b = c; }
            else { r = c; b = x; }
            return {
                r: (r + m) * 255,
                g: (g + m) * 255,
                b: (b + m) * 255
            };
        }

        function openCustomColorPicker(anchor, initialColor, onChange) {
            document.querySelectorAll('.custom-color-picker').forEach(el => el.remove());
            const rgb = hexToRgb(initialColor);
            const hsv = rgbToHsv(rgb.r, rgb.g, rgb.b);
            const picker = document.createElement('div');
            picker.className = 'custom-color-picker';
            picker.innerHTML = `
                <div class="picker-main">
                    <div class="sv-panel"><div class="sv-cursor"></div></div>
                    <div class="hue-panel"><div class="hue-cursor"></div></div>
                </div>
                <div class="alpha-row"><div class="alpha-fill"></div></div>
                <div class="picker-bottom">
                    <input class="picker-hex" type="text" />
                    <button type="button" class="picker-btn picker-clear">Clear</button>
                    <button type="button" class="picker-btn picker-ok">OK</button>
                </div>
            `;
            document.body.appendChild(picker);

            const rect = anchor.getBoundingClientRect();
            const maxLeft = window.innerWidth - picker.offsetWidth - 8;
            const maxTop = window.innerHeight - picker.offsetHeight - 8;
            picker.style.left = clamp(rect.left, 8, Math.max(8, maxLeft)) + 'px';
            picker.style.top = clamp(rect.bottom + 6, 8, Math.max(8, maxTop)) + 'px';

            const svPanel = picker.querySelector('.sv-panel');
            const huePanel = picker.querySelector('.hue-panel');
            const svCursor = picker.querySelector('.sv-cursor');
            const hueCursor = picker.querySelector('.hue-cursor');
            const hexInput = picker.querySelector('.picker-hex');
            const alphaFill = picker.querySelector('.alpha-fill');
            let current = { ...hsv };

            const apply = () => {
                const rgb2 = hsvToRgb(current.h, current.s, current.v);
                const hex = rgbToHex(rgb2.r, rgb2.g, rgb2.b);
                svPanel.style.background = `linear-gradient(to top, #000, transparent), linear-gradient(to right, #fff, transparent), hsl(${current.h}, 100%, 50%)`;
                svCursor.style.left = (current.s * 100) + '%';
                svCursor.style.top = ((1 - current.v) * 100) + '%';
                hueCursor.style.top = (current.h / 360 * 100) + '%';
                hexInput.value = hex;
                alphaFill.style.background = `linear-gradient(to right, rgba(${Math.round(rgb2.r)},${Math.round(rgb2.g)},${Math.round(rgb2.b)},0), rgba(${Math.round(rgb2.r)},${Math.round(rgb2.g)},${Math.round(rgb2.b)},1))`;
                onChange(hex);
            };

            const pointFromEvent = (e) => {
                const t = e.touches?.[0] || e.changedTouches?.[0];
                return { x: e.clientX ?? t?.clientX ?? 0, y: e.clientY ?? t?.clientY ?? 0 };
            };

            const bindDrag = (panel, handler) => {
                const move = (e) => {
                    if (e.cancelable) e.preventDefault();
                    handler(pointFromEvent(e));
                    apply();
                };
                const up = () => {
                    document.removeEventListener('mousemove', move);
                    document.removeEventListener('mouseup', up);
                    document.removeEventListener('touchmove', move);
                    document.removeEventListener('touchend', up);
                    document.removeEventListener('touchcancel', up);
                };
                const down = (e) => {
                    e.stopPropagation();
                    move(e);
                    document.addEventListener('mousemove', move);
                    document.addEventListener('mouseup', up);
                    document.addEventListener('touchmove', move, { passive: false });
                    document.addEventListener('touchend', up, { passive: true });
                    document.addEventListener('touchcancel', up, { passive: true });
                };
                panel.addEventListener('mousedown', down);
                panel.addEventListener('touchstart', down, { passive: false });
            };

            bindDrag(svPanel, (pt) => {
                const r = svPanel.getBoundingClientRect();
                current.s = clamp((pt.x - r.left) / r.width, 0, 1);
                current.v = 1 - clamp((pt.y - r.top) / r.height, 0, 1);
            });
            bindDrag(huePanel, (pt) => {
                const r = huePanel.getBoundingClientRect();
                current.h = clamp((pt.y - r.top) / r.height, 0, 1) * 360;
            });

            hexInput.addEventListener('input', () => {
                if (!/^#?[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$/.test(hexInput.value.trim())) return;
                const rgb3 = hexToRgb(hexInput.value);
                current = rgbToHsv(rgb3.r, rgb3.g, rgb3.b);
                apply();
            });
            picker.querySelector('.picker-clear').addEventListener('click', () => {
                onChange('');
                picker.remove();
            });
            picker.querySelector('.picker-ok').addEventListener('click', () => picker.remove());

            setTimeout(() => {
                const close = (e) => {
                    if (!picker.contains(e.target) && e.target !== anchor) {
                        picker.remove();
                        document.removeEventListener('mousedown', close);
                        document.removeEventListener('touchstart', close);
                    }
                };
                document.addEventListener('mousedown', close);
                document.addEventListener('touchstart', close, { passive: true });
            }, 0);

            apply();
        }

        function bindEditableFocus(control) {
            if (!control || control.dataset.focusBound === '1') return;
            control.dataset.focusBound = '1';
            const focusNow = (e) => {
                e.stopPropagation();
                try {
                    control.focus({ preventScroll: true });
                } catch (err) {
                    control.focus();
                }
            };
            control.addEventListener('touchstart', focusNow, { passive: true });
            control.addEventListener('touchend', focusNow, { passive: true });
            control.addEventListener('mousedown', focusNow);
            control.addEventListener('click', focusNow);
        }

        function bindLongPressMove(el, id, options = {}) {
            let press = null;
            let timer = null;

            const clearPress = () => {
                if (timer) {
                    clearTimeout(timer);
                    timer = null;
                }
                if (press && !press.dragging) {
                    el.classList.remove('long-press-pending');
                }
                press = null;
            };

            const startPress = (e) => {
                if (e.type === 'mousedown' && e.button !== 0) return;
                if (e.target.closest('.drag-handle') || e.target.closest('.resize-handle') || e.target.closest('.edge-handle')) return;
                if (e.target.closest('input, textarea, select, [contenteditable="true"]')) return;
                // 容器/分页：不触发长按拖动（通过左上角圆点拖动）
                if (data.type === 'container' || data.type === 'pager') return;
                // 其他组件：只在边缘区域触发长按拖动
                const rect = el.getBoundingClientRect();
                const point = getPointerPoint(e);
                const distLeft = point.x - rect.left;
                const distRight = rect.right - point.x;
                const distTop = point.y - rect.top;
                const distBottom = rect.bottom - point.y;
                const EDGE_THRESHOLD = 15;
                const onEdge = distLeft < EDGE_THRESHOLD || distRight < EDGE_THRESHOLD || distTop < EDGE_THRESHOLD || distBottom < EDGE_THRESHOLD;
                if (!onEdge) return;

                press = { x: point.x, y: point.y, dragging: false };
                el.classList.add('long-press-pending');

                timer = setTimeout(() => {
                    if (!press) return;
                    press.dragging = true;
                    timer = null;
                    suppressComponentClickUntil = Date.now() + 900;
                    el.classList.remove('long-press-pending');
                    if (navigator.vibrate) navigator.vibrate(20);
                    if (options.containerId) {
                        startDragMoveChild(options.containerId, id, press.x, press.y);
                    } else {
                        startDragMove(id, press.x, press.y);
                    }
                }, options.delay || LONG_PRESS_DRAG_DELAY);
            };

            const cancelIfMoved = (e) => {
                if (!press || press.dragging) return;
                const point = getPointerPoint(e);
                const dx = point.x - press.x;
                const dy = point.y - press.y;
                if (Math.hypot(dx, dy) > LONG_PRESS_MOVE_TOLERANCE) {
                    clearPress();
                }
            };

            const endPress = () => {
                if (press && press.dragging) {
                    suppressComponentClickUntil = Date.now() + 900;
                }
                clearPress();
            };

            el.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                if (Date.now() < suppressComponentClickUntil) {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    e.stopImmediatePropagation();
                }
            }, true);
            el.addEventListener('mousedown', startPress);
            el.addEventListener('touchstart', startPress, { passive: true });
            document.addEventListener('mousemove', cancelIfMoved);
            document.addEventListener('touchmove', cancelIfMoved, { passive: true });
            document.addEventListener('mouseup', endPress);
            document.addEventListener('touchend', endPress, { passive: true });
            document.addEventListener('touchcancel', endPress, { passive: true });
        }

        // ================================================================
        //  渲染组件
        // ================================================================
        function renderComponent(data, renderOptions = {}) {
            const el = document.createElement('div');
            el.className = 'canvas-component';
            el.dataset.id = data.id;
            el.dataset.type = data.type;
            el.dataset.subtype = data.subtype;
            el.style.left = (data.x || 50) + 'px';
            el.style.top = (data.y || 50) + 'px';
            if (!data.baseWidth && typeof data.width === 'number') data.baseWidth = data.width;
            if (!data.baseHeight && typeof data.height === 'number') data.baseHeight = data.height;
            data.scale = Number(data.scale || 1);
            const justifyMap = { left: 'flex-start', center: 'center', right: 'flex-end' };
            const alignMap = { top: 'flex-start', center: 'center', bottom: 'flex-end' };
            el.style.setProperty('--content-justify', justifyMap[data.contentAlignX || 'center'] || 'center');
            el.style.setProperty('--content-align', alignMap[data.contentAlignY || 'center'] || 'center');
            if (data.width && data.width !== 'auto') {
                el.style.width = typeof data.width === 'number' ? data.width + 'px' : data.width;
            } else if (data.width === 'auto') {
                el.style.width = 'auto';
            }
            if (data.height && data.height !== 'auto') {
                el.style.height = typeof data.height === 'number' ? data.height + 'px' : data.height;
            }
            if (typeof data.width === 'number' && typeof data.height === 'number') {
                const handleSize = Math.round(clamp(Math.min(data.width, data.height) * 0.24, 8, 20));
                el.style.setProperty('--handle-size', handleSize + 'px');
                el.style.setProperty('--handle-offset', '-' + Math.ceil(handleSize / 2) + 'px');
                el.style.borderWidth = Math.min(data.width, data.height) < 48 ? '1px' : '2px';
            }

            const content = document.createElement('div');
            content.className = 'comp-content';
            content.style.transform = `scale(${data.scale || 1})`;
            el.appendChild(content);

            const handle = document.createElement('div');
            handle.className = 'drag-handle';
            handle.title = '拖拽移动';
            el.appendChild(handle);

            const resize = document.createElement('div');
            resize.className = 'resize-handle';
            resize.title = '拖拽缩放';
            el.appendChild(resize);

            // 四边中间的边框调节手柄（只改外框尺寸，内容大小不变）
            ['top','bottom','left','right'].forEach(edge => {
                const eh = document.createElement('div');
                eh.className = 'edge-handle edge-' + edge;
                eh.title = '调整' + ({top:'上',bottom:'下',left:'左',right:'右'}[edge]) + '边框';
                const bindEdge = (startEv) => {
                    startEv.stopPropagation();
                    startEv.preventDefault();
                    const cx = startEv.clientX || startEv.touches?.[0]?.clientX || 0;
                    const cy = startEv.clientY || startEv.touches?.[0]?.clientY || 0;
                    const rect = el.getBoundingClientRect();
                    const startX = data.x || 0, startY = data.y || 0;
                    const startW = rect.width, startH = rect.height;
                    const onMove = (ev) => {
                        if (ev.cancelable) ev.preventDefault();
                        const mx = ev.clientX || ev.touches?.[0]?.clientX || cx;
                        const my = ev.clientY || ev.touches?.[0]?.clientY || cy;
                        const dx = mx - cx, dy = my - cy;
                        if (edge === 'right') {
                            el.style.width = Math.max(30, startW + dx) + 'px';
                            data.width = Math.max(30, startW + dx);
                        } else if (edge === 'bottom') {
                            el.style.height = Math.max(20, startH + dy) + 'px';
                            data.height = Math.max(20, startH + dy);
                        } else if (edge === 'left') {
                            const nw = Math.max(30, startW - dx);
                            el.style.width = nw + 'px';
                            el.style.left = (startX + startW - nw) + 'px';
                            data.width = nw;
                            data.x = startX + startW - nw;
                        } else if (edge === 'top') {
                            const nh = Math.max(20, startH - dy);
                            el.style.height = nh + 'px';
                            el.style.top = (startY + startH - nh) + 'px';
                            data.height = nh;
                            data.y = startY + startH - nh;
                        }
                        ensureCanvasSize();
                    };
                    const onUp = () => {
                        document.removeEventListener('mousemove', onMove);
                        document.removeEventListener('mouseup', onUp);
                        document.removeEventListener('touchmove', onMove);
                        document.removeEventListener('touchend', onUp);
                        updatePropertiesPanel();
                        updateCodePanel();
                        autoSave();
                        ensureCanvasSize();
                    };
                    document.addEventListener('mousemove', onMove);
                    document.addEventListener('mouseup', onUp);
                    document.addEventListener('touchmove', onMove, { passive: false });
                    document.addEventListener('touchend', onUp, { passive: true });
                };
                eh.addEventListener('mousedown', bindEdge);
                eh.addEventListener('touchstart', bindEdge, { passive: false });
                el.appendChild(eh);
            });

            if (data.action && data.action.type && data.action.type !== 'none') {
                const badge = document.createElement('div');
                badge.className = 'action-badge';
                badge.textContent = '⚡' + getActionLabel(data.action.type);
                el.appendChild(badge);
            }

            const p = data.props;
            const style =
                `font-size:${p.fontSize||14}px;${p.color?'color:'+p.color+';':''}${p.bgColor?'background-color:'+p.bgColor+';':''}${p.width&&p.width!=='auto'?'width:'+p.width+';':''}`;

            if (data.customCss) {
                const styleTag = document.createElement('style');
                styleTag.textContent = `.canvas-component[data-id="${data.id}"] { ${data.customCss} }`;
                el.appendChild(styleTag);
            }

            // ============================================================
            //  容器组件 (支持滚动)
            // ============================================================
            if (data.type === 'container') {
                content.style.pointerEvents = 'auto';
                const containerDiv = document.createElement('div');
                containerDiv.className = 'container-wrapper';
                containerDiv.style.backgroundColor = p.bgColor || '#f5f0e8';
                containerDiv.style.width = '100%';
                containerDiv.style.height = '100%';
                containerDiv.style.minHeight = '120px';
                containerDiv.style.minWidth = '120px';
                containerDiv.style.overflow = 'hidden';
                containerDiv.style.position = 'relative';

                const titleDiv = document.createElement('div');
                titleDiv.className = 'container-title';
                titleDiv.textContent = p.text || '容器';
                titleDiv.style.cursor = 'pointer';
                titleDiv.style.pointerEvents = 'auto';
                titleDiv.style.position = 'sticky';
                titleDiv.style.top = '0';
                titleDiv.style.zIndex = '5';
                titleDiv.style.backgroundColor = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.9)';
                titleDiv.style.backdropFilter = 'blur(4px)';
                containerDiv.appendChild(titleDiv);

                const innerDiv = document.createElement('div');
                innerDiv.className = 'container-inner';
                innerDiv.style.position = 'relative';
                innerDiv.style.width = '100%';
                innerDiv.style.minHeight = '60px';
                containerDiv.appendChild(innerDiv);

                if (data.children && data.children.length > 0) {
                    for (const child of data.children) {
                        const childEl = renderComponent(child, { containerId: data.id });
                        child.element = childEl;
                        innerDiv.appendChild(childEl);
                        bindChildHandleResize(childEl, child, data);
                        childEl.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                            if (isInteractiveTarget(e.target)) return;
                            if (e.target.closest('.drag-handle') || e.target.closest('.resize-handle'))
                                return;
                            selectComponent(child.id);
                        });
                    }
                } else {
                    const emptyDiv = document.createElement('div');
                    emptyDiv.className = 'container-empty';
                    emptyDiv.textContent = '拖入组件到此处';
                    emptyDiv.style.cssText = 'position:absolute;inset:0;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;color:#9aa0a6;font-size:12px;';
                    innerDiv.appendChild(emptyDiv);
                }

                content.appendChild(containerDiv);

                el.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                    if (isInteractiveTarget(e.target)) return;
                    if (e.target.closest('.drag-handle') || e.target.closest('.resize-handle')) return;
                    // 如果点击的是子组件，让子组件自己的 handler 处理
                    const targetComp = e.target.closest('.canvas-component');
                    if (targetComp && targetComp !== el) return;
                    selectComponent(data.id);
                    e.stopPropagation();
                });

                handle.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
                    e.stopPropagation();
                    e.preventDefault();
                    startDragMove(data.id, e.clientX, e.clientY);
                });
                handle.addEventListener('touchstart', (e) => {
                    e.stopPropagation();
                    e.preventDefault();
                    const t = e.touches[0];
                    startDragMove(data.id, t.clientX, t.clientY);
                }, { passive: false });

                resize.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
                    e.stopPropagation();
                    e.preventDefault();
                    startDragResize(data.id, e.clientX, e.clientY);
                });
                resize.addEventListener('touchstart', (e) => {
                    e.stopPropagation();
                    e.preventDefault();
                    const t = e.touches[0];
                    startDragResize(data.id, t.clientX, t.clientY);
                }, { passive: false });

                bindLongPressMove(el, data.id, renderOptions);
                return el;
            }

            // ============================================================
            //  计数器组件
            // ============================================================
            if (data.type === 'counter') {
                const counterDiv = document.createElement('div');
                counterDiv.style.cssText =
                    'display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;gap:4px;width:100%;height:100%;justify-content:center;';

                const display = document.createElement('div');
                display.className = 'counter-display';
                display.textContent = p.value || 0;
                display.style.backgroundColor = p.bgColor || '#e8e8e8';
                display.style.color = p.color || '#333';
                counterDiv.appendChild(display);

                const controls = document.createElement('div');
                controls.className = 'counter-controls';
                const minusBtn = document.createElement('button');
                minusBtn.textContent = '-';
                minusBtn.style.pointerEvents = 'auto';
                minusBtn.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                    e.stopPropagation();
                    let val = parseInt(display.textContent) || 0;
                    val--;
                    display.textContent = val;
                    p.value = val;
                    if (data.customJs && data.customJs.trim()) {
                        try {
                            const fn = new Function('comp', 'event', 'showToast', data.customJs);
                            fn(data, e, showToast);
                        } catch (err) {}
                    }
                    autoSave();
                });
                const plusBtn = document.createElement('button');
                plusBtn.textContent = '+';
                plusBtn.style.pointerEvents = 'auto';
                plusBtn.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                    e.stopPropagation();
                    let val = parseInt(display.textContent) || 0;
                    val++;
                    display.textContent = val;
                    p.value = val;
                    if (data.customJs && data.customJs.trim()) {
                        try {
                            const fn = new Function('comp', 'event', 'showToast', data.customJs);
                            fn(data, e, showToast);
                        } catch (err) {}
                    }
                    autoSave();
                });
                controls.appendChild(minusBtn);
                controls.appendChild(plusBtn);
                counterDiv.appendChild(controls);
                content.appendChild(counterDiv);

                el.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                    if (e.target.closest('button')) return;
                    selectComponent(data.id);
                });

                handle.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
                    e.stopPropagation();
                    e.preventDefault();
                    startDragMove(data.id, e.clientX, e.clientY);
                });
                handle.addEventListener('touchstart', (e) => {
                    e.stopPropagation();
                    e.preventDefault();
                    const t = e.touches[0];
                    startDragMove(data.id, t.clientX, t.clientY);
                }, { passive: false });

                resize.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
                    e.stopPropagation();
                    e.preventDefault();
                    startDragResize(data.id, e.clientX, e.clientY);
                });
                resize.addEventListener('touchstart', (e) => {
                    e.stopPropagation();
                    e.preventDefault();
                    const t = e.touches[0];
                    startDragResize(data.id, t.clientX, t.clientY);
                }, { passive: false });

                bindLongPressMove(el, data.id, renderOptions);
                return el;
            }

            // ============================================================
            //  答题组件
            // ============================================================
            if (data.type === 'quiz') {
                const quizDiv = document.createElement('div');
                quizDiv.className = 'quiz-container';

                const titleDiv = document.createElement('div');
                titleDiv.className = 'quiz-title';
                titleDiv.textContent = p.title || '选择题';
                quizDiv.appendChild(titleDiv);

                const qContainer = document.createElement('div');
                qContainer.style.cssText = 'flex:1;overflow:auto;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:8px;padding:4px 0;';

                const questions = p.questions || [];
                if (!data.userAnswers) data.userAnswers = {};

                questions.forEach((q, qIdx) => {
                    const qBlock = document.createElement('div');
                    qBlock.className = 'quiz-question';
                    qBlock.dataset.qid = q.id;

                    const qText = document.createElement('div');
                    qText.className = 'q-text';
                    qText.innerHTML =
                        `<span style="color:var(--primary);">${qIdx+1}.</span> ${escapeHtml(q.text)}`;
                    const typeSpan = document.createElement('span');
                    typeSpan.className = `q-type ${q.type === 'single' ? 'single' : 'multi'}`;
                    typeSpan.textContent = q.type === 'single' ? '单选题' : '多选题';
                    qText.appendChild(typeSpan);
                    qBlock.appendChild(qText);

                    const optContainer = document.createElement('div');
                    optContainer.className = 'q-options';
                    optContainer.dataset.qid = q.id;
                    optContainer.dataset.type = q.type;

                    q.options.forEach((opt, oIdx) => {
                        const optDiv = document.createElement('div');
                        optDiv.className = 'q-opt';
                        optDiv.dataset.optIdx = oIdx;
                        optDiv.dataset.correct = q.correct.includes(oIdx) ? 'true' : 'false';

                        const checkbox = document.createElement('input');
                        checkbox.type = q.type === 'single' ? 'radio' : 'checkbox';
                        checkbox.name = `q_${q.id}`;
                        checkbox.value = oIdx;
                        checkbox.dataset.qid = q.id;
                        checkbox.dataset.optIdx = oIdx;

                        if (data.userAnswers[q.id] && data.userAnswers[q.id].includes(oIdx)) {
                            checkbox.checked = true;
                        }

                        const optLabel = document.createElement('label');
                        optLabel.textContent = `${String.fromCharCode(65 + oIdx)}. ${opt}`;

                        const mark = document.createElement('span');
                        mark.className = 'q-mark';

                        optDiv.appendChild(checkbox);
                        optDiv.appendChild(optLabel);
                        optDiv.appendChild(mark);

                        optDiv.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                            if (e.target.closest('input')) return;
                            const cb = optDiv.querySelector('input');
                            if (cb) {
                                if (q.type === 'single') {
                                    const siblings = optContainer.querySelectorAll('input');
                                    siblings.forEach(s => { if (s !== cb) s.checked = false; });
                                    cb.checked = true;
                                } else {
                                    cb.checked = !cb.checked;
                                }
                                cb.dispatchEvent(new Event('change'));
                            }
                        });

                        checkbox.addEventListener('change', () => {
                            const answers = data.userAnswers[q.id] || [];
                            if (checkbox.checked) {
                                if (q.type === 'single') {
                                    data.userAnswers[q.id] = [oIdx];
                                } else {
                                    if (!answers.includes(oIdx)) answers.push(oIdx);
                                    data.userAnswers[q.id] = answers;
                                }
                            } else {
                                const idx = answers.indexOf(oIdx);
                                if (idx > -1) answers.splice(idx, 1);
                                data.userAnswers[q.id] = answers;
                                if (answers.length === 0) delete data.userAnswers[q.id];
                            }
                            updateQuizOptionStyles(q.id);
                        });

                        const updateQuizOptionStyles = (qid) => {
                            const block = qContainer.querySelector(`[data-qid="${qid}"]`);
                            if (!block) return;
                            const opts = block.querySelectorAll('.q-opt');
                            opts.forEach(optEl => {
                                const cb = optEl.querySelector('input');
                                const markEl = optEl.querySelector('.q-mark');
                                if (p.showResult) {
                                    markEl.style.display = 'inline';
                                    const isCorrect = optEl.dataset.correct === 'true';
                                    const isSelected = cb && cb.checked;
                                    if (isCorrect && isSelected) {
                                        markEl.textContent = '✅';
                                        markEl.style.color = '#34a853';
                                        optEl.style.background = '#e6f4ea';
                                    } else if (isCorrect && !isSelected) {
                                        markEl.textContent = '❌';
                                        markEl.style.color = '#ea4335';
                                        optEl.style.background = '#fce8e6';
                                    } else if (!isCorrect && isSelected) {
                                        markEl.textContent = '✖️';
                                        markEl.style.color = '#ea4335';
                                        optEl.style.background = '#fce8e6';
                                    } else {
                                        markEl.textContent = '';
                                        optEl.style.background = '';
                                    }
                                } else {
                                    markEl.style.display = 'none';
                                    optEl.style.background = '';
                                }
                            });
                        };

                        optContainer.appendChild(optDiv);
                    });

                    qBlock.appendChild(optContainer);
                    qContainer.appendChild(qBlock);
                });

                quizDiv.appendChild(qContainer);

                const submitBtn = document.createElement('button');
submitBtn.className = 'quiz-submit-btn';
submitBtn.textContent = '📤 交卷';
submitBtn.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
    e.stopPropagation();
    // 编辑器模式：只显示提示，不执行交卷
    showToast('📝 请在「运行」窗口中答题交卷');
});
quizDiv.appendChild(submitBtn);

                content.appendChild(quizDiv);

                // 双击编辑答题
                el.addEventListener('dblclick', (e) => {
                    if (isInteractiveTarget(e.target)) return;
                    if (e.target.closest('.drag-handle') || e.target.closest('.resize-handle')) return;
                    openDialog({
                        title: '📝 编辑答题',
                        content: { type: 'quizEditor', comp: data },
                    });
                });

                el.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                    if (isInteractiveTarget(e.target)) return;
                    if (e.target.closest('.drag-handle') || e.target.closest('.resize-handle')) return;
                    selectComponent(data.id);
                });

                handle.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
                    e.stopPropagation();
                    e.preventDefault();
                    startDragMove(data.id, e.clientX, e.clientY);
                });
                handle.addEventListener('touchstart', (e) => {
                    e.stopPropagation();
                    e.preventDefault();
                    const t = e.touches[0];
                    startDragMove(data.id, t.clientX, t.clientY);
                }, { passive: false });

                resize.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
                    e.stopPropagation();
                    e.preventDefault();
                    startDragResize(data.id, e.clientX, e.clientY);
                });
                resize.addEventListener('touchstart', (e) => {
                    e.stopPropagation();
                    e.preventDefault();
                    const t = e.touches[0];
                    startDragResize(data.id, t.clientX, t.clientY);
                }, { passive: false });

                bindLongPressMove(el, data.id, renderOptions);
                return el;
            }

            // ============================================================
            //  原有组件渲染
            // ============================================================
            switch (data.type) {
                case 'button': {
                    const btn = document.createElement('button');
                    let cls = 'btn';
                    if (data.subtype === 'toggle') {
                        const states = p.toggleStates || ['编辑', '完成', '已保存', '发布'];
                        const idx = data.toggleState || 0;
                        const stateClass = ['btn-state-1', 'btn-state-2', 'btn-state-3', 'btn-state-4'];
                        cls += ' ' + (stateClass[idx % stateClass.length] || 'btn-state-1');
                        if (p.text !== states[idx]) { p.text = states[idx]; }
                        btn.dataset.toggle = idx;
                        btn.dataset.toggleStates = JSON.stringify(states);
                    } else {
                        cls += ` btn-default`;
                    }
                    btn.className = cls;
                    btn.textContent = p.text || '按钮';
                    applyVisualProps(btn, p);
                    btn.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                        e.stopPropagation();
                        executeComponentAction(data.id);
                        selectComponent(data.id);
                    });
                    content.appendChild(btn);
                    break;
                }
                case 'textlabel': {
                    const div = document.createElement('div');
                    div.textContent = p.text || '文字说明';
                    div.style.cssText = 'padding:2px 4px;border-radius:4px;white-space:pre-wrap;line-height:1.4;text-align:left;';
                    applyVisualProps(div, p);
                    content.appendChild(div);
                    break;
                }
                case 'pager': {
                    content.style.pointerEvents = 'auto';
                    const pages = p.pages || [];
                    const activeIdx = p.activeIndex || 0;
                    const tabH = p.tabHeight || 36;
                    const pagerWrap = document.createElement('div');
                    pagerWrap.style.cssText = 'display:flex;flex-direction:column;width:100%;height:100%;overflow:hidden;border-radius:6px;border:1px solid #e0e0e0;background:' + (p.bgColor || '#fff') + ';';

                    // 标签栏
                    const tabBar = document.createElement('div');
                    tabBar.style.cssText = 'display:flex;flex-shrink:0;border-bottom:1px solid #e0e0e0;overflow-x:auto;overflow-y:hidden;background:' + (p.tabBgColor || '#f1f3f4') + ';';
                    pages.forEach((pg, idx) => {
                        const tab = document.createElement('div');
                        tab.textContent = pg.name || ('页' + (idx + 1));
                        tab.style.cssText = 'padding:0 14px;height:' + tabH + 'px;display:flex;align-items:center;cursor:pointer;font-size:13px;font-weight:500;white-space:nowrap;color:' + (idx === activeIdx ? (p.tabActiveColor || '#FF6B00') : (p.color || '#333')) + ';border-bottom:' + (idx === activeIdx ? '2.5px solid ' + (p.tabActiveColor || '#FF6B00') : '2.5px solid transparent') + ';background:' + (idx === activeIdx ? (p.bgColor || '#fff') : 'transparent') + ';transition:all 0.2s;pointer-events:auto;user-select:none;';
                        tab.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                            e.stopPropagation();
                            p.activeIndex = idx;
                            refreshComponent(data);
                            selectComponent(data.id);
                            autoSave();
                        });
                        tabBar.appendChild(tab);
                    });
                    pagerWrap.appendChild(tabBar);

                    // 页面内容区 - 像容器一样放子组件
                    const pageArea = document.createElement('div');
                    pageArea.className = 'pager-page-area';
                    pageArea.style.cssText = 'flex:1;overflow:auto;position:relative;min-height:40px;';
                    const activePage = pages[activeIdx];
                    if (activePage && activePage.children && activePage.children.length > 0) {
                        for (const child of activePage.children) {
                            const childEl = renderComponent(child, { containerId: data.id });
                            child.element = childEl;
                            pageArea.appendChild(childEl);
                            bindChildHandleResize(childEl, child, data);
                            childEl.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                                if (isInteractiveTarget(e.target)) return;
                                if (e.target.closest('.drag-handle') || e.target.closest('.resize-handle') || e.target.closest('.edge-handle'))
                                    return;
                                e.stopPropagation();
                                selectComponent(child.id);
                            });
                        }
                    } else {
                        const emptyDiv = document.createElement('div');
                        emptyDiv.className = 'container-empty';
                        emptyDiv.textContent = '拖入组件到此处';
                        emptyDiv.style.cssText = 'position:absolute;inset:0;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;color:#9aa0a6;font-size:12px;';
                        pageArea.appendChild(emptyDiv);
                    }
                    pagerWrap.appendChild(pageArea);
                    content.appendChild(pagerWrap);

                    // 点击内容区选中分页组件
                    pagerWrap.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                        if (isInteractiveTarget(e.target)) return;
                        if (e.target.closest('.drag-handle') || e.target.closest('.resize-handle') || e.target.closest('.edge-handle'))
                            return;
                        if (e.target.closest('.pager-page-area') && !e.target.closest('.canvas-component')) {
                            selectComponent(data.id);
                        }
                    });

                    // 子组件放入当前活跃子页面
                    if (!data.pagerDropBound) {
                        data.pagerDropBound = true;
                        // 在 startDragFromPanel 的 drop 逻辑中处理
                    }
                    break;
                }
                case 'input': {
                    const wrap = document.createElement('div');
                    wrap.className = 'form-widget';
                    applyVisualProps(wrap, p);
                    const controlWrap = createConfigControlWrap(p, '输入框');
                    const inp = document.createElement('input');
                    inp.className = 'input-field';
                    inp.type = 'text';
                    inp.placeholder = p.placeholder || '输入内容';
                    const savedVal = state.inputValues[data.id];
                    inp.value = savedVal !== undefined ? savedVal : (p.defaultValue || '');
                    applyVisualProps(inp, p);
                    inp.style.pointerEvents = 'auto';
                    inp.dataset.id = data.id;
                    inp.addEventListener('input', () => {
                        state.inputValues[data.id] = inp.value;
                        p.defaultValue = inp.value;
                        autoSave();
                    });
                    bindEditableFocus(inp);
                    controlWrap.appendChild(inp);
                    wrap.appendChild(controlWrap);
                    content.appendChild(wrap);
                    break;
                }
                case 'radio': {
                    const wrap = document.createElement('div');
                    wrap.className = 'form-widget';
                    applyVisualProps(wrap, p);
                    const controlWrap = createConfigControlWrap(p, '单选配置');
                    const optsBox = document.createElement('div');
                    optsBox.className = 'form-options';
                    if ((p.optionLayout || 'vertical') === 'horizontal') {
                        optsBox.style.flexDirection = 'row';
                        optsBox.style.flexWrap = 'wrap';
                    }
                    const opts = parseOptionList(p.options || '选项1|选项2');
                    opts.forEach((opt, idx) => {
                        const label = document.createElement('label');
                        label.className = 'form-option';
                        applyVisualProps(label, p, { applyWidth: false });
                        label.innerHTML = `<input type="radio" name="editor_radio_${data.id}" ${opt === (p.defaultValue || opts[0]) ? 'checked' : ''}> <span>${escapeHtml(opt)}</span>`;
                        label.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                            e.stopPropagation();
                            p.defaultValue = opt;
                            selectComponent(data.id);
                            autoSave();
                        });
                        optsBox.appendChild(label);
                    });
                    controlWrap.appendChild(optsBox);
                    wrap.appendChild(controlWrap);
                    content.appendChild(wrap);
                    break;
                }
                case 'checkbox': {
                    const wrap = document.createElement('div');
                    wrap.className = 'form-widget';
                    applyVisualProps(wrap, p);
                    const controlWrap = createConfigControlWrap(p, '多选配置');
                    const optsBox = document.createElement('div');
                    optsBox.className = 'form-options';
                    if ((p.optionLayout || 'vertical') === 'horizontal') {
                        optsBox.style.flexDirection = 'row';
                        optsBox.style.flexWrap = 'wrap';
                    }
                    const opts = parseOptionList(p.options || '选项1|选项2');
                    const selected = parseOptionList(p.defaultValue || '');
                    opts.forEach(opt => {
                        const label = document.createElement('label');
                        label.className = 'form-option';
                        applyVisualProps(label, p, { applyWidth: false });
                        label.innerHTML = `<input type="checkbox" ${selected.includes(opt) ? 'checked' : ''}> <span>${escapeHtml(opt)}</span>`;
                        label.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
                            e.stopPropagation();
                            const values = Array.from(wrap.querySelectorAll('input:checked')).map(input => input.nextElementSibling.textContent);
                            p.defaultValue = values.join('|');
                            selectComponent(data.id);
                            autoSave();
                        });
                        optsBox.appendChild(label);
                    });
                    controlWrap.appendChild(optsBox);
