                    wrap.appendChild(controlWrap);
                    content.appendChild(wrap);
                    break;
                }
                case 'select': {
                    const wrap = document.createElement('div');
                    wrap.className = 'form-widget';
                    applyVisualProps(wrap, p);
                    const opts = parseOptionList(p.options || '选项1|选项2');
                    const controlWrap = createConfigControlWrap(p, '下拉配置');
                    const sel = createEditorCustomSelect(data, p, opts);
                    applyVisualProps(sel.querySelector('.custom-select-display'), p, { applyWidth: false });
                    controlWrap.appendChild(sel);
                    wrap.appendChild(controlWrap);
                    content.appendChild(wrap);
                    break;
                }
                case 'switch': {
                    const opts = parseOptionList(p.options || '开|关');
                    const onText = opts[0] || '开';
                    const offText = opts[1] || '关';
                    const isOn = (p.defaultValue || onText) === onText;
                    const wrap = document.createElement('div');
                    wrap.className = 'switch-widget';
                    applyVisualProps(wrap, p);
                    const controlWrap = createConfigControlWrap(p, '转换开关');
                    const stateText = document.createElement('small');
                    stateText.textContent = isOn ? onText : offText;
                    applyVisualProps(stateText, p, { applyWidth: false });
                    const track = document.createElement('span');
                    track.className = `switch-track ${isOn ? 'on' : ''}`;
                    track.innerHTML = '<span class="switch-thumb"></span>';
                    controlWrap.appendChild(stateText);
                    controlWrap.appendChild(track);
                    wrap.appendChild(controlWrap);
                    wrap.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                        e.stopPropagation();
                        p.defaultValue = p.defaultValue === onText ? offText : onText;
                        refreshComponent(data);
                        selectComponent(data.id);
                        autoSave();
                    });
                    content.appendChild(wrap);
                    break;
                }
                case 'autostart': {
                    const btn = document.createElement('button');
                    btn.className = 'btn btn-success';
                    btn.textContent = p.text || '🚀 启动任务';
                    applyVisualProps(btn, p);
                    btn.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                        e.stopPropagation();
                        selectComponent(data.id);
                        showToast('🚀 运行窗口中会提交配置并启动任务');
                    });
                    content.appendChild(btn);
                    break;
                }
                case 'taskstatus': {
                    const div = document.createElement('div');
                    div.className = 'status-widget';
                    applyVisualProps(div, p);
                    div.innerHTML = `<strong>${escapeHtml(p.text || '任务状态')}</strong><br><span>${escapeHtml(p.defaultValue || '未运行')}</span>`;
                    content.appendChild(div);
                    break;
                }
                case 'message': {
                    const div = document.createElement('div');
                    div.className = 'msg-box msg-info';
                    div.textContent = p.text || '消息';
                    applyVisualProps(div, p);
                    div.style.pointerEvents = 'auto';
                    div.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                        e.stopPropagation();
                        selectComponent(data.id);
                        executeComponentAction(data.id);
                    });
                    content.appendChild(div);
                    break;
                }
                case 'effect': {
                    const div = document.createElement('div');
                    const animClass = data.subtype && ['fade', 'slide', 'bounce', 'rotate'].includes(data.subtype) ?
                        data.subtype : 'fade';
                    div.className = `effect-demo anim-${animClass}`;
                    div.textContent = p.text || '效果';
                    applyVisualProps(div, p);
                    content.appendChild(div);
                    break;
                }
                case 'appbar': {
                    const bar = document.createElement('div');
                    bar.className = 'app-bar';
                    applyVisualProps(bar, p);
                    const left = document.createElement('span');
                    left.textContent = p.text || '应用栏';
                    bar.appendChild(left);
                    const actions = document.createElement('div');
                    actions.className = 'bar-actions';
                    ['菜单', '搜索', '更多'].forEach(t => {
                        const s = document.createElement('span');
                        s.textContent = t;
                        actions.appendChild(s);
                    });
                    bar.appendChild(actions);
                    content.appendChild(bar);
                    break;
                }
                case 'image': {
                    const img = document.createElement('img');
                    img.className = 'img-display';
                    img.src = p.src || '';
                    img.alt = p.alt || '图片';
                    img.style.width = '100%';
                    img.style.height = 'auto';
                    img.style.maxHeight = '200px';
                    img.style.objectFit = 'contain';
                    img.style.pointerEvents = 'auto';
                    img.onerror = function() {
                        this.src = '';
                        this.alt = '图片加载失败';
                    };
                    img.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                        e.stopPropagation();
                        selectComponent(data.id);
                        if (data.customJs && data.customJs.trim()) {
                            try {
                                const fn = new Function('comp', 'event', 'showToast', data.customJs);
                                fn(data, e, showToast);
                            } catch (err) { showToast('❌ 自定义JS错误: ' + err.message); }
                        }
                    });
                    content.appendChild(img);
                    break;
                }
                case 'fileinput': {
                    const btn = document.createElement('button');
                    btn.className = 'btn btn-success';
                    btn.textContent = p.text || '📂 导入数据';
                    applyVisualProps(btn, p);
                    btn.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                        e.stopPropagation();
                        executeComponentAction(data.id);
                        selectComponent(data.id);
                    });
                    content.appendChild(btn);
                    break;
                }
                case 'filedownload': {
                    const btn = document.createElement('button');
                    btn.className = 'btn btn-warning';
                    btn.textContent = p.text || '💾 下载数据';
                    applyVisualProps(btn, p);
                    btn.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                        e.stopPropagation();
                        executeComponentAction(data.id);
                        selectComponent(data.id);
                    });
                    content.appendChild(btn);
                    break;
                }
                case 'custom': {
                    const div = document.createElement('div');
                    div.textContent = p.text || '🧩 自定义';
                    div.style.padding = '6px 14px';
                    div.style.background = '#f0f0f0';
                    div.style.borderRadius = '5px';
                    applyVisualProps(div, p);
                    div.style.cursor = 'pointer';
                    div.style.pointerEvents = 'auto';
                    div.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                        e.stopPropagation();
                        selectComponent(data.id);
                        executeComponentAction(data.id);
                    });
                    content.appendChild(div);
                    break;
                }
                default: {
                    const div = document.createElement('div');
                    div.textContent = p.text || '组件';
                    div.style.padding = '4px 10px';
                    div.style.background = '#f1f3f4';
                    div.style.borderRadius = '5px';
                    applyVisualProps(div, p);
                    content.appendChild(div);
                }
            }

            if (data.customCss || data.customJs) {
                const info = document.createElement('div');
                info.className = 'custom-css';
                let infoText = '';
                if (data.customCss) infoText += '🎨 CSS ';
                if (data.customJs) infoText += '⚡ JS ';
                info.innerHTML = `<code>${infoText || '自定义'}</code>`;
                el.appendChild(info);
            }

            el.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                if (isInteractiveTarget(e.target)) return;
                if (e.target.closest('.drag-handle') || e.target.closest('.resize-handle')) return;
                selectComponent(data.id);
            });

            el.addEventListener('dblclick', (e) => {
                if (isInteractiveTarget(e.target)) return;
                if (e.target.closest('.drag-handle') || e.target.closest('.resize-handle')) return;
                const comp = getComponent(data.id);
                if (comp) {
                    if (comp.type === 'image') {
                        const url = prompt('请输入图片URL：', comp.props.src || 'https://via.placeholder.com/300x200');
                        if (url !== null) {
                            comp.props.src = url;
                            refreshComponent(comp);
                            selectComponent(data.id);
                            updateCodePanel();
                            autoSave();
                            showToast('🖼️ 图片已更新');
                        }
                    } else if (comp.type === 'custom') {
                        showCustomEditor(comp);
                    } else if (comp.type === 'container') {
                        const newTitle = prompt('编辑容器标题：', comp.props.text);
                        if (newTitle !== null) {
                            comp.props.text = newTitle;
                            refreshComponent(comp);
                            selectComponent(data.id);
                            updateCodePanel();
                            autoSave();
                            showToast('容器标题已更新');
                        }
                    } else if (comp.type === 'counter') {
                        const newVal = prompt('设置计数器默认值：', comp.props.value || 0);
                        if (newVal !== null && !isNaN(parseInt(newVal))) {
                            comp.props.value = parseInt(newVal);
                            refreshComponent(comp);
                            selectComponent(data.id);
                            updateCodePanel();
                            autoSave();
                            showToast('计数器值已更新');
                        }
                    } else if (comp.type === 'quiz') {
                        openDialog({
                            title: '📝 编辑答题',
                            content: { type: 'quizEditor', comp: comp },
                        });
                    } else {
                        const newText = prompt('编辑文本内容：', comp.props.text);
                        if (newText !== null) {
                            comp.props.text = newText;
                            refreshComponent(comp);
                            selectComponent(data.id);
                            updateCodePanel();
                            autoSave();
                            showToast('文本已更新');
                        }
                    }
                }
            });

            handle.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
                e.stopPropagation();
                e.preventDefault();
                startDragMove(data.id, e.clientX, e.clientY);
            });
            handle.addEventListener('touchstart', (e) => {
                e.stopPropagation();
                e.preventDefault();
                const t = e.touches[0];
                startDragMove(data.id, t.clientX, t.clientY);
            }, { passive: false });

            resize.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
                e.stopPropagation();
                e.preventDefault();
                startDragResize(data.id, e.clientX, e.clientY);
            });
            resize.addEventListener('touchstart', (e) => {
                e.stopPropagation();
                e.preventDefault();
                const t = e.touches[0];
                startDragResize(data.id, t.clientX, t.clientY);
            }, { passive: false });

            bindLongPressMove(el, data.id, renderOptions);
            return el;
        }

        // ================================================================
        //  容器/分页内子组件拖拽和缩放
        // ================================================================
        function bindChildHandleResize(childEl, child, parentData) {
            const childHandle = childEl.querySelector('.drag-handle');
            if (childHandle) {
                childHandle.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
                    e.stopPropagation();
                    e.preventDefault();
                    startDragMoveChild(parentData.id, child.id, e.clientX, e.clientY);
                });
                childHandle.addEventListener('touchstart', (e) => {
                    e.stopPropagation();
                    e.preventDefault();
                    const t = e.touches[0];
                    startDragMoveChild(parentData.id, child.id, t.clientX, t.clientY);
                }, { passive: false });
            }
            const childResize = childEl.querySelector('.resize-handle');
            if (childResize) {
                childResize.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
                    e.stopPropagation();
                    e.preventDefault();
                    startDragResizeChild(parentData.id, child.id, e.clientX, e.clientY);
                });
                childResize.addEventListener('touchstart', (e) => {
                    e.stopPropagation();
                    e.preventDefault();
                    const t = e.touches[0];
                    startDragResizeChild(parentData.id, child.id, t.clientX, t.clientY);
                }, { passive: false });
            }
        }

        function startDragResizeChild(containerId, childId, startX, startY) {
            const container = getComponent(containerId);
            if (!container) return;
            let child = null;
            if (container.children) {
                child = container.children.find(c => c.id === childId);
            }
            if (!child && container.props && container.props.pages) {
                for (const pg of container.props.pages) {
                    if (pg.children) {
                        child = pg.children.find(c => c.id === childId);
                        if (child) break;
                    }
                }
            }
            if (!child) return;
            const el = child.element;
            if (!el) return;
            selectComponent(childId);
            const startW = child.width || el.offsetWidth || 80;
            const startH = child.height || el.offsetHeight || 30;
            const onMove = (ev) => {
                if (ev.cancelable) ev.preventDefault();
                const cx = ev.clientX || ev.touches?.[0]?.clientX || startX;
                const cy = ev.clientY || ev.touches?.[0]?.clientY || startY;
                const newW = Math.max(30, startW + (cx - startX));
                const newH = Math.max(20, startH + (cy - startY));
                child.width = newW;
                child.height = newH;
                el.style.width = newW + 'px';
                el.style.height = newH + 'px';
                updatePropertiesPanel();
            };
            const onUp = () => {
                document.removeEventListener('mousemove', onMove);
                document.removeEventListener('mouseup', onUp);
                document.removeEventListener('touchmove', onMove);
                document.removeEventListener('touchend', onUp);
                document.removeEventListener('touchcancel', onUp);
                updateCodePanel();
                autoSave();
            };
            document.addEventListener('mousemove', onMove);
            document.addEventListener('mouseup', onUp);
            document.addEventListener('touchmove', onMove, { passive: false });
            document.addEventListener('touchend', onUp);
            document.addEventListener('touchcancel', onUp);
        }

        function startDragMoveChild(containerId, childId, startX, startY) {
            const container = getComponent(containerId);
            if (!container) return;
            // 兼容容器和分页结构
            let child = null;
            if (container.children) {
                child = container.children.find(c => c.id === childId);
            }
            if (!child && container.props && container.props.pages) {
                for (const pg of container.props.pages) {
                    if (pg.children) {
                        child = pg.children.find(c => c.id === childId);
                        if (child) break;
                    }
                }
            }
            if (!child) return;
            const el = child.element;
            if (!el) return;
            selectComponent(childId);
            el.classList.add('dragging');
            const rect = el.getBoundingClientRect();
            const containerRect = container.element.getBoundingClientRect();
            const offsetX = startX - rect.left;
            const offsetY = startY - rect.top;

            const onMove = (ev) => {
                if (ev.cancelable) ev.preventDefault();
                const cx = ev.clientX || ev.touches?.[0]?.clientX || startX;
                const cy = ev.clientY || ev.touches?.[0]?.clientY || startY;
                let newX = cx - containerRect.left - offsetX;
                let newY = cy - containerRect.top - offsetY;
                newX = Math.max(0, Math.min(newX, container.width - (child.width || 40)));
                newY = Math.max(0, Math.min(newY, container.height - (child.height || 30)));
                child.x = newX;
                child.y = newY;
                el.style.left = newX + 'px';
                el.style.top = newY + 'px';
                updatePropertiesPanel();
            };

            const onUp = () => {
                document.removeEventListener('mousemove', onMove);
                document.removeEventListener('mouseup', onUp);
                document.removeEventListener('touchmove', onMove);
                document.removeEventListener('touchend', onUp);
                document.removeEventListener('touchcancel', onUp);
                el.classList.remove('dragging');
                updateCodePanel();
                autoSave();
            };

            document.addEventListener('mousemove', onMove);
            document.addEventListener('mouseup', onUp);
            document.addEventListener('touchmove', onMove, { passive: false });
            document.addEventListener('touchend', onUp, { passive: true });
            document.addEventListener('touchcancel', onUp, { passive: true });
        }

        function addComponent(type, subtype, props = {}) {
            const page = getCurrentPage();
            if (!page) return null;
            const hint = canvas.querySelector('.empty-hint');
            if (hint) hint.remove();

            const data = createComponentData(type, subtype, props);
            const cw = canvas.scrollWidth || 800;
            const ch = canvas.scrollHeight || 600;
            data.x = 20 + Math.random() * (cw - 160);
            data.y = 20 + Math.random() * (ch - 120);
            data.x = Math.max(10, Math.min(data.x, cw - 120));
            data.y = Math.max(10, Math.min(data.y, ch - 80));

            if (type === 'image') {
                data.props.src = props.src || 'https://via.placeholder.com/300x200/FF6B00/fff?text=图片';
            }
            if (type === 'container') {
                data.children = [];
                let counter = 1;
                for (const c of page.components) {
                    if (c.type === 'container' && c.props.text && c.props.text.startsWith('容器')) {
                        counter++;
                    }
                }
                data.props.text = '容器 ' + counter;
            }
            if (type === 'counter') {
                data.props.value = props.value || 0;
                data.props.text = '计数';
            }
            if (type === 'quiz') {
                data.props.questions = props.questions || [
                    { id: 1, text: '1 + 1 = ?', options: ['1', '2', '3', '4'], correct: [1], type: 'single' },
                    { id: 2, text: '以下哪些是水果？', options: ['苹果', '香蕉', '白菜', '萝卜'], correct: [0, 1],
                    type: 'multi' }
                ];
                data.userAnswers = {};
            }

            page.components.push(data);
            const el = renderComponent(data);
            data.element = el;
            canvas.appendChild(el);
            ensureCanvasSize();
            selectComponent(data.id);
            updateCodePanel();
            autoSave();
            showToast(`已添加 ${getTypeLabel(type, subtype)}`);
            return data.id;
        }

        function ensureCanvasSize() {
            const page = getCurrentPage();
            if (!page) return;
            let maxX = 0,
                maxY = 0;
            page.components.forEach(c => {
                const w = typeof c.width === 'number' ? c.width : 100;
                const h = typeof c.height === 'number' ? c.height : 40;
                const rx = (c.x || 0) + w + 40;
                const ry = (c.y || 0) + h + 40;
                if (rx > maxX) maxX = rx;
                if (ry > maxY) maxY = ry;
            });
            const minW = 800,
                minH = 600;
            const newW = Math.max(minW, maxX + 100);
            const newH = Math.max(minH, maxY + 100);
            canvas.style.minHeight = newH + 'px';
            canvas.style.width = '100%';
            canvas.style.height = newH + 'px';
        }

        function deleteComponent(id) {
            const page = getCurrentPage();
            if (!page) return;
            const idx = page.components.findIndex(c => c.id === id);
            if (idx === -1) {
                for (const c of page.components) {
                    if (c.type === 'container' && c.children) {
                        const childIdx = c.children.findIndex(ch => ch.id === id);
                        if (childIdx !== -1) {
                            const child = c.children[childIdx];
                            if (child.element && child.element.parentNode) {
                                child.element.parentNode.removeChild(child.element);
                            }
                            c.children.splice(childIdx, 1);
                            if (state.selectedId === id) {
                                state.selectedId = null;
                                updatePropertiesPanel();
                                updateCodePanel();
                            }
                            autoSave();
                            showToast('已删除容器内组件');
                            return;
                        }
                    }
                    if (c.type === 'pager' && c.props.pages) {
                        for (const pg of c.props.pages) {
                            if (pg.children) {
                                const childIdx = pg.children.findIndex(ch => ch.id === id);
                                if (childIdx !== -1) {
                                    if (pg.children[childIdx].element && pg.children[childIdx].element.parentNode) {
                                        pg.children[childIdx].element.parentNode.removeChild(pg.children[childIdx].element);
                                    }
                                    pg.children.splice(childIdx, 1);
                                    if (state.selectedId === id) {
                                        state.selectedId = null;
                                        updatePropertiesPanel();
                                        updateCodePanel();
                                    }
                                    refreshComponent(c);
                                    autoSave();
                                    showToast('已删除分页内组件');
                                    return;
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
                showToast('组件不存在');
                return;
            }
            const data = page.components[idx];
            if (data.element && data.element.parentNode) {
                data.element.parentNode.removeChild(data.element);
            }
            if (data.type === 'container' && data.children) {
                for (const child of data.children) {
                    if (child.element && child.element.parentNode) {
                        child.element.parentNode.removeChild(child.element);
                    }
                }
            }
            page.components.splice(idx, 1);
            delete state.inputValues[id];
            if (state.selectedId === id) {
                state.selectedId = null;
                updatePropertiesPanel();
                updateCodePanel();
            }
            if (page.components.length === 0) {
                canvas.innerHTML = `<div class="empty-hint">👈 拖拽组件到此处 · 短按选中 · 长按组件拖动</div>`;
                canvas.style.minHeight = '500px';
                canvas.style.height = '500px';
            } else {
                ensureCanvasSize();
            }
            autoSave();
            showToast('已删除');
        }

        function selectComponent(id) {
            const page = getCurrentPage();
            if (!page) return;
            if (state.selectedId !== null) {
                const old = page.components.find(c => c.id === state.selectedId);
                if (old && old.element) old.element.classList.remove('selected');
                for (const c of page.components) {
                    if (c.type === 'container' && c.children) {
                        const child = c.children.find(ch => ch.id === state.selectedId);
                        if (child && child.element) child.element.classList.remove('selected');
                    }
                    if (c.type === 'pager' && c.props.pages) {
                        for (const pg of c.props.pages) {
                            if (pg.children) {
                                const child = pg.children.find(ch => ch.id === state.selectedId);
                                if (child && child.element) child.element.classList.remove('selected');
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            state.selectedId = id;
            const comp = page.components.find(c => c.id === id);
            if (comp && comp.element) {
                comp.element.classList.add('selected');
                comp.element.scrollIntoView({ block: 'nearest', behavior: 'smooth' });
            } else {
                for (const c of page.components) {
                    if (c.type === 'container' && c.children) {
                        const child = c.children.find(ch => ch.id === id);
                        if (child && child.element) {
                            child.element.classList.add('selected');
                            child.element.scrollIntoView({ block: 'nearest', behavior: 'smooth' });
                            break;
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            updatePropertiesPanel();
            updateCodePanel();
        }

        function refreshComponent(data) {
            if (!data.element) return;
            const parent = data.element.parentNode;
            if (!parent) return;
            const renderOptions = data.parentId ? { containerId: data.parentId } : {};
            const newEl = renderComponent(data, renderOptions);
            parent.replaceChild(newEl, data.element);
            data.element = newEl;
            if (state.selectedId === data.id) {
                newEl.classList.add('selected');
            }
            if (data.type === 'container' && data.children) {
                for (const child of data.children) {
                    const childEl = newEl.querySelector(`.canvas-component[data-id="${child.id}"]`);
                    if (childEl) {
                        child.element = childEl;
                    }
                }
            }
            if (data.type === 'pager' && data.props.pages) {
                for (const pg of data.props.pages) {
                    if (pg.children) {
                        for (const child of pg.children) {
                            const childEl = newEl.querySelector(`.canvas-component[data-id="${child.id}"]`);
                            if (childEl) {
                                child.element = childEl;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            ensureCanvasSize();
        }

        // ================================================================
        //  拖拽
        // ================================================================
        function startDragMove(id, startX, startY) {
            const page = getCurrentPage();
            if (!page) return;
            const comp = page.components.find(c => c.id === id);
            if (!comp) return;
            const el = comp.element;
            if (!el) return;
            selectComponent(id);
            el.classList.add('dragging');
            const rect = el.getBoundingClientRect();
            const canvasRect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
            const offsetX = startX - rect.left;
            const offsetY = startY - rect.top;

            const onMove = (ev) => {
                if (ev.cancelable) ev.preventDefault();
                const cx = ev.clientX || ev.touches?.[0]?.clientX || startX;
                const cy = ev.clientY || ev.touches?.[0]?.clientY || startY;
                const newX = cx - canvasRect.left - offsetX;
                const newY = cy - canvasRect.top - offsetY;
                comp.x = Math.max(0, newX);
                comp.y = Math.max(0, newY);
                el.style.left = comp.x + 'px';
                el.style.top = comp.y + 'px';
                if (comp.type === 'container' && comp.children) {
                    for (const child of comp.children) {
                        if (child.element) {
                            child.element.style.left = (child.x || 10) + 'px';
                            child.element.style.top = (child.y || 10) + 'px';
                        }
                    }
                }
                updatePropertiesPanel();
                ensureCanvasSize();
            };

            const onUp = () => {
                document.removeEventListener('mousemove', onMove);
                document.removeEventListener('mouseup', onUp);
                document.removeEventListener('touchmove', onMove);
                document.removeEventListener('touchend', onUp);
                document.removeEventListener('touchcancel', onUp);
                el.classList.remove('dragging');
                updateCodePanel();
                autoSave();
                ensureCanvasSize();
            };

            document.addEventListener('mousemove', onMove);
            document.addEventListener('mouseup', onUp);
            document.addEventListener('touchmove', onMove, { passive: false });
            document.addEventListener('touchend', onUp, { passive: true });
            document.addEventListener('touchcancel', onUp, { passive: true });
        }

        function startDragResize(id, startX, startY) {
            const page = getCurrentPage();
            if (!page) return;
            const comp = page.components.find(c => c.id === id);
            if (!comp) return;
            const el = comp.element;
            if (!el) return;
            const rect = el.getBoundingClientRect();
            const startW = rect.width;
            const startH = rect.height;
            const baseW = comp.baseWidth || startW || 40;
            const baseH = comp.baseHeight || startH || 24;

            const onMove = (ev) => {
                if (ev.cancelable) ev.preventDefault();
                const cx = ev.clientX || ev.touches?.[0]?.clientX || startX;
                const cy = ev.clientY || ev.touches?.[0]?.clientY || startY;
                const dw = cx - startX;
                const dh = cy - startY;
                let newW = Math.max(40, startW + dw);
                let newH = Math.max(24, startH + dh);
                const scaleX = newW / baseW;
                const scaleY = newH / baseH;
                const nextScale = Math.max(0.2, Math.min(6, Math.min(scaleX, scaleY)));
                comp.width = newW;
                comp.height = newH;
                comp.scale = nextScale;
                el.style.width = newW + 'px';
                el.style.height = newH + 'px';
                const content = el.querySelector('.comp-content');
                if (content) content.style.transform = `scale(${nextScale})`;
                updatePropertiesPanel();
                ensureCanvasSize();
            };

            const onUp = () => {
                document.removeEventListener('mousemove', onMove);
                document.removeEventListener('mouseup', onUp);
                document.removeEventListener('touchmove', onMove);
                document.removeEventListener('touchend', onUp);
                updateCodePanel();
                autoSave();
                ensureCanvasSize();
            };

            document.addEventListener('mousemove', onMove);
            document.addEventListener('mouseup', onUp);
            document.addEventListener('touchmove', onMove, { passive: false });
            document.addEventListener('touchend', onUp, { passive: true });
        }

        // ================================================================
        //  属性面板
        // ================================================================
        function updatePropertiesPanel() {
            const page = getCurrentPage();
            if (!page) { propContent.innerHTML = `<div class="no-selection">请先创建页面</div>`; return; }
            let comp = page.components.find(c => c.id === state.selectedId);
            if (!comp) {
                for (const c of page.components) {
                    if (c.type === 'container' && c.children) {
                        const child = c.children.find(ch => ch.id === state.selectedId);
                        if (child) { comp = child; break; }
                    }
                    if (c.type === 'pager' && c.props.pages) {
                        let found = false;
                        for (const pg of c.props.pages) {
                            if (pg.children) {
                                const child = pg.children.find(ch => ch.id === state.selectedId);
                                if (child) { comp = child; found = true; break; }
                            }
                        }
                        if (found) break;
                    }
                }
            }
            if (!comp) {
                // 未选中组件时显示页面背景设置
                const pg = getCurrentPage();
                const bgImg = pg ? (pg.bgImage || '') : '';
                const bgBlur = pg ? (pg.bgBlur || 0) : 0;
                const bgFit = pg ? (pg.bgFit || 'cover') : 'cover';
                propContent.innerHTML = `<div style="padding:4px 0;">
                    <div class="prop-group"><label>🖼️ 页面背景图</label>
                        <div style="display:flex;gap:4px;align-items:center;">
                            <input type="text" id="propPageBgImage" value="${escapeHtml(bgImg)}" placeholder="文件路径或URL" style="flex:1;padding:4px 8px;border:1px solid #dadce0;border-radius:5px;font-size:12px;" />
                            <button id="btnPageBgPick" style="padding:4px 10px;border:1px solid var(--primary);border-radius:5px;background:transparent;color:var(--primary);cursor:pointer;font-size:11px;white-space:nowrap;">选择</button>
                        </div>
                        <div style="font-size:10px;color:var(--text-secondary);margin-top:3px;">支持本地路径(如 /sdcard/bg.jpg)或URL</div>
                    </div>
                    <div class="prop-group"><label>🌫️ 背景虚化(px)</label>
                        <input type="number" id="propPageBgBlur" value="${bgBlur}" min="0" max="50" />
                    </div>
                    <div class="prop-group"><label>📐 适应方式</label>
                        <select id="propPageBgFit" style="width:100%;padding:4px 8px;border:1px solid #dadce0;border-radius:5px;font-size:12px;">
                            <option value="cover" ${bgFit==='cover'?'selected':''}>cover(铺满)</option>
                            <option value="contain" ${bgFit==='contain'?'selected':''}>contain(适应)</option>
                            <option value="fill" ${bgFit==='fill'?'selected':''}>fill(拉伸)</option>
                            <option value="auto" ${bgFit==='auto'?'selected':''}>auto(原始)</option>
                        </select>
                    </div>
                    <div style="margin-top:8px;">
                        <button id="btnClearPageBg" style="padding:4px 12px;border:1px solid #ea4335;border-radius:5px;background:transparent;color:#ea4335;cursor:pointer;font-size:12px;width:100%;">清除背景图</button>
                    </div>
                </div>`;
                propTypeLabel.textContent = '📄 页面设置';

                // 绑定页面背景事件
                setTimeout(() => {
                    const bgInput = document.getElementById('propPageBgImage');
                    const blurInput = document.getElementById('propPageBgBlur');
                    const fitSelect = document.getElementById('propPageBgFit');
                    const pickBtn = document.getElementById('btnPageBgPick');
                    const clearBtn = document.getElementById('btnClearPageBg');
                    if (bgInput) {
                        bgInput.addEventListener('input', () => {
                            pg.bgImage = bgInput.value;
                            renderPage(pg);
                            autoSave();
                        });
                    }
                    if (blurInput) {
                        blurInput.addEventListener('input', () => {
                            pg.bgBlur = parseInt(blurInput.value) || 0;
                            renderPage(pg);
                            autoSave();
                        });
                    }
                    if (fitSelect) {
                        fitSelect.addEventListener('change', () => {
                            pg.bgFit = fitSelect.value;
                            renderPage(pg);
                            autoSave();
                        });
                    }
                    if (pickBtn) {
                        pickBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
                            const path = prompt('请输入背景图文件路径：', '/storage/emulated/0/Pictures/');
                            if (path && path.trim()) {
                                pg.bgImage = path.trim();
                                renderPage(pg);
                                updatePropertiesPanel();
                                autoSave();
                                showToast('✅ 背景图已设置');
                            }
                        });
                    }
                    if (clearBtn) {
                        clearBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
                            pg.bgImage = '';
                            pg.bgBlur = 0;
                            renderPage(pg);
                            updatePropertiesPanel();
                            autoSave();
                            showToast('背景图已清除');
                        });
                    }
                }, 50);
                return;
            }

            propTypeLabel.textContent = getTypeLabel(comp.type, comp.subtype);
            const p = comp.props;
            const action = comp.action || { type: 'none', params: {} };

            let html = '';

            html += `<div class="prop-group">
                        <div class="btn-row">
                            <button class="btn-del-comp" id="propDelBtn">🗑 删除</button>
                            <button class="btn-copy-comp" id="propCopyBtn">📋 复制</button>
                        </div>
                    </div>`;

            html += `<div class="prop-group">
                        <label>📍 位置</label>
                        <div class="pos-row">
                            <input type="number" id="propX" value="${Math.round(comp.x||0)}" placeholder="X" />
                            <input type="number" id="propY" value="${Math.round(comp.y||0)}" placeholder="Y" />
                        </div>
                    </div>`;

            html += `<div class="prop-group">
                        <label>📐 尺寸</label>
                        <div class="pos-row">
                            <input type="number" id="propW" value="${typeof comp.width==='number'?Math.round(comp.width):''}" placeholder="宽" />
                            <input type="number" id="propH" value="${typeof comp.height==='number'?Math.round(comp.height):''}" placeholder="高" />
                        </div>
                    </div>`;

            html += `<div class="prop-group">
                        <label>🔍 内容缩放比例</label>
                        <input type="number" id="propScale" value="${Math.round((comp.scale || 1) * 100)}" min="20" max="600" step="5" />
                    </div>`;

            html += `<div class="prop-group">
                        <label>↔️ 内容水平对齐</label>
                        <div class="btn-row">
                            <button type="button" class="content-align-x-btn" data-align="left" style="background:${(comp.contentAlignX || 'center') === 'left' ? 'var(--primary)' : '#f1f3f4'};color:${(comp.contentAlignX || 'center') === 'left' ? '#fff' : 'var(--text)'};">左</button>
                            <button type="button" class="content-align-x-btn" data-align="center" style="background:${(comp.contentAlignX || 'center') === 'center' ? 'var(--primary)' : '#f1f3f4'};color:${(comp.contentAlignX || 'center') === 'center' ? '#fff' : 'var(--text)'};">中</button>
                            <button type="button" class="content-align-x-btn" data-align="right" style="background:${(comp.contentAlignX || 'center') === 'right' ? 'var(--primary)' : '#f1f3f4'};color:${(comp.contentAlignX || 'center') === 'right' ? '#fff' : 'var(--text)'};">右</button>
                        </div>
                    </div>`;

            html += `<div class="prop-group">
                        <label>↕️ 内容垂直对齐</label>
                        <div class="btn-row">
                            <button type="button" class="content-align-y-btn" data-align="top" style="background:${(comp.contentAlignY || 'center') === 'top' ? 'var(--primary)' : '#f1f3f4'};color:${(comp.contentAlignY || 'center') === 'top' ? '#fff' : 'var(--text)'};">上</button>
                            <button type="button" class="content-align-y-btn" data-align="center" style="background:${(comp.contentAlignY || 'center') === 'center' ? 'var(--primary)' : '#f1f3f4'};color:${(comp.contentAlignY || 'center') === 'center' ? '#fff' : 'var(--text)'};">中</button>
                            <button type="button" class="content-align-y-btn" data-align="bottom" style="background:${(comp.contentAlignY || 'center') === 'bottom' ? 'var(--primary)' : '#f1f3f4'};color:${(comp.contentAlignY || 'center') === 'bottom' ? '#fff' : 'var(--text)'};">下</button>
                        </div>
                    </div>`;

            html += `<div class="prop-group">
                        <label>📝 文本</label>
                        <input type="text" id="propText" value="${escapeHtml(p.text||'')}" />
                    </div>`;

            // 答题特殊属性
            if (comp.type === 'quiz') {
                html += `<div class="prop-group" style="border-top:1px solid var(--border);padding-top:6px;margin-top:4px;">
                            <label>📝 标题</label>
                            <input type="text" id="quizTitle" value="${escapeHtml(p.title||'选择题')}" />
                        </div>`;
                html += `<div class="prop-group">
                            <label>📊 题目数</label>
                            <div style="display:flex;gap:4px;">
                                <input type="number" id="quizCount" value="${(p.questions||[]).length}" min="1" max="20" style="flex:1;" />
                                <button id="quizApplyCount" style="padding:2px 10px;border:none;border-radius:4px;background:var(--primary);color:#fff;cursor:pointer;font-size:11px;">更新</button>

            if (comp.type === 'container') {

                                <button id="containerRemoveChild" style="padding:2px 10px;border:none;border-radius:4px;background:#ea4335;color:#fff;font-size:11px;cursor:pointer;">➖ 移出组件</button>
                            </div>
                            <div style="margin-top:4px;font-size:10px;color:var(--text-secondary);">
                                <span>当前子组件: ${(comp.children||[]).length} 个</span>
                            </div>
                        </div>`;
            }

            if (comp.type === 'counter') {

                            <label>🔢 默认值</label>

            }

            if (comp.type === 'input') {
                html += `<div class="prop-group">
                            <label>📌 占位符</label>
                            <input type="text" id="propPlaceholder" value="${escapeHtml(p.placeholder||'')}" />
                        </div>`;
            }

            if (['input', 'radio', 'checkbox', 'select', 'switch'].includes(comp.type)) {
                const showLabel = p.showLabel !== false;
                const labelPosition = p.labelPosition || 'top';
                html += `<div class="prop-group" style="border-top:1px solid var(--border);padding-top:6px;margin-top:4px;">
                            <label>🔑 自动化变量名</label>

                        </div>`;
                html += `<div class="prop-group">
                            <label>🏷️ 显示配置名称</label>
                            <div class="btn-row">
                                <button type="button" class="label-visible-btn" data-visible="true" style="background:${showLabel ? 'var(--primary)' : '#f1f3f4'};color:${showLabel ? '#fff' : 'var(--text)'};">显示</button>
                                <button type="button" class="label-visible-btn" data-visible="false" style="background:${!showLabel ? 'var(--primary)' : '#f1f3f4'};color:${!showLabel ? '#fff' : 'var(--text)'};">隐藏</button>
                            </div>
                        </div>`;
                html += `<div class="prop-group">
                            <label>↕️ 名称位置</label>
                            <div class="btn-row">
                                <button type="button" class="label-position-btn" data-position="top" style="background:${labelPosition === 'top' ? 'var(--primary)' : '#f1f3f4'};color:${labelPosition === 'top' ? '#fff' : 'var(--text)'};">上方</button>
                                <button type="button" class="label-position-btn" data-position="left" style="background:${labelPosition === 'left' ? 'var(--primary)' : '#f1f3f4'};color:${labelPosition === 'left' ? '#fff' : 'var(--text)'};">左方</button>
                            </div>
                        </div>`;
                html += `<div class="prop-group">
                            <label>📏 名称宽度 / 间距</label>
                            <div class="pos-row">
                                <input type="number" id="propLabelWidth" value="${parseInt(p.labelWidth || 52)}" min="0" max="240" placeholder="名称宽" />
                                <input type="number" id="propLabelGap" value="${parseInt(p.labelGap || 4)}" min="0" max="80" placeholder="间距" />
                            </div>
                        </div>`;
            }

            if (['radio', 'checkbox', 'select'].includes(comp.type)) {
                html += `<div class="prop-group">
                            <label>📚 子选项（用 | 分隔）</label>
                            <input type="text" id="propOptions" value="${escapeHtml(p.options || '选项1|选项2')}" placeholder="选项1|选项2|选项3" />
                        </div>`;
                html += `<div class="prop-group">
                            <label>✅ 默认值${comp.type === 'checkbox' ? '（多项用 | 分隔）' : ''}</label>
                            <input type="text" id="propDefaultValue" value="${escapeHtml(p.defaultValue || '')}" />
                        </div>`;
            }

            if (['radio', 'checkbox'].includes(comp.type)) {
                const currentLayout = p.optionLayout || 'vertical';
                html += `<div class="prop-group">
                            <label>↔️ 子选项排列方向</label>
                            <div class="btn-row">
                                <button type="button" class="layout-btn" data-layout="vertical" style="background:${currentLayout === 'vertical' ? 'var(--primary)' : '#f1f3f4'};color:${currentLayout === 'vertical' ? '#fff' : 'var(--text)'};">竖向</button>
                                <button type="button" class="layout-btn" data-layout="horizontal" style="background:${currentLayout === 'horizontal' ? 'var(--primary)' : '#f1f3f4'};color:${currentLayout === 'horizontal' ? '#fff' : 'var(--text)'};">横向</button>
                            </div>
                        </div>`;
            }

            if (comp.type === 'switch') {
                html += `<div class="prop-group">
                            <label>🔀 开关选项（开|关）</label>
                            <input type="text" id="propOptions" value="${escapeHtml(p.options || '开|关')}" />
                        </div>`;
                html += `<div class="prop-group">
                            <label>✅ 默认状态</label>
                            <select id="propDefaultValue">
                                ${parseOptionList(p.options || '开|关').map(opt => `<option value="${escapeHtml(opt)}" ${opt === p.defaultValue ? 'selected' : ''}>${escapeHtml(opt)}</option>`).join('')}
                            </select>
                        </div>`;
            }

            if (comp.type === 'autostart') {
                html += `<div class="prop-group" style="border-top:1px solid var(--border);padding-top:6px;margin-top:4px;">
                            <label>🚀 任务名称</label>
                            <input type="text" id="propAutoTaskName" value="${escapeHtml(p.taskName || '第一个任务')}" />
                        </div>`;
                html += `<div class="prop-group">
                            <label>🔁 执行模式</label>
                            <select id="propLoopMode">
                                <option value="1" ${String(p.loopMode || '1') === '1' ? 'selected' : ''}>循环执行</option>
                                <option value="0" ${String(p.loopMode || '1') === '0' ? 'selected' : ''}>执行一次</option>
                            </select>
                        </div>`;
            }

            if (comp.type === 'message') {
                html += `<div class="prop-group">
                            <label>⏱ 显示时长 (秒)</label>
                            <input type="number" id="propDuration" value="${p.duration||2}" min="0.5" step="0.5" />
                        </div>`;
            }

            if (comp.type === 'effect') {

                                ${['fade','slide','bounce','rotate'].map(t =>
                                    `<option value="${t}" ${comp.subtype===t?'selected':''}>${t}</option>`

                        </div>`;
            }

            if (comp.type === 'image') {

                            <label>🖼️ 图片URL</label>

            if (comp.type === 'button' && comp.subtype === 'toggle') {

                        </div>
                    </div>`;

            html += `<div class="prop-group">
                        <label>🖌 背景颜色</label>
                        <div class="color-row">
                            <button type="button" class="color-swatch" id="propBgColorSwatch" style="background:${p.bgColor||'#ffffff'};"></button>
                            <input type="hidden" id="propBgColor" value="${p.bgColor||'#ffffff'}" />
                            <input type="text" id="propBgColorText" value="${p.bgColor||'#ffffff'}" />
                        </div>
                    </div>`;

            html += `<div class="prop-group">
                        <label>📏 字号 (px)</label>
                        <input type="number" id="propFontSize" value="${p.fontSize||14}" min="10" max="48" />

            html += `<div class="prop-group">
                        <label>📐 宽度 (CSS)</label>
                        <input type="text" id="propWidth" value="${p.width||'auto'}" placeholder="auto / 100% / 200px" />
                    </div>`;

            if (comp.type !== 'counter' && comp.type !== 'container' && comp.type !== 'quiz' && comp.type !== 'pager') {
                html += `<div class="prop-group" style="border-top:1px solid var(--border);padding-top:8px;margin-top:4px;">

                        </div>`;
            }

            // 分页组件：管理子页面
            if (comp.type === 'pager') {
                const pagerPages = p.pages || [];
                let pagerHtml = `<div class="prop-group" style="border-top:1px solid var(--border);padding-top:8px;margin-top:4px;">
                    <label>📑 子页面管理</label>
                    <div id="pagerPagesList" style="max-height:200px;overflow:auto;">`;
                pagerPages.forEach((pg, idx) => {
                    const isActive = idx === (p.activeIndex || 0);
                    pagerHtml += `<div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:4px;padding:4px 0;border-bottom:1px solid #f0f0f0;">
                        <span style="font-size:12px;min-width:20px;color:#9aa0a6;">${idx+1}.</span>

                        <button class="pager-del-page" data-idx="${idx}" style="padding:1px 8px;border:none;border-radius:4px;background:#ea4335;color:#fff;cursor:pointer;font-size:11px;">✕</button>
                    </div>`;
                });
                pagerHtml += `</div>
                    <div style="display:flex;gap:4px;margin-top:6px;">
                        <button id="pagerAddPage" style="padding:3px 10px;border:1px dashed var(--primary);border-radius:4px;background:transparent;cursor:pointer;color:var(--primary);font-size:12px;">+ 添加子页面</button>
                    </div>
                    <div style="display:flex;gap:4px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-top:6px;">
                        <button id="pagerAddChild" style="padding:2px 10px;border:none;border-radius:4px;background:#34a853;color:#fff;font-size:11px;cursor:pointer;">➕ 移入组件</button>
                        <button id="pagerRemoveChild" style="padding:2px 10px;border:none;border-radius:4px;background:#ea4335;color:#fff;font-size:11px;cursor:pointer;">➖ 移出组件</button>

                    <div style="margin-top:4px;font-size:10px;color:var(--text-secondary);">
                        <span>当前页面子组件: ${(pagerPages[p.activeIndex||0]?.children||[]).length} 个</span>
                    </div>
                </div>`;
                // 每个子页面的组件管理
                pagerPages.forEach((pg, pgIdx) => {
                    const pgChildren = pg.children || [];
                    const isAct = pgIdx === (p.activeIndex || 0);
                    pagerHtml += '<div class="prop-group" style="border-top:1px solid #f0f0f0;padding-top:6px;margin-top:4px;">';
                    pagerHtml += '<label style="cursor:pointer;" class="pager-pg-toggle" data-pgidx="' + pgIdx + '">🧩 ' + escapeHtml(pg.name || ('子页面'+(pgIdx+1))) + ' (' + pgChildren.length + '个组件) ' + (isAct ? '◀' : '▶') + '</label>';
                    pagerHtml += '<div class="pager-pg-comps" data-pgidx="' + pgIdx + '" style="' + (isAct ? '' : 'display:none;') + 'max-height:150px;overflow:auto;margin-top:4px;">';

                        pagerHtml += '<div style="font-size:11px;color:#9aa0a6;padding:2px 0;">暂无组件</div>';
                    } else {
                        pgChildren.forEach((child, cidx) => {
                            const cLabel = (getTypeNames()[child.type] || {}).default || child.type;
                            pagerHtml += '<div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:4px;padding:2px 0;border-bottom:1px solid #f5f5f5;font-size:11px;">';

                            pagerHtml += '<button class="pager-del-comp" data-pgidx="' + pgIdx + '" data-cidx="' + cidx + '" style="padding:0 6px;border:none;border-radius:3px;background:#ea4335;color:#fff;cursor:pointer;font-size:10px;">✕</button>';

                        });

                    pagerHtml += '<button class="pager-add-comp-btn" data-pgidx="' + pgIdx + '" style="padding:2px 10px;border:1px dashed var(--primary);border-radius:4px;background:transparent;cursor:pointer;color:var(--primary);font-size:11px;margin-top:4px;width:100%;">+ 添加组件</button>';
                    pagerHtml += '</div></div>';
                });
                pagerHtml += '<div class="prop-group" style="border-top:1px solid var(--border);padding-top:6px;margin-top:4px;">';
                pagerHtml += '<label>🎨 标签栏背景</label>';
                pagerHtml += '<input type="text" id="propTabBgColor" value="' + (p.tabBgColor||'#f1f3f4') + '" style="width:100%;padding:4px 8px;border:1px solid #dadce0;border-radius:5px;font-size:12px;" />';
                pagerHtml += '</div>';
                pagerHtml += '<div class="prop-group">';
                pagerHtml += '<label>🏷 选中标签颜色</label>';
                pagerHtml += '<input type="text" id="propTabActiveColor" value="' + (p.tabActiveColor||'#FF6B00') + '" style="width:100%;padding:4px 8px;border:1px solid #dadce0;border-radius:5px;font-size:12px;" />';
                pagerHtml += '</div>';
                pagerHtml += '<div class="prop-group">';
                pagerHtml += '<label>📏 标签栏高度</label>';
                pagerHtml += '<input type="number" id="propTabHeight" value="' + (p.tabHeight||36) + '" min="24" max="80" style="width:100%;padding:4px 8px;border:1px solid #dadce0;border-radius:5px;font-size:12px;" />';
                pagerHtml += '</div>';
                html += pagerHtml;
            }

            // 动作选择
            const actionTypes = ['none', 'dialog', 'save', 'download', 'message', 'switchPage', 'triggerButton', 'navigate',
                'runPreview', 'importData', 'exportData', 'toggleState', 'hideShow', 'disableEnable', 'replaceComponent',
                'autoConfigFinish', 'autoSaveTaskConfig', 'autoStartTask', 'autoStopTask'
            ];
            if (comp.type === 'quiz') {
                actionTypes.push('quiz_submit');
            }
            html += `<div class="prop-group" style="border-top:1px solid var(--border);padding-top:8px;margin-top:4px;">
                        <label>⚡ 点击动作</label>
                        <select id="propActionType">
                            ${actionTypes.map(t =>
                                `<option value="${t}" ${action.type===t?'selected':''}>${getActionLabel(t)}</option>`
                            ).join('')}
                        </select>
                    </div>`;

            html += `<div class="prop-group" id="actionParamsContainer">
                        <div class="action-params" id="actionParamsInner">`;

            const params = action.params || {};
            switch (action.type) {
                case 'dialog':
                    html += `
                        <div class="param-row"><label>弹窗标题</label><input type="text" id="ap_dialog_title" value="${escapeHtml(params.title||'提示')}" /></div>
                        <div class="param-row"><label>弹窗内容</label><input type="text" id="ap_dialog_content" value="${escapeHtml(params.content||'这是弹窗内容')}" /></div>
                        <div class="param-row"><label>选项 (逗号分隔)</label><input type="text" id="ap_dialog_options" value="${escapeHtml(params.options||'确定, 取消')}" placeholder="选项1, 选项2" /></div>
                        <div class="param-row"><label>确定后执行动作</label>
                            <select id="ap_dialog_nextAction">
                                <option value="none" ${params.nextAction==='none'?'selected':''}>无</option>
                                <option value="message" ${params.nextAction==='message'?'selected':''}>消息弹出</option>
                                <option value="save" ${params.nextAction==='save'?'selected':''}>保存</option>
                                <option value="navigate" ${params.nextAction==='navigate'?'selected':''}>跳转链接</option>
                                <option value="hideShow" ${params.nextAction==='hideShow'?'selected':''}>隐藏/显示</option>
                                <option value="disableEnable" ${params.nextAction==='disableEnable'?'selected':''}>禁止/允许点击</option>
                            </select>
                        </div>
                        <div class="param-row" id="ap_dialog_nextParams">
                            <label>动作参数 (JSON)</label>
                            <input type="text" id="ap_dialog_nextParamsInput" value='${escapeHtml(JSON.stringify(params.nextParams||{}))}' placeholder='{"text":"消息内容"}' />
                        </div>
                    `;
                    break;
                case 'save':
                    html += `
                        <div class="param-row"><label>保存提示</label><input type="text" id="ap_save_message" value="${escapeHtml(params.message||'✅ 已保存')}" /></div>
                        <div class="param-row"><label>显示时长 (秒)</label><input type="number" id="ap_save_duration" value="${params.duration||1}" min="0.5" step="0.5" /></div>
                    `;
                    break;
                case 'download':
                    html += `
                        <div class="param-row"><label>下载说明</label><div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--text-secondary);">下载当前页面的 HTML 文件，包含所有输入框的值。</div></div>
                    `;
                    break;
                case 'message':
                    html += `
                        <div class="param-row"><label>消息内容</label><input type="text" id="ap_message_text" value="${escapeHtml(params.text||'📢 这是一条消息')}" /></div>
                        <div class="param-row"><label>显示时长 (秒)</label><input type="number" id="ap_message_duration" value="${params.duration||1.5}" min="0.5" step="0.5" /></div>
                    `;
                    break;
                case 'switchPage':
                    html += `
                        <div class="param-row"><label>目标页面名称</label>
                            <select id="ap_switch_target">
                                ${state.pages.map(p =>
                                    `<option value="${escapeHtml(p.name)}" ${params.targetPage===p.name?'selected':''}>${escapeHtml(p.name)}</option>`
                                ).join('')}
                            </select>
                        </div>
                    `;
                    break;
                case 'triggerButton':
                    const buttons = page.components.filter(c => c.type === 'button' && c.id !== comp.id);
                    html += `
                        <div class="param-row"><label>目标按钮</label>
                            <select id="ap_trigger_target">
                                <option value="">-- 请选择 --</option>
                                ${buttons.map(b =>
                                    `<option value="${b.id}" ${params.targetId==b.id?'selected':''}>${escapeHtml(b.props.text||'按钮')} (ID:${b.id})</option>`
                                ).join('')}
                            </select>
                        </div>
                    `;
                    break;
                case 'navigate':
                    html += `
                        <div class="param-row"><label>链接地址</label><input type="text" id="ap_navigate_url" value="${escapeHtml(params.url||'https://example.com')}" placeholder="https://..." /></div>
                        <div class="param-row"><label>打开方式</label>
                            <select id="ap_navigate_target">
                                <option value="_blank" ${params.target==='_blank'?'selected':''}>新窗口</option>
                                <option value="_self" ${params.target==='_self'?'selected':''}>当前窗口</option>
                            </select>
                        </div>
                    `;
                    break;
                case 'importData':
                    html += `<div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--text-secondary);padding:4px 0;">点击按钮选择JSON文件导入输入框数据</div>`;
                    break;
                case 'exportData':
                    html += `<div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--text-secondary);padding:4px 0;">点击按钮导出所有输入框数据为JSON文件</div>`;
                    break;
                case 'hideShow':
                    const allComps = page.components.filter(c => c.id !== comp.id);
                    const containerComps = [];
                    for (const c of page.components) {
                        if (c.type === 'container' && c.children) {
                            for (const ch of c.children) {
                                if (ch.id !== comp.id) containerComps.push(ch);
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    const targetList = [...allComps, ...containerComps];
                    html += `
                        <div class="param-row"><label>目标组件</label>
                            <select id="ap_hide_target">
                                <option value="">-- 请选择 --</option>
                                ${targetList.map(c =>
                                    `<option value="${c.id}" ${params.targetId==c.id?'selected':''}>${escapeHtml(c.props.text||c.type)} (ID:${c.id})</option>`
                                ).join('')}
                            </select>
                        </div>
                    `;
                    break;
                case 'disableEnable':
                    const allComps2 = page.components.filter(c => c.id !== comp.id);
                    const containerComps2 = [];
                    for (const c of page.components) {
                        if (c.type === 'container' && c.children) {
                            for (const ch of c.children) {
                                if (ch.id !== comp.id) containerComps2.push(ch);
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    const targetList2 = [...allComps2, ...containerComps2];
                    html += `
                        <div class="param-row"><label>目标组件</label>
                            <select id="ap_disable_target">
                                <option value="">-- 请选择 --</option>
                                ${targetList2.map(c =>
                                    `<option value="${c.id}" ${params.targetId==c.id?'selected':''}>${escapeHtml(c.props.text||c.type)} (ID:${c.id})</option>`
                                ).join('')}
                            </select>
                        </div>
                    `;
                    break;
                case 'replaceComponent':
                    const allComps3 = page.components.filter(c => c.id !== comp.id);
                    const containerComps3 = [];
                    for (const c of page.components) {
                        if (c.type === 'container' && c.children) {
                            for (const ch of c.children) {
                                if (ch.id !== comp.id) containerComps3.push(ch);
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    const targetList3 = [...allComps3, ...containerComps3];
                    html += `
                        <div class="param-row"><label>目标组件</label>
                            <select id="ap_replace_target">
                                <option value="">-- 请选择 --</option>
                                ${targetList3.map(c =>
                                    `<option value="${c.id}" ${params.targetId==c.id?'selected':''}>${escapeHtml(c.props.text||c.type)} (ID:${c.id})</option>`
                                ).join('')}
                            </select>
                        </div>
                        <div class="param-row"><label>替换为</label>
                            <select id="ap_replace_type">
                                <option value="button" ${params.replaceType==='button'?'selected':''}>🔘 按钮</option>
                                <option value="input" ${params.replaceType==='input'?'selected':''}>📝 输入框</option>
                                <option value="message" ${params.replaceType==='message'?'selected':''}>💬 消息</option>
                                <option value="image" ${params.replaceType==='image'?'selected':''}>🖼️ 图片</option>
                            </select>
                        </div>
                    `;
                    break;
                case 'autoConfigFinish':
                    html += `
                        <div class="param-row"><label>任务名称</label><input type="text" id="ap_auto_task" value="${escapeHtml(params.taskName || p.taskName || '第一个任务')}" /></div>
                        <div class="param-row"><label>执行模式</label>
                            <select id="ap_auto_loop">
                                <option value="1" ${String(params.loopMode || p.loopMode || '1') === '1' ? 'selected' : ''}>循环执行</option>
                                <option value="0" ${String(params.loopMode || p.loopMode || '1') === '0' ? 'selected' : ''}>执行一次</option>
                            </select>
                        </div>
                        <div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--text-secondary);padding:4px 0;">调用 auto.config_finish(JSON配置)，并把页面表单控件收集为 configs。</div>
                    `;
                    break;
                case 'autoSaveTaskConfig':
                    html += `
                        <div class="param-row"><label>任务名称</label><input type="text" id="ap_auto_task" value="${escapeHtml(params.taskName || p.taskName || '第一个任务')}" /></div>
                        <div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--text-secondary);padding:4px 0;">调用 auto.save_task_config(taskName, JSON配置)。</div>
                    `;
                    break;
                case 'autoStartTask':
                    html += `
                        <div class="param-row"><label>任务名称</label><input type="text" id="ap_auto_task" value="${escapeHtml(params.taskName || p.taskName || '第一个任务')}" /></div>
                        <div class="param-row"><label>循环执行</label>
                            <select id="ap_auto_loop">
                                <option value="1" ${String(params.loopMode || p.loopMode || '1') === '1' ? 'selected' : ''}>是</option>
                                <option value="0" ${String(params.loopMode || p.loopMode || '1') === '0' ? 'selected' : ''}>否</option>
                            </select>
                        </div>
                        <div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--text-secondary);padding:4px 0;">调用 start_task(JSON任务列表, [], loop)。</div>
                    `;
                    break;
                case 'autoStopTask':
                    html += `<div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--text-secondary);padding:4px 0;">调用 stop_task() 停止任务。</div>`;
                    break;
                default:
                    html += `<div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--text-secondary);padding:4px 0;">无动作参数</div>`;
            }
            html += `</div></div>`;

            propContent.innerHTML = html;
            bindPropEvents(comp);
        }

        function bindPropEvents(comp) {
            const p = comp.props;
            const action = comp.action || { type: 'none', params: {} };

            propContent.querySelectorAll('input, textarea, select').forEach(bindEditableFocus);

            const delBtn = document.getElementById('propDelBtn');
            if (delBtn) delBtn.addEventListener('click', () => deleteComponent(comp.id));

            const copyBtn = document.getElementById('propCopyBtn');
            if (copyBtn) copyBtn.addEventListener('click', () => copyComponent(comp.id));

            // 答题特殊事件
            if (comp.type === 'quiz') {
                const titleInput = document.getElementById('quizTitle');
                if (titleInput) {
                    titleInput.addEventListener('input', () => {
                        p.title = titleInput.value;
                        refreshComponent(comp);
                        autoSave();
                    });
                }

                const countInput = document.getElementById('quizCount');
                const applyBtn = document.getElementById('quizApplyCount');
                if (applyBtn && countInput) {
                    applyBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
                        const count = parseInt(countInput.value) || 1;
                        updateQuizQuestions(comp, count);
                        refreshComponent(comp);
                        updatePropertiesPanel();
                        autoSave();
                        showToast('✅ 题目数量已更新');
                    });
                }

                const editBtn = document.getElementById('quizEditBtn');
                if (editBtn) {
                    editBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
                        openDialog({
                            title: '📝 编辑答题',
                            content: { type: 'quizEditor', comp: comp },
                        });
                    });
                }

                const showResultSelect = document.getElementById('quizShowResult');
                if (showResultSelect) {
                    showResultSelect.addEventListener('change', () => {
                        p.showResult = showResultSelect.value === 'true';
                        refreshComponent(comp);
                        autoSave();
                    });
                }
            }

            // 容器特殊按钮
            if (comp.type === 'container') {
                const addChildBtn = document.getElementById('containerAddChild');
                if (addChildBtn) {
                    addChildBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
                        const page = getCurrentPage();
                        if (!page) return;
                        const available = page.components.filter(c =>
                            c.id !== comp.id && c.type !== 'container' && !c.parentId
                        );
                        if (available.length === 0) {
                            showToast('没有可移入的组件，请先在画布中添加组件');
                            return;
                        }
                        const options = available.map(c =>
                            ({ label: `${c.props.text||c.type} (ID:${c.id})`, value: c.id })
                        );
                        openDialog({
                            title: '➕ 移入组件',
                            content: options,
                            type: 'radio',
                        }).then(result => {
                            if (result && result.value) {
                                const targetId = parseInt(result.value);
                                const targetComp = page.components.find(c => c.id === targetId);
                                if (!targetComp) { showToast('组件不存在'); return; }
                                const idx = page.components.indexOf(targetComp);
                                if (idx !== -1) {
                                    // 删除画布上的旧 DOM 元素
                                    if (targetComp.element && targetComp.element.parentNode) {
                                        targetComp.element.parentNode.removeChild(targetComp.element);
                                    }
                                    targetComp.element = null;
                                    page.components.splice(idx, 1);
                                    if (!comp.children) comp.children = [];
                                    targetComp.parentId = comp.id;
                                    targetComp.x = 10 + Math.random() * 50;
                                    targetComp.y = 10 + Math.random() * 50;
                                    comp.children.push(targetComp);
                                    refreshComponent(comp);
                                    ensureCanvasSize();
