                                    updateCodePanel();
                                    autoSave();
                                    showToast('✅ 已移入容器');
                                }
                            }
                        });
                    });
                }

                const removeChildBtn = document.getElementById('containerRemoveChild');
                if (removeChildBtn) {
                    removeChildBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
                        if (!comp.children || comp.children.length === 0) {
                            showToast('容器内没有组件');
                            return;
                        }
                        const options = comp.children.map(c =>
                            ({ label: `${c.props.text||c.type} (ID:${c.id})`, value: c.id })
                        );
                        openDialog({
                            title: '➖ 移出组件',
                            content: options,
                            type: 'radio',
                        }).then(result => {
                            if (result && result.value) {
                                const targetId = parseInt(result.value);
                                const idx = comp.children.findIndex(c => c.id === targetId);
                                if (idx === -1) { showToast('组件不存在'); return; }
                                const child = comp.children[idx];
                                child.parentId = undefined;
                                child.x = (comp.x || 50) + (child.x || 10) + 20;
                                child.y = (comp.y || 50) + (child.y || 10) + 20;
                                child.element = null;
                                comp.children.splice(idx, 1);
                                const page = getCurrentPage();
                                if (page) {
                                    page.components.push(child);
                                    const el = renderComponent(child);
                                    child.element = el;
                                    canvas.appendChild(el);
                                }
                                refreshComponent(comp);
                                ensureCanvasSize();
                                updateCodePanel();
                                autoSave();
                                showToast('✅ 已移出容器');
                            }
                        });
                    });
                }
            }

            if (comp.type === 'counter') {
                const valInput = document.getElementById('propCounterValue');
                if (valInput) {
                    valInput.addEventListener('input', () => {
                        const val = parseInt(valInput.value) || 0;
                        p.value = val;
                        refreshComponent(comp);
                        updateCodePanel();
                        autoSave();
                    });
                }
            }

            const xInp = document.getElementById('propX');
            const yInp = document.getElementById('propY');
            if (xInp && yInp) {
                xInp.addEventListener('input', () => {
                    const v = parseFloat(xInp.value);
                    if (!isNaN(v)) { comp.x = v; if (comp.element) comp.element.style.left = v + 'px';
                        updateCodePanel();
                        autoSave();
                        ensureCanvasSize(); }
                });
                yInp.addEventListener('input', () => {
                    const v = parseFloat(yInp.value);
                    if (!isNaN(v)) { comp.y = v; if (comp.element) comp.element.style.top = v + 'px';
                        updateCodePanel();
                        autoSave();
                        ensureCanvasSize(); }
                });
            }

            const wInp = document.getElementById('propW');
            const hInp = document.getElementById('propH');
            if (wInp && hInp) {
                wInp.addEventListener('input', () => {
                    const v = parseFloat(wInp.value);
                    if (!isNaN(v) && v > 10) { comp.width = v; if (comp.element) comp.element.style.width = v +
                            'px';
                        updateComponentScaleFromSize(comp);
                        updateCodePanel();
                        autoSave();
                        ensureCanvasSize(); }
                });
                hInp.addEventListener('input', () => {
                    const v = parseFloat(hInp.value);
                    if (!isNaN(v) && v > 10) { comp.height = v; if (comp.element) comp.element.style.height = v +
                            'px';
                        updateComponentScaleFromSize(comp);
                        updateCodePanel();
                        autoSave();
                        ensureCanvasSize(); }
                });
            }

            const scaleInp = document.getElementById('propScale');
            if (scaleInp) {
                scaleInp.addEventListener('input', () => {
                    const pct = parseFloat(scaleInp.value);
                    if (!isNaN(pct) && pct >= 20 && pct <= 600) {
                        setComponentScale(comp, pct / 100);
                        if (wInp && typeof comp.width === 'number') wInp.value = Math.round(comp.width);
                        if (hInp && typeof comp.height === 'number') hInp.value = Math.round(comp.height);
                        updateCodePanel();
                        autoSave();
                        ensureCanvasSize();
                    }
                });
            }

            document.querySelectorAll('.content-align-x-btn').forEach(btn => {
                btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
                    comp.contentAlignX = btn.dataset.align || 'center';
                    refreshComponent(comp);
                    updatePropertiesPanel();
                    updateCodePanel();
                    autoSave();
                });
            });

            document.querySelectorAll('.content-align-y-btn').forEach(btn => {
                btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
                    comp.contentAlignY = btn.dataset.align || 'center';
                    refreshComponent(comp);
                    updatePropertiesPanel();
                    updateCodePanel();
                    autoSave();
                });
            });

            // 分页组件属性绑定
            if (comp.type === 'pager') {
                const addPageBtn = document.getElementById('pagerAddPage');
                if (addPageBtn) {
                    addPageBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
                        if (!p.pages) p.pages = [];
                        p.pages.push({ name: '新页面' + (p.pages.length + 1), content: '', children: [] });
                        updatePropertiesPanel();
                        refreshComponent(comp);
                        autoSave();
                    });
                }
                // 分页移入组件
                const pagerAddChildBtn = document.getElementById('pagerAddChild');
                if (pagerAddChildBtn) {
                    pagerAddChildBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
                        const page = getCurrentPage();
                        if (!page) return;
                        const activeIdx = p.activeIndex || 0;
                        const activePg = (p.pages || [])[activeIdx];
                        if (!activePg) { showToast('没有活动子页面'); return; }
                        const available = page.components.filter(c =>
                            c.id !== comp.id && c.type !== 'pager' && !c.parentId
                        );
                        if (available.length === 0) {
                            showToast('没有可移入的组件，请先在画布中添加组件');
                            return;
                        }
                        const options = available.map(c =>
                            ({ label: (c.props.text || c.type) + ' (ID:' + c.id + ')', value: c.id })
                        );
                        openDialog({
                            title: '➕ 移入组件到: ' + (activePg.name || '子页面'),
                            content: options,
                            type: 'radio',
                        }).then(result => {
                            if (result && result.value) {
                                const targetId = parseInt(result.value);
                                const targetComp = page.components.find(c => c.id === targetId);
                                if (!targetComp) { showToast('组件不存在'); return; }
                                const idx = page.components.indexOf(targetComp);
                                if (idx !== -1) {
                                    if (targetComp.element && targetComp.element.parentNode) {
                                        targetComp.element.parentNode.removeChild(targetComp.element);
                                    }
                                    targetComp.element = null;
                                    page.components.splice(idx, 1);
                                    if (!activePg.children) activePg.children = [];
                                    targetComp.parentId = comp.id;
                                    targetComp.x = 10 + Math.random() * 50;
                                    targetComp.y = 10 + Math.random() * 50;
                                    activePg.children.push(targetComp);
                                    refreshComponent(comp);
                                    updatePropertiesPanel();
                                    ensureCanvasSize();
                                    autoSave();
                                    showToast('✅ 已移入分页');
                                }
                            }
                        });
                    });
                }
                // 分页移出组件
                const pagerRemoveChildBtn = document.getElementById('pagerRemoveChild');
                if (pagerRemoveChildBtn) {
                    pagerRemoveChildBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
                        const activeIdx = p.activeIndex || 0;
                        const activePg = (p.pages || [])[activeIdx];
                        if (!activePg || !activePg.children || activePg.children.length === 0) {
                            showToast('当前页面没有组件');
                            return;
                        }
                        const options = activePg.children.map(c =>
                            ({ label: (c.props.text || c.type) + ' (ID:' + c.id + ')', value: c.id })
                        );
                        openDialog({
                            title: '➖ 移出组件',
                            content: options,
                            type: 'radio',
                        }).then(result => {
                            if (result && result.value) {
                                const targetId = parseInt(result.value);
                                const idx = activePg.children.findIndex(c => c.id === targetId);
                                if (idx === -1) { showToast('组件不存在'); return; }
                                const child = activePg.children[idx];
                                child.parentId = undefined;
                                child.x = (comp.x || 50) + (child.x || 10) + 20;
                                child.y = (comp.y || 50) + (child.y || 10) + 20;
                                child.element = null;
                                activePg.children.splice(idx, 1);
                                const page = getCurrentPage();
                                if (page) {
                                    page.components.push(child);
                                    const el = renderComponent(child);
                                    child.element = el;
                                    canvas.appendChild(el);
                                }
                                refreshComponent(comp);
                                updatePropertiesPanel();
                                ensureCanvasSize();
                                autoSave();
                                showToast('✅ 已移出到画布');
                            }
                        });
                    });
                }
                document.querySelectorAll('.pager-del-page').forEach(btn => {
                    btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
                        const idx = parseInt(btn.dataset.idx);
                        if (p.pages && p.pages.length > 1) {
                            p.pages.splice(idx, 1);
                            if (p.activeIndex >= p.pages.length) p.activeIndex = p.pages.length - 1;
                            updatePropertiesPanel();
                            refreshComponent(comp);
                            autoSave();
                        } else {
                            showToast('至少保留一个子页面');
                        }
                    });
                });
                document.querySelectorAll('.pager-page-name').forEach(inp => {
                    inp.addEventListener('input', () => {
                        const idx = parseInt(inp.dataset.idx);
                        if (p.pages && p.pages[idx]) {
                            p.pages[idx].name = inp.value;
                            refreshComponent(comp);
                            autoSave();
                        }
                    });
                });
                const tabBgInput = document.getElementById('propTabBgColor');
                if (tabBgInput) {
                    tabBgInput.addEventListener('input', () => {
                        p.tabBgColor = tabBgInput.value;
                        refreshComponent(comp);
                        autoSave();
                    });
                }
                const tabActiveInput = document.getElementById('propTabActiveColor');
                if (tabActiveInput) {
                    tabActiveInput.addEventListener('input', () => {
                        p.tabActiveColor = tabActiveInput.value;
                        refreshComponent(comp);
                        autoSave();
                    });
                }
                const tabHInput = document.getElementById('propTabHeight');
                if (tabHInput) {
                    tabHInput.addEventListener('input', () => {
                        p.tabHeight = parseInt(tabHInput.value) || 36;
                        refreshComponent(comp);
                        autoSave();
                    });
                }
                // 分页折叠切换
                document.querySelectorAll('.pager-pg-toggle').forEach(toggle => {
                    toggle.addEventListener('click', () => {
                        const pgidx = toggle.dataset.pgidx;
                        const compsDiv = document.querySelector('.pager-pg-comps[data-pgidx="' + pgidx + '"]');
                        if (compsDiv) {
                            const visible = compsDiv.style.display !== 'none';
                            compsDiv.style.display = visible ? 'none' : '';
                            toggle.textContent = toggle.textContent.replace(visible ? '◀' : '▶', visible ? '▶' : '◀');
                        }
                    });
                });
                // 分页内添加组件
                document.querySelectorAll('.pager-add-comp-btn').forEach(btn => {
                    btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
                        const pgidx = parseInt(btn.dataset.pgidx);
                        showPagerAddMenu(comp, pgidx);
                    });
                });
                // 分页内删除组件
                document.querySelectorAll('.pager-del-comp').forEach(btn => {
                    btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
                        const pgidx = parseInt(btn.dataset.pgidx);
                        const cidx = parseInt(btn.dataset.cidx);
                        const pg = (p.pages || [])[pgidx];
                        if (pg && pg.children && pg.children[cidx]) {
                            if (pg.children[cidx].element && pg.children[cidx].element.parentNode) {
                                pg.children[cidx].element.parentNode.removeChild(pg.children[cidx].element);
                            }
                            pg.children.splice(cidx, 1);
                            refreshComponent(comp);
                            updatePropertiesPanel();
                            autoSave();
                            showToast('已移除组件');
                        }
                    });
                });
            }

            const textInput = document.getElementById('propText');
            if (textInput) {
                textInput.addEventListener('input', () => {
                    p.text = textInput.value;
                    refreshComponent(comp);
                    updateCodePanel();
                    autoSave();
                });
            }

            const placeholderInput = document.getElementById('propPlaceholder');
            if (placeholderInput) {
                placeholderInput.addEventListener('input', () => {
                    p.placeholder = placeholderInput.value;
                    refreshComponent(comp);
                    updateCodePanel();
                    autoSave();
                });
            }

            const configNameInput = document.getElementById('propConfigName');
            if (configNameInput) {
                configNameInput.addEventListener('input', () => {
                    p.configName = configNameInput.value.trim() || ('config_' + comp.id);
                    updateCodePanel();
                    autoSave();
                });
            }

            document.querySelectorAll('.label-visible-btn').forEach(btn => {
                btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
                    p.showLabel = btn.dataset.visible === 'true';
                    refreshComponent(comp);
                    updatePropertiesPanel();
                    updateCodePanel();
                    autoSave();
                });
            });

            document.querySelectorAll('.label-position-btn').forEach(btn => {
                btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
                    p.labelPosition = btn.dataset.position || 'top';
                    refreshComponent(comp);
                    updatePropertiesPanel();
                    updateCodePanel();
                    autoSave();
                });
            });

            const labelWidthInput = document.getElementById('propLabelWidth');
            if (labelWidthInput) {
                labelWidthInput.addEventListener('input', () => {
                    p.labelWidth = Math.max(0, parseInt(labelWidthInput.value) || 0);
                    refreshComponent(comp);
                    updateCodePanel();
                    autoSave();
                });
            }

            const labelGapInput = document.getElementById('propLabelGap');
            if (labelGapInput) {
                labelGapInput.addEventListener('input', () => {
                    p.labelGap = Math.max(0, parseInt(labelGapInput.value) || 0);
                    refreshComponent(comp);
                    updateCodePanel();
                    autoSave();
                });
            }

            const optionsInput = document.getElementById('propOptions');
            if (optionsInput) {
                optionsInput.addEventListener('input', () => {
                    p.options = optionsInput.value;
                    const opts = parseOptionList(p.options);
                    if (opts.length && !opts.includes(p.defaultValue)) {
                        if (comp.type === 'checkbox') {
                            const selected = parseOptionList(p.defaultValue).filter(v => opts.includes(v));
                            p.defaultValue = selected.length ? selected.join('|') : opts[0];
                        } else {
                            p.defaultValue = opts[0];
                        }
                    }
                    refreshComponent(comp);
                    updatePropertiesPanel();
                    updateCodePanel();
                    autoSave();
                });
            }

            const defaultValueInput = document.getElementById('propDefaultValue');
            if (defaultValueInput) {
                defaultValueInput.addEventListener('input', () => {
                    p.defaultValue = defaultValueInput.value;
                    refreshComponent(comp);
                    updateCodePanel();
                    autoSave();
                });
                defaultValueInput.addEventListener('change', () => {
                    p.defaultValue = defaultValueInput.value;
                    refreshComponent(comp);
                    updateCodePanel();
                    autoSave();
                });
            }

            document.querySelectorAll('.layout-btn').forEach(btn => {
                btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
                    p.optionLayout = btn.dataset.layout || 'vertical';
                    refreshComponent(comp);
                    updatePropertiesPanel();
                    updateCodePanel();
                    autoSave();
                });
            });

            const autoTaskNameInput = document.getElementById('propAutoTaskName');
            if (autoTaskNameInput) {
                autoTaskNameInput.addEventListener('input', () => {
                    p.taskName = autoTaskNameInput.value || '第一个任务';
                    if (!comp.action) comp.action = { type: 'autoConfigFinish', params: {} };
                    comp.action.params.taskName = p.taskName;
                    updateCodePanel();
                    autoSave();
                });
            }

            const loopModeSelect = document.getElementById('propLoopMode');
            if (loopModeSelect) {
                loopModeSelect.addEventListener('change', () => {
                    p.loopMode = loopModeSelect.value;
                    if (!comp.action) comp.action = { type: 'autoConfigFinish', params: {} };
                    comp.action.params.loopMode = p.loopMode;
                    updateCodePanel();
                    autoSave();
                });
            }

            const durInput = document.getElementById('propDuration');
            if (durInput) {
                durInput.addEventListener('input', () => {
                    p.duration = parseFloat(durInput.value) || 2;
                    refreshComponent(comp);
                    updateCodePanel();
                    autoSave();
                });
            }

            const effectType = document.getElementById('propEffectType');
            if (effectType) {
                effectType.addEventListener('change', () => {
                    comp.subtype = effectType.value;
                    refreshComponent(comp);
                    updatePropertiesPanel();
                    updateCodePanel();
                    autoSave();
                });
            }

            const imgSrc = document.getElementById('propImageSrc');
            if (imgSrc) {
                imgSrc.addEventListener('input', () => {
                    p.src = imgSrc.value;
                    refreshComponent(comp);
                    updateCodePanel();
                    autoSave();
                });
            }
            const imgAlt = document.getElementById('propImageAlt');
            if (imgAlt) {
                imgAlt.addEventListener('input', () => {
                    p.alt = imgAlt.value;
                    refreshComponent(comp);
                    updateCodePanel();
                    autoSave();
                });
            }
            const imgUpload = document.getElementById('propImageUpload');
            if (imgUpload) {
                imgUpload.addEventListener('change', function(e) {
                    const file = this.files[0];
                    if (!file) return;
                    const reader = new FileReader();
                    reader.onload = function(ev) {
                        p.src = ev.target.result;
                        refreshComponent(comp);
                        updateCodePanel();
                        autoSave();
                        showToast('🖼️ 图片已上传');
                    };
                    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
                    this.value = '';
                });
            }

            const toggleStates = document.getElementById('propToggleStates');
            if (toggleStates) {
                toggleStates.addEventListener('input', () => {
                    const states = toggleStates.value.split(',').map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
                    if (states.length > 0) {
                        p.toggleStates = states;
                        refreshComponent(comp);
                        updateCodePanel();
                        autoSave();
                    }
                });
            }
            const toggleIndex = document.getElementById('propToggleIndex');
            if (toggleIndex) {
                toggleIndex.addEventListener('input', () => {
                    const idx = parseInt(toggleIndex.value) || 0;
                    const states = p.toggleStates || ['编辑', '完成', '已保存', '发布'];
                    if (idx >= 0 && idx < states.length) {
                        comp.toggleState = idx;
                        p.text = states[idx];
                        refreshComponent(comp);
                        updateCodePanel();
                        autoSave();
                    }
                });
            }

            const colorInput = document.getElementById('propColor');
            const colorText = document.getElementById('propColorText');
            const colorSwatch = document.getElementById('propColorSwatch');
            if (colorInput && colorText && colorSwatch) {
                const setTextColor = (value) => {
                    colorInput.value = value;
                    colorText.value = value;
                    colorSwatch.style.background = value || 'transparent';
                    p.color = value;
                    refreshComponent(comp);
                    updateCodePanel();
                    autoSave();
                };
                const openTextPicker = (e) => {
                    e.stopPropagation();
                    openCustomColorPicker(colorSwatch, colorText.value || p.color || '#202124', setTextColor);
                };
                colorSwatch.addEventListener('click', openTextPicker);
                colorSwatch.addEventListener('touchend', openTextPicker, { passive: true });
                colorText.addEventListener('input', () => {
                    if (/^#[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$/.test(colorText.value.trim())) {
                        setTextColor(colorText.value.trim().startsWith('#') ? colorText.value.trim().toUpperCase() : ('#' + colorText.value.trim()).toUpperCase());
                    }
                });
            }

            const bgInput = document.getElementById('propBgColor');
            const bgText = document.getElementById('propBgColorText');
            const bgSwatch = document.getElementById('propBgColorSwatch');
            if (bgInput && bgText && bgSwatch) {
                const setBgColor = (value) => {
                    bgInput.value = value;
                    bgText.value = value;
                    bgSwatch.style.background = value || 'transparent';
                    p.bgColor = value;
                    refreshComponent(comp);
                    updateCodePanel();
                    autoSave();
                };
                const openBgPicker = (e) => {
                    e.stopPropagation();
                    openCustomColorPicker(bgSwatch, bgText.value || p.bgColor || '#ffffff', setBgColor);
                };
                bgSwatch.addEventListener('click', openBgPicker);
                bgSwatch.addEventListener('touchend', openBgPicker, { passive: true });
                bgText.addEventListener('input', () => {
                    if (/^#[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$/.test(bgText.value.trim())) {
                        setBgColor(bgText.value.trim().startsWith('#') ? bgText.value.trim().toUpperCase() : ('#' + bgText.value.trim()).toUpperCase());
                    }
                });
            }

            const fontSizeInput = document.getElementById('propFontSize');
            if (fontSizeInput) {
                fontSizeInput.addEventListener('input', () => {
                    p.fontSize = parseInt(fontSizeInput.value) || 14;
                    refreshComponent(comp);
                    updateCodePanel();
                    autoSave();
                });
            }

            const widthInput = document.getElementById('propWidth');
            if (widthInput) {
                widthInput.addEventListener('input', () => {
                    syncComponentWidthFromCss(comp, widthInput.value || 'auto');
                    refreshComponent(comp);
                    updateCodePanel();
                    autoSave();
                });
            }

            const cssInput = document.getElementById('propCustomCss');
            if (cssInput) {
                cssInput.addEventListener('input', () => {
                    comp.customCss = cssInput.value;
                    refreshComponent(comp);
                    updateCodePanel();
                    autoSave();
                });
            }
            const jsInput = document.getElementById('propCustomJs');
            if (jsInput) {
                jsInput.addEventListener('input', () => {
                    comp.customJs = jsInput.value;
                    refreshComponent(comp);
                    updateCodePanel();
                    autoSave();
                });
            }

            const actionTypeSelect = document.getElementById('propActionType');
            if (actionTypeSelect) {
                actionTypeSelect.addEventListener('change', () => {
                    const newType = actionTypeSelect.value;
                    comp.action = { type: newType, params: {} };
                    switch (newType) {
                        case 'dialog':
                            comp.action.params = { title: '提示', content: '这是弹窗内容', options: '确定, 取消',
                                nextAction: 'none', nextParams: {} };
                            break;
                        case 'save':
                            comp.action.params = { message: '✅ 已保存', duration: 1 };
                            break;
                        case 'download':
                            comp.action.params = {};
                            break;
                        case 'message':
                            comp.action.params = { text: '📢 这是一条消息', duration: 1.5 };
                            break;
                        case 'switchPage':
                            comp.action.params = { targetPage: state.pages.length > 0 ? state.pages[0].name :
                                    '' };
                            break;
                        case 'triggerButton':
                            comp.action.params = { targetId: '' };
                            break;
                        case 'navigate':
                            comp.action.params = { url: 'https://example.com', target: '_blank' };
                            break;
                        case 'importData':
                            comp.action.params = {};
                            break;
                        case 'exportData':
                            comp.action.params = {};
                            break;
                        case 'hideShow':
                            comp.action.params = { targetId: '' };
                            break;
                        case 'disableEnable':
                            comp.action.params = { targetId: '' };
                            break;
                        case 'replaceComponent':
                            comp.action.params = { targetId: '', replaceType: 'button' };
                            break;
                        case 'quiz_submit':
                            comp.action.params = {};
                            break;
                        case 'autoConfigFinish':
                        case 'autoSaveTaskConfig':
                        case 'autoStartTask':
                            comp.action.params = { taskName: p.taskName || '第一个任务', loopMode: p.loopMode || '1' };
                            break;
                        case 'autoStopTask':
                            comp.action.params = {};
                            break;
                        default:
                            comp.action.params = {};
                    }
                    refreshComponent(comp);
                    updatePropertiesPanel();
                    updateCodePanel();
                    autoSave();
                    showToast('动作已切换: ' + getActionLabel(newType));
                });
            }

            setTimeout(() => {
                bindActionParams(comp);
            }, 50);
        }

        function bindActionParams(comp) {
            const action = comp.action;
            if (!action) return;
            const params = action.params || {};
            const type = action.type;

            switch (type) {
                case 'dialog': {
                    const titleInp = document.getElementById('ap_dialog_title');
                    const contentInp = document.getElementById('ap_dialog_content');
                    const optionsInp = document.getElementById('ap_dialog_options');
                    const nextActionSel = document.getElementById('ap_dialog_nextAction');
                    const nextParamsInp = document.getElementById('ap_dialog_nextParamsInput');
                    if (titleInp) titleInp.addEventListener('input', () => { params.title = titleInp.value;
                        updateCodePanel();
                        autoSave(); });
                    if (contentInp) contentInp.addEventListener('input', () => { params.content = contentInp.value;
                        updateCodePanel();
                        autoSave(); });
                    if (optionsInp) optionsInp.addEventListener('input', () => { params.options = optionsInp.value;
                        updateCodePanel();
                        autoSave(); });
                    if (nextActionSel) {
                        nextActionSel.addEventListener('change', () => {
                            params.nextAction = nextActionSel.value;
                            updateCodePanel();
                            autoSave();
                        });
                    }
                    if (nextParamsInp) {
                        nextParamsInp.addEventListener('input', () => {
                            try {
                                params.nextParams = JSON.parse(nextParamsInp.value || '{}');
                            } catch (e) {
                                params.nextParams = {};
                            }
                            updateCodePanel();
                            autoSave();
                        });
                    }
                    break;
                }
                case 'save': {
                    const msgInp = document.getElementById('ap_save_message');
                    const durInp = document.getElementById('ap_save_duration');
                    if (msgInp) msgInp.addEventListener('input', () => { params.message = msgInp.value;
                        updateCodePanel();
                        autoSave(); });
                    if (durInp) durInp.addEventListener('input', () => { params.duration = parseFloat(durInp.value) ||
                            1;
                        updateCodePanel();
                        autoSave(); });
                    break;
                }
                case 'message': {
                    const textInp = document.getElementById('ap_message_text');
                    const durInp = document.getElementById('ap_message_duration');
                    if (textInp) textInp.addEventListener('input', () => { params.text = textInp.value;
                        updateCodePanel();
                        autoSave(); });
                    if (durInp) durInp.addEventListener('input', () => { params.duration = parseFloat(durInp.value) ||
                            1.5;
                        updateCodePanel();
                        autoSave(); });
                    break;
                }
                case 'switchPage': {
                    const targetSel = document.getElementById('ap_switch_target');
                    if (targetSel) {
                        targetSel.addEventListener('change', () => {
                            params.targetPage = targetSel.value;
                            updateCodePanel();
                            autoSave();
                        });
                    }
                    break;
                }
                case 'triggerButton': {
                    const targetSel = document.getElementById('ap_trigger_target');
                    if (targetSel) {
                        targetSel.addEventListener('change', () => {
                            params.targetId = targetSel.value;
                            updateCodePanel();
                            autoSave();
                        });
                    }
                    break;
                }
                case 'navigate': {
                    const urlInp = document.getElementById('ap_navigate_url');
                    const targetSel = document.getElementById('ap_navigate_target');
                    if (urlInp) urlInp.addEventListener('input', () => { params.url = urlInp.value;
                        updateCodePanel();
                        autoSave(); });
                    if (targetSel) targetSel.addEventListener('change', () => { params.target = targetSel.value;
                        updateCodePanel();
                        autoSave(); });
                    break;
                }
                case 'autoConfigFinish':
                case 'autoSaveTaskConfig':
                case 'autoStartTask': {
                    const taskInp = document.getElementById('ap_auto_task');
                    const loopSel = document.getElementById('ap_auto_loop');
                    if (taskInp) taskInp.addEventListener('input', () => {
                        params.taskName = taskInp.value || '第一个任务';
                        p.taskName = params.taskName;
                        updateCodePanel();
                        autoSave();
                    });
                    if (loopSel) loopSel.addEventListener('change', () => {
                        params.loopMode = loopSel.value;
                        p.loopMode = params.loopMode;
                        updateCodePanel();
                        autoSave();
                    });
                    break;
                }
                case 'hideShow': {
                    const targetSel = document.getElementById('ap_hide_target');
                    if (targetSel) {
                        targetSel.addEventListener('change', () => {
                            params.targetId = targetSel.value;
                            updateCodePanel();
                            autoSave();
                        });
                    }
                    break;
                }
                case 'disableEnable': {
                    const targetSel = document.getElementById('ap_disable_target');
                    if (targetSel) {
                        targetSel.addEventListener('change', () => {
                            params.targetId = targetSel.value;
                            updateCodePanel();
                            autoSave();
                        });
                    }
                    break;
                }
                case 'replaceComponent': {
                    const targetSel = document.getElementById('ap_replace_target');
                    const typeSel = document.getElementById('ap_replace_type');
                    if (targetSel) {
                        targetSel.addEventListener('change', () => {
                            params.targetId = targetSel.value;
                            updateCodePanel();
                            autoSave();
                        });
                    }
                    if (typeSel) {
                        typeSel.addEventListener('change', () => {
                            params.replaceType = typeSel.value;
                            updateCodePanel();
                            autoSave();
                        });
                    }
                    break;
                }
                default:
                    break;
            }
        }

        // ================================================================
        //  自定义组件编辑器
        // ================================================================
        function showCustomEditor(comp) {
            openDialog({
                title: '🧩 编辑自定义组件',
                content: {
                    type: 'customEditor',
                    name: comp.props.text || '自定义组件',
                    css: comp.customCss || '',
                    js: comp.customJs || '',
                }
            }).then(result => {
                if (result) {
                    if (result.name) comp.props.text = result.name;
                    if (result.css !== undefined) comp.customCss = result.css;
                    if (result.js !== undefined) comp.customJs = result.js;
                    refreshComponent(comp);
                    selectComponent(comp.id);
                    updatePropertiesPanel();
                    updateCodePanel();
                    autoSave();
                    showToast('✅ 自定义组件已更新');
                }
            });
        }

        function createCustomComponent() {
            openDialog({
                title: '🧩 创建自定义组件',
                content: {
                    type: 'customEditor',
                    name: '',
                    css: '',
                    js: 'showToast("🧩 自定义组件");',
                }
            }).then(result => {
                if (result && result.name && result.name.trim()) {
                    const id = addComponent('custom', 'default', {
                        text: result.name.trim(),
                        customCss: result.css || '',
                        customJs: result.js || '',
                    });
                    if (id) {
                        const comp = getComponent(id);
                        if (comp) {
                            comp.customCss = result.css || '';
                            comp.customJs = result.js || '';
                            refreshComponent(comp);
                            autoSave();
                            showToast('✅ 自定义组件已创建');
                        }
                    }
                } else if (result && result.name !== undefined && !result.name.trim()) {
                    showToast('⚠️ 组件名称不能为空');
                }
            });
        }

        // ================================================================
        //  页面管理
        // ================================================================
        function createPage(name, parentId) {
            const id = 'page_' + uid();
            const page = { id, name: name || '页面 ' + (state.pages.length + 1), components: [], parentId: parentId || null, children: [] };
            state.pages.push(page);
            if (parentId) {
                const parent = state.pages.find(p => p.id === parentId);
                if (parent) {
                    if (!parent.children) parent.children = [];
                    parent.children.push(id);
                }
            }
            autoSave();
            return page;
        }

        function switchPage(id) {
            const page = getPage(id);
            if (!page) return;
            state.currentPageId = id;
            renderPage(page);
            updatePageTabs();
            state.selectedId = null;
            updatePropertiesPanel();
            updateCodePanel();
            autoSave();
            ensureCanvasSize();
        }

        function renderPage(page) {
            canvas.innerHTML = '';
            // 页面背景图层
            const bgImg = page.bgImage || '';
            const bgBlur = page.bgBlur || 0;
            const bgFit = page.bgFit || 'cover';
            if (bgImg) {
                const bgLayer = document.createElement('div');
                bgLayer.id = 'canvas-bg-layer';
                bgLayer.style.cssText = 'position:absolute;inset:0;z-index:-1;pointer-events:none;overflow:hidden;border-radius:8px;';
                const img = document.createElement('img');
                img.src = bgImg;
                img.style.cssText = 'width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:' + bgFit + ';filter:blur(' + bgBlur + 'px);transform:scale(' + (1 + bgBlur * 0.03) + ');';
                img.onerror = function() { bgLayer.style.display = 'none'; };
                bgLayer.appendChild(img);
                canvas.appendChild(bgLayer);
            }
            if (page.components.length === 0 && !bgImg) {
                canvas.innerHTML = `<div class="empty-hint">👈 拖拽组件到此处 · 短按选中 · 长按组件拖动</div>`;
                canvas.style.minHeight = '500px';
                canvas.style.height = '500px';
                return;
            }
            // 清理残留：parentId 指向容器/分页的组件不应在顶层
            page.components = page.components.filter(c => {
                if (!c.parentId) return true;
                const parent = page.components.find(p => p.id === c.parentId);
                if (parent && (parent.type === 'container' || parent.type === 'pager')) return false;
                return true;
            });
            const topLevel = page.components.filter(c => !c.parentId);
            for (const data of topLevel) {
                const el = renderComponent(data);
                data.element = el;
                canvas.appendChild(el);
                if (state.selectedId === data.id) el.classList.add('selected');
                if (data.type === 'container' && data.children) {
                    for (const child of data.children) {
                        const childEl = el.querySelector(`.canvas-component[data-id="${child.id}"]`);
                        if (childEl) child.element = childEl;
                    }
                }
                if (data.type === 'pager' && data.props.pages) {
                    for (const pg of data.props.pages) {
                        if (pg.children) {
                            for (const child of pg.children) {
                                const childEl = el.querySelector(`.canvas-component[data-id="${child.id}"]`);
                                if (childEl) child.element = childEl;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            const orphans = page.components.filter(c => c.parentId && !page.components.find(p => p.id === c.parentId));
            for (const data of orphans) {
                data.parentId = undefined;
                const el = renderComponent(data);
                data.element = el;
                canvas.appendChild(el);
                if (state.selectedId === data.id) el.classList.add('selected');
            }
            ensureCanvasSize();
        }

        function updatePageTabs() {
            pageTabs.innerHTML = '';
            const topLevel = state.pages.filter(p => !p.parentId);
            function renderTab(page, depth) {
                const tab = document.createElement('div');
                tab.className = `page-tab ${page.id === state.currentPageId ? 'active' : ''}`;
                tab.dataset.id = page.id;
                tab.style.paddingLeft = (12 + depth * 14) + 'px';
                const nameSpan = document.createElement('span');
                nameSpan.textContent = (depth > 0 ? '└ ' : '') + page.name;
                if (page.children && page.children.length > 0) nameSpan.textContent += ' ▾';
                tab.appendChild(nameSpan);
                if (page.children && page.children.length > 0) {
                    const badge = document.createElement('span');
                    badge.style.cssText = 'font-size:10px;background:rgba(255,255,255,0.3);padding:0 5px;border-radius:8px;margin-left:4px;';
                    badge.textContent = page.children.length;
                    tab.appendChild(badge);
                }
                if (state.pages.length > 1) {
                    const del = document.createElement('span');
                    del.className = 'del-page';
                    del.textContent = '✕';
                    del.title = '删除此页面';
                    del.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                        e.stopPropagation();
                        deletePage(page.id);
                    });
                    tab.appendChild(del);
                }
                const addSub = document.createElement('span');
                addSub.className = 'del-page';
                addSub.textContent = '+';
                addSub.title = '添加子页面';
                addSub.style.opacity = '0.5';
                addSub.addEventListener('mouseenter', () => addSub.style.opacity = '1');
                addSub.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => addSub.style.opacity = '0.5');
                addSub.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                    e.stopPropagation();
                    const subName = prompt('子页面名称：', page.name + ' > 子页面');
                    if (subName !== null && subName.trim()) {
                        const subPage = createPage(subName.trim(), page.id);
                        switchPage(subPage.id);
                        showToast('✅ 已创建子页面');
                    }
                });
                tab.appendChild(addSub);
                tab.addEventListener('click', () => {
                    if (page.id !== state.currentPageId) switchPage(page.id);
                });
                pageTabs.appendChild(tab);
                if (page.children && page.children.length > 0) {
                    page.children.forEach(childId => {
                        const childPage = state.pages.find(p => p.id === childId);
                        if (childPage) renderTab(childPage, depth + 1);
                    });
                }
            }
            topLevel.forEach(p => renderTab(p, 0));
        }

        function deletePage(id) {
            function collectIds(pageId) {
                const pg = state.pages.find(p => p.id === pageId);
                if (!pg) return [pageId];
                let ids = [pageId];
                if (pg.children) pg.children.forEach(cid => { ids = ids.concat(collectIds(cid)); });
                return ids;
            }
            const allIds = collectIds(id);
            if (state.pages.length - allIds.length < 1) { showToast('至少保留一个页面'); return; }
            // 从父页面的 children 中移除
            const page = state.pages.find(p => p.id === id);
            if (page && page.parentId) {
                const parent = state.pages.find(p => p.id === page.parentId);
                if (parent && parent.children) {
                    parent.children = parent.children.filter(cid => cid !== id);
                }
            }
            // 删除自身和所有子页面
            state.pages = state.pages.filter(p => !allIds.includes(p.id));
            if (allIds.includes(state.currentPageId)) switchPage(state.pages[0].id);
            else updatePageTabs();
            autoSave();
            showToast('已删除页面' + (allIds.length > 1 ? '（含子页面）' : ''));
        }

        // ================================================================
        //  代码生成 (包含答题组件)
        // ================================================================
        function wrapRuntimeComponent(comp, innerHtml) {
            const w = typeof comp.width === 'number' ? comp.width + 'px' : (comp.width || 'auto');
            const h = typeof comp.height === 'number' ? comp.height + 'px' : (comp.height || 'auto');
            const scale = Number(comp.scale || 1);
            const justifyMap = { left: 'flex-start', center: 'center', right: 'flex-end' };
            const alignMap = { top: 'flex-start', center: 'center', bottom: 'flex-end' };
            const justify = justifyMap[comp.contentAlignX || 'center'] || 'center';
            const align = alignMap[comp.contentAlignY || 'center'] || 'center';
            return `<div class="runtime-component" style="left:${comp.x || 0}px;top:${comp.y || 0}px;width:${w};height:${h};" data-id="${comp.id}" data-type="${comp.type}" data-subtype="${comp.subtype}" data-scale="${scale}">
                <div class="runtime-content" style="transform:scale(${scale});justify-content:${justify};align-items:${align};">
                    ${innerHtml}
                </div>
            </div>`;
        }

        function getComponentHTML(comp) {
            const p = comp.props;
            const style =
                `font-size:${p.fontSize||14}px;${p.color?'color:'+p.color+';':''}${p.bgColor?'background-color:'+p.bgColor+';':''}${p.width&&p.width!=='auto'?'width:'+p.width+';':''}`;
            const actionAttr = comp.action && comp.action.type && comp.action.type !== 'none' ?
                ` data-action="${comp.action.type}" data-action-params='${JSON.stringify(comp.action.params||{})}'` :
                '';
            const posAttr =
                ` data-x="${comp.x||0}" data-y="${comp.y||0}" data-width="${comp.width||'auto'}" data-height="${comp.height||'auto'}" data-id="${comp.id}" data-type="${comp.type}" data-subtype="${comp.subtype}"`;
            const toggleAttr = comp.toggleState !== undefined ? ` data-toggle="${comp.toggleState}"` : '';
            const customCssAttr = comp.customCss ? ` data-custom-css="${escapeHtml(comp.customCss)}"` : '';
            const customJsAttr = comp.customJs ? ` data-custom-js="${escapeHtml(comp.customJs)}"` : '';

            if (comp.type === 'container') {
                let childrenHtml = '';
                if (comp.children && comp.children.length > 0) {
                    for (const child of comp.children) {
                        childrenHtml += wrapRuntimeComponent(child, getComponentHTML(child));
                    }
                } else {
                    childrenHtml = `<div class="container-empty">暂无组件</div>`;
                }
                return `<div class="container-wrapper" style="background-color:${p.bgColor||'#f5f0e8'};width:100%;height:100%;min-height:120px;min-width:120px;display:flex;flex-direction:column;padding:8px;overflow:auto;"${actionAttr}${posAttr}${customCssAttr}${customJsAttr}>
                            <div class="container-title" style="position:sticky;top:0;background:rgba(255,255,255,0.9);backdrop-filter:blur(4px);z-index:5;">${escapeHtml(p.text||'容器')}</div>
                            <div class="container-inner" style="position:relative;width:100%;flex:1;min-height:60px;">
                                ${childrenHtml}
                            </div>
                        </div>`;
            }

            if (comp.type === 'counter') {
                return `<div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;gap:4px;width:100%;height:100%;justify-content:center;"${actionAttr}${posAttr}${customCssAttr}${customJsAttr}>
                            <div class="counter-display" style="background-color:${p.bgColor||'#e8e8e8'};color:${p.color||'#333'};font-size:28px;font-weight:700;padding:6px 20px;border-radius:8px;min-width:60px;text-align:center;">${p.value||0}</div>
                            <div class="counter-controls" style="display:flex;gap:6px;margin-top:4px;">
                                <button style="padding:2px 12px;border:none;border-radius:4px;background:#ddd;cursor:pointer;font-size:14px;font-weight:600;">-</button>
                                <button style="padding:2px 12px;border:none;border-radius:4px;background:#ddd;cursor:pointer;font-size:14px;font-weight:600;">+</button>
                            </div>
                        </div>`;
            }

            // 答题组件HTML生成
            if (comp.type === 'quiz') {
                const questions = p.questions || [];
                const userAnswers = comp.userAnswers || {};
                let qHtml = '';
                questions.forEach((q, idx) => {
                    const userAns = userAnswers[q.id] || [];
                    let // 新代码（添加 data-correct）
optHtml = q.options.map((opt, oi) => {
    const checked = userAns.includes(oi) ? 'checked' : '';
    const isCorrect = q.correct.includes(oi);
    const showMark = p.showResult;
    const markText = showMark ? (isCorrect ? '✅' : (checked ? '✖️' : '')) : '';
    const markColor = showMark ? (isCorrect ? '#34a853' : '#ea4335') : '';
    const bgStyle = showMark && isCorrect ? 'background:#e6f4ea;' :
        (showMark && checked && !isCorrect ? 'background:#fce8e6;' : '');
    return `
        <div class="q-opt" style="${bgStyle}display:flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;padding:3px 6px;border-radius:4px;" data-correct="${isCorrect ? 'true' : 'false'}">
            <input type="${q.type === 'single' ? 'radio' : 'checkbox'}" name="q_${q.id}" value="${oi}" ${checked} ${showMark ? 'disabled' : ''} style="width:14px;height:14px;cursor:${showMark ? 'default' : 'pointer'};flex-shrink:0;">
            <label style="font-size:13px;cursor:${showMark ? 'default' : 'pointer'};flex:1;">${String.fromCharCode(65+oi)}. ${escapeHtml(opt)}</label>
            ${markText ? `<span class="q-mark" style="font-size:12px;color:${markColor};display:inline;">${markText}</span>` : '<span class="q-mark" style="display:none;"></span>'}
        </div>
    `;
}).join('');

                    const correctText = q.correct.map(i => String.fromCharCode(65 + i)).join(', ');
                    qHtml += `
                        <div class="quiz-question" style="border:1px solid #e8eaed;border-radius:6px;padding:8px 10px;background:#fafafa;margin-bottom:8px;">
                            <div class="q-text" style="font-weight:600;font-size:14px;margin-bottom:6px;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;color:#202124;">
                                <span style="color:var(--primary);">${idx+1}.</span> ${escapeHtml(q.text)}
                                <span class="q-type ${q.type === 'single' ? 'single' : 'multi'}" style="font-size:10px;color:#fff;padding:0 8px;border-radius:10px;margin-left:auto;background:${q.type==='single'?'#1a73e8':'#34a853'};">${q.type==='single'?'单选题':'多选题'}</span>
                            </div>
                            <div class="q-options" style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:4px;padding-left:4px;">
                                ${optHtml}
                            </div>
                            ${p.showResult ? `<div style="font-size:12px;color:#1a73e8;margin-top:4px;">✅ 正确答案: ${correctText}</div>` : ''}
                        </div>
                    `;
                });

                return `
                    <div class="quiz-container" style="width:100%;height:100%;overflow:auto;background:#fff;border-radius:8px;padding:12px;box-sizing:border-box;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:8px;border:1px solid #e0e0e0;max-height:100%;"${actionAttr}${posAttr}${customCssAttr}${customJsAttr}>
                        <div class="quiz-title" style="font-size:16px;font-weight:700;color:#202124;border-bottom:2px solid var(--primary);padding-bottom:6px;position:sticky;top:0;background:#fff;z-index:2;">${escapeHtml(p.title||'选择题')}</div>
                        <div style="flex:1;overflow:auto;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:8px;padding:4px 0;">
                            ${qHtml}
                        </div>
                        <button class="quiz-submit-btn" style="padding:6px 20px;background:var(--primary);color:#fff;border:none;border-radius:5px;font-size:14px;font-weight:600;cursor:pointer;align-self:center;position:sticky;bottom:0;" onclick="if(window._showToast) window._showToast('📝 交卷完成')">📤 交卷</button>
                    </div>
                `;
            }

            let html = '';
            switch (comp.type) {
                case 'button': {
                    let cls = 'btn';
                    if (comp.subtype === 'toggle') {
                        const states = p.toggleStates || ['编辑', '完成', '已保存', '发布'];
                        const idx = comp.toggleState || 0;
                        const stateClass = ['btn-state-1', 'btn-state-2', 'btn-state-3', 'btn-state-4'];
                        cls += ' ' + (stateClass[idx % stateClass.length] || 'btn-state-1');
                        const toggleData = { states: states, current: idx };
                        html =
                            `<button class="${cls}" style="${style}"${actionAttr}${posAttr}${toggleAttr} data-toggle-states='${JSON.stringify(states)}' data-toggle-current="${idx}"${customCssAttr}${customJsAttr}>${escapeHtml(p.text||'编辑')}</button>`;
                    } else {
                        cls += ` btn-default`;
                        html =
                            `<button class="${cls}" style="${style}"${actionAttr}${posAttr}${toggleAttr}${customCssAttr}${customJsAttr}>${escapeHtml(p.text||'按钮')}</button>`;
                    }
                    break;
                }
                case 'textlabel': {
                    html =
                        `<div style="padding:2px 4px;border-radius:4px;white-space:pre-wrap;line-height:1.4;text-align:left;${style}"${actionAttr}${posAttr}${customCssAttr}${customJsAttr}>${escapeHtml(p.text||'文字说明')}</div>`;
                    break;
                }
                case 'pager': {
                    const pgs = p.pages || [];
                    const aIdx = p.activeIndex || 0;
                    const tH = p.tabHeight || 36;
                    let tabsHtml = pgs.map((pg, i) =>
                        `<div class="pager-tab${i===aIdx?' active':''}" data-pager-idx="${i}" style="padding:0 14px;height:${tH}px;display:flex;align-items:center;cursor:pointer;font-size:13px;font-weight:500;white-space:nowrap;color:${i===aIdx?(p.tabActiveColor||'#FF6B00'):(p.color||'#333')};border-bottom:${i===aIdx?'2.5px solid '+(p.tabActiveColor||'#FF6B00'):'2.5px solid transparent'};background:${i===aIdx?(p.bgColor||'#fff'):'transparent'};">${escapeHtml(pg.name||('页'+(i+1)))}</div>`
                    ).join('');
                    let pagesHtml = pgs.map((pg, i) => {
                        let pageContent = '';
                        if (pg.children && pg.children.length > 0) {
                            for (const child of pg.children) {
                                pageContent += wrapRuntimeComponent(child, getComponentHTML(child));
                            }
                        } else {
                            pageContent = `<div class="container-empty" style="color:#9aa0a6;font-size:12px;text-align:center;padding:20px;">暂无组件</div>`;
                        }
                        return `<div class="pager-page" data-pager-page="${i}" style="display:${i===aIdx?'block':'none'};padding:8px;color:${p.color||'#333'};font-size:${p.fontSize||14}px;line-height:1.5;min-height:40px;position:relative;">${pageContent}</div>`;
                    }).join('');
                    html = `<div class="pager-runtime" data-pager="${comp.id}" style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;width:100%;height:100%;overflow:hidden;border-radius:6px;border:1px solid #e0e0e0;background:${p.bgColor||'#fff'};">
                        <div class="pager-tabs" style="display:flex;flex-shrink:0;border-bottom:1px solid #e0e0e0;overflow-x:auto;background:${p.tabBgColor||'#f1f3f4'};">${tabsHtml}</div>
                        <div class="pager-pages">${pagesHtml}</div>
                    </div>`;
                    break;
                }
                case 'input': {
                    const val = state.inputValues[comp.id] !== undefined ? state.inputValues[comp.id] : (p.defaultValue || '');
                    const labelClass = (p.labelPosition || 'top') === 'left' ? 'label-left' : 'label-top';
                    const textVisualStyle = `${p.fontSize ? 'font-size:' + (parseInt(p.fontSize) || 14) + 'px;' : ''}${p.color ? 'color:' + p.color + ';' : ''}`;
                    const labelControlStyle = `gap:${parseInt(p.labelGap || 4)}px;`;
                    const labelTitleStyle = (p.labelPosition || 'top') === 'left' ? `min-width:${parseInt(p.labelWidth || 52)}px;max-width:${parseInt(p.labelWidth || 52)}px;` : '';
                    html =
                        `<div class="form-widget" style="${style}"${actionAttr}${posAttr}${customCssAttr}${customJsAttr}><div class="config-control ${labelClass}" style="${labelControlStyle}">${p.showLabel === false ? '' : `<div class="form-title" style="${textVisualStyle}${labelTitleStyle}">${escapeHtml(p.text || '输入框')}</div>`}<input class="input-field auto-config-field" type="text" data-control-type="input" data-config-name="${escapeHtml(p.configName || ('config_' + comp.id))}" placeholder="${escapeHtml(p.placeholder||'输入')}" value="${escapeHtml(val)}" style="${style}" /></div></div>`;
                    break;
                }
                case 'radio': {
                    const opts = parseOptionList(p.options || '选项1|选项2');
                    const current = p.defaultValue || opts[0] || '';
                    const name = 'radio_' + comp.id;
                    const optionLayoutStyle = (p.optionLayout || 'vertical') === 'horizontal' ? 'flex-direction:row;flex-wrap:wrap;' : '';
                    const textVisualStyle = `${p.fontSize ? 'font-size:' + (parseInt(p.fontSize) || 14) + 'px;' : ''}${p.color ? 'color:' + p.color + ';' : ''}`;
                    const labelClass = (p.labelPosition || 'top') === 'left' ? 'label-left' : 'label-top';
                    const labelControlStyle = `gap:${parseInt(p.labelGap || 4)}px;`;
                    const labelTitleStyle = (p.labelPosition || 'top') === 'left' ? `min-width:${parseInt(p.labelWidth || 52)}px;max-width:${parseInt(p.labelWidth || 52)}px;` : '';
                    html = `<div class="form-widget auto-config-field" data-control-type="radio" data-config-name="${escapeHtml(p.configName || ('config_' + comp.id))}" style="${style}"${actionAttr}${posAttr}${customCssAttr}${customJsAttr}>
                        <div class="config-control ${labelClass}" style="${labelControlStyle}">
                        ${p.showLabel === false ? '' : `<div class="form-title" style="${textVisualStyle}${labelTitleStyle}">${escapeHtml(p.text || '单选配置')}</div>`}
                        <div class="form-options" style="${optionLayoutStyle}">
                            ${opts.map(opt => `<label class="form-option" style="${textVisualStyle}"><input type="radio" name="${name}" value="${escapeHtml(opt)}" ${opt === current ? 'checked' : ''}> <span>${escapeHtml(opt)}</span></label>`).join('')}
                        </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>`;
                    break;
                }
                case 'checkbox': {
                    const opts = parseOptionList(p.options || '选项1|选项2');
                    const selected = parseOptionList(p.defaultValue || '');
                    const optionLayoutStyle = (p.optionLayout || 'vertical') === 'horizontal' ? 'flex-direction:row;flex-wrap:wrap;' : '';
                    const textVisualStyle = `${p.fontSize ? 'font-size:' + (parseInt(p.fontSize) || 14) + 'px;' : ''}${p.color ? 'color:' + p.color + ';' : ''}`;
                    const labelClass = (p.labelPosition || 'top') === 'left' ? 'label-left' : 'label-top';
                    const labelControlStyle = `gap:${parseInt(p.labelGap || 4)}px;`;
                    const labelTitleStyle = (p.labelPosition || 'top') === 'left' ? `min-width:${parseInt(p.labelWidth || 52)}px;max-width:${parseInt(p.labelWidth || 52)}px;` : '';
                    html = `<div class="form-widget auto-config-field" data-control-type="checkbox" data-config-name="${escapeHtml(p.configName || ('config_' + comp.id))}" style="${style}"${actionAttr}${posAttr}${customCssAttr}${customJsAttr}>
                        <div class="config-control ${labelClass}" style="${labelControlStyle}">
                        ${p.showLabel === false ? '' : `<div class="form-title" style="${textVisualStyle}${labelTitleStyle}">${escapeHtml(p.text || '多选配置')}</div>`}
                        <div class="form-options" style="${optionLayoutStyle}">
                            ${opts.map(opt => `<label class="form-option" style="${textVisualStyle}"><input type="checkbox" value="${escapeHtml(opt)}" ${selected.includes(opt) ? 'checked' : ''}> <span>${escapeHtml(opt)}</span></label>`).join('')}
                        </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>`;
                    break;
                }
                case 'select': {
                    const opts = parseOptionList(p.options || '选项1|选项2');
                    const current = p.defaultValue || opts[0] || '';
                    const labelClass = (p.labelPosition || 'top') === 'left' ? 'label-left' : 'label-top';
                    const textVisualStyle = `${p.fontSize ? 'font-size:' + (parseInt(p.fontSize) || 14) + 'px;' : ''}${p.color ? 'color:' + p.color + ';' : ''}`;
                    const labelControlStyle = `gap:${parseInt(p.labelGap || 4)}px;`;
                    const labelTitleStyle = (p.labelPosition || 'top') === 'left' ? `min-width:${parseInt(p.labelWidth || 52)}px;max-width:${parseInt(p.labelWidth || 52)}px;` : '';
                    html = `<div class="form-widget" style="${style}"${actionAttr}${posAttr}${customCssAttr}${customJsAttr}>
                        <div class="config-control ${labelClass}" style="${labelControlStyle}">
                        ${p.showLabel === false ? '' : `<div class="form-title" style="${textVisualStyle}${labelTitleStyle}">${escapeHtml(p.text || '下拉配置')}</div>`}
                        <div class="custom-select auto-config-field" data-control-type="select" data-config-name="${escapeHtml(p.configName || ('config_' + comp.id))}" data-value="${escapeHtml(current)}">
                            <div class="custom-select-display" style="${textVisualStyle}"><span>${escapeHtml(current)}</span><span>▾</span></div>
                            <div class="custom-select-menu">${opts.map(opt => `<div class="custom-select-option ${opt === current ? 'active' : ''}" data-value="${escapeHtml(opt)}">${escapeHtml(opt)}</div>`).join('')}</div>
                        </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>`;
                    break;
                }
                case 'switch': {
                    const opts = parseOptionList(p.options || '开|关');
                    const onText = opts[0] || '开';
                    const offText = opts[1] || '关';
                    const current = p.defaultValue || onText;
                    const isOn = current === onText;
                    const labelClass = (p.labelPosition || 'left') === 'left' ? 'label-left' : 'label-top';
                    const textVisualStyle = `${p.fontSize ? 'font-size:' + (parseInt(p.fontSize) || 14) + 'px;' : ''}${p.color ? 'color:' + p.color + ';' : ''}`;
                    const labelControlStyle = `gap:${parseInt(p.labelGap || 4)}px;`;
                    const labelTitleStyle = (p.labelPosition || 'left') === 'left' ? `min-width:${parseInt(p.labelWidth || 52)}px;max-width:${parseInt(p.labelWidth || 52)}px;` : '';
                    html = `<button type="button" class="switch-widget auto-config-field" data-control-type="switch" data-config-name="${escapeHtml(p.configName || ('config_' + comp.id))}" data-on="${escapeHtml(onText)}" data-off="${escapeHtml(offText)}" data-value="${escapeHtml(current)}" style="${style}"${actionAttr}${posAttr}${customCssAttr}${customJsAttr}>
                        <span class="config-control ${labelClass}" style="${labelControlStyle}">${p.showLabel === false ? '' : `<strong class="form-title" style="${textVisualStyle}${labelTitleStyle}">${escapeHtml(p.text || '转换开关')}</strong>`}<small style="${textVisualStyle}">${escapeHtml(current)}</small></span>
                        <span class="switch-track ${isOn ? 'on' : ''}"><span class="switch-thumb"></span></span>
                    </button>`;
                    break;
                }
                case 'autostart': {
                    html =
                        `<button class="btn btn-success" style="${style}" data-action="autoConfigFinish" data-action-params='${JSON.stringify({taskName: p.taskName || '第一个任务', loopMode: p.loopMode || '1'})}'${posAttr}${customCssAttr}${customJsAttr}>${escapeHtml(p.text||'🚀 启动任务')}</button>`;
                    break;
                }
                case 'taskstatus': {
                    html =
                        `<div class="status-widget" data-role="task-status" style="${style}"${posAttr}${customCssAttr}${customJsAttr}><strong>${escapeHtml(p.text||'任务状态')}</strong><br><span>${escapeHtml(p.defaultValue||'未运行')}</span></div>`;
                    break;
                }
                case 'message': {
                    html =
                        `<div class="msg-box msg-info" style="${style}"${actionAttr}${posAttr}${customCssAttr}${customJsAttr}>${escapeHtml(p.text||'消息')}</div>`;
                    break;
                }
                case 'effect': {
                    const anim = comp.subtype && ['fade', 'slide', 'bounce', 'rotate'].includes(comp.subtype) ? comp
                        .subtype : 'fade';
                    html =
                        `<div class="effect-demo anim-${anim}" style="${style}"${actionAttr}${posAttr}${customCssAttr}${customJsAttr}>${escapeHtml(p.text||'效果')}</div>`;
                    break;
                }
                case 'appbar': {
                    html =
                        `<div class="app-bar" style="${style}"${actionAttr}${posAttr}${customCssAttr}${customJsAttr}><span>${escapeHtml(p.text||'应用栏')}</span><div class="bar-actions"><span>菜单</span><span>搜索</span><span>更多</span></div></div>`;
                    break;
                }
                case 'image': {
                    html =
                        `<img class="img-display" src="${escapeHtml(p.src||'')}" alt="${escapeHtml(p.alt||'图片')}" style="max-width:100%;max-height:200px;border-radius:5px;object-fit:contain;"${actionAttr}${posAttr}${customCssAttr}${customJsAttr} />`;
                    break;
                }
                case 'fileinput': {
                    html =
                        `<button class="btn btn-success" style="${style}"${actionAttr}${posAttr}${customCssAttr}${customJsAttr}>${escapeHtml(p.text||'📂 导入数据')}</button>`;
                    break;
                }
                case 'filedownload': {
                    html =
                        `<button class="btn btn-warning" style="${style}"${actionAttr}${posAttr}${customCssAttr}${customJsAttr}>${escapeHtml(p.text||'💾 下载数据')}</button>`;
                    break;
                }
                case 'custom': {
                    var customStyle =
                        `padding:6px 14px;background:#f0f0f0;border-radius:5px;${p.color?'color:'+p.color+';':''}${p.bgColor?'background-color:'+p.bgColor+';':''}cursor:pointer;`;
                    html =
                        `<div style="${customStyle}"${actionAttr}${posAttr}${customCssAttr}${customJsAttr} data-type="custom">${escapeHtml(p.text||'🧩 自定义')}</div>`;
                    break;
                }
                default:
                    html =
                        `<div style="padding:4px 10px;background:#f1f3f4;border-radius:5px;${p.color?'color:'+p.color+';':''}${p.bgColor?'background-color:'+p.bgColor+';':''}"${actionAttr}${posAttr}>${escapeHtml(p.text||'组件')}</div>`;
            }
            return html;
        }

        // ================================================================
        //  generateHTML
        // ================================================================
        function generateHTML() {
    const page = getCurrentPage();
    if (!page) return '<html><body>无页面</body></html>';
    if (page.components.length === 0) {
        return `<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="zh-CN">
<head><meta charset="UTF-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1"><title>${page.name}</title>
<style>body{font-family:system-ui,sans-serif;padding:40px;background:#f0f2f5;text-align:center;color:#9aa0a6;}</style>
</head><body><div style="font-size:18px;">✨ 页面为空</div></body></html>`;
    }

    let styles = `
