.quiz-container .quiz-submit-btn:hover { background:#CC5500; transform:scale(1.02); }
${state.globalStyles}
${page.components.filter(c => c.customCss).map(c => `.runtime-component[data-id="${c.id}"] { ${c.customCss} }`).join('\n')}
`;

    const inputValues = {};
    page.components.forEach(c => {
        if (c.type === 'input') {
            const val = state.inputValues[c.id] !== undefined ? state.inputValues[c.id] : (c.props.defaultValue || '');
            inputValues[c.id] = val;
        }
        if (c.type === 'container' && c.children) {
            for (const child of c.children) {
                if (child.type === 'input') {
                    const val = state.inputValues[child.id] !== undefined ? state.inputValues[child.id] : (child.props.defaultValue || '');
                    inputValues[child.id] = val;
                }
            }
        }
    });

    const pagesData = state.pages.map(p => ({ id: p.id, name: p.name }));

    // ============================================================
    // 运行时脚本 - 完整版本
    // ============================================================
    let script = `
<script>
// ============================================================
// 运行时脚本顶部 - 工具函数和变量定义
// ============================================================

var _pages = ${JSON.stringify(pagesData)};
var _currentPageId = "${state.currentPageId}";
var _inputValues = ${JSON.stringify(inputValues)};
var _globalScripts = ${JSON.stringify(state.globalScripts || '')};
var _pageName = ${JSON.stringify(state.pageName || page.name || '页面')};
var _taskName = ${JSON.stringify(state.taskName || '测试')};

// HTML转义函数
function escapeHtml(str) {
    if (!str) return '';
    var d = document.createElement('div');
    d.textContent = str;
    return d.innerHTML;
}

// Toast消息
function showToast(msg, duration) {
    duration = duration || 2000;
    var container = document.getElementById('toast-container');
    if (!container) {
        container = document.createElement('div');
        container.id = 'toast-container';
        container.className = 'toast-container';
        document.body.appendChild(container);
    }
    var el = document.createElement('div');
    el.className = 'toast';
    el.textContent = msg;
    container.appendChild(el);
    requestAnimationFrame(function() { el.classList.add('show'); });
    setTimeout(function() { el.classList.remove('show'); setTimeout(function() { el.remove(); }, 400); }, duration);
}

function bindRuntimeEditableFocus(control) {
    if (!control || control.dataset.focusBound === '1') return;
    control.dataset.focusBound = '1';
    var focusNow = function(e) {
        e.stopPropagation();
        try {
            control.focus({ preventScroll: true });
        } catch (err) {
            control.focus();
        }
    };
    control.addEventListener('touchstart', focusNow, { passive: true });
    control.addEventListener('touchend', focusNow, { passive: true });
    control.addEventListener('mousedown', focusNow);
    control.addEventListener('click', focusNow);
}

function sanitizeHtmlFileName(name) {
    var safeName = String(name || '页面')
        .trim()
        .replace(/[\\\\/:*?"<>|]/g, '_')
        .replace(/\\s+/g, '_')
        .replace(/^\\.+/, '')
        .slice(0, 80) || '页面';
    return /\\.(html?|HTML?)$/.test(safeName) ? safeName : safeName + '.htm';
}

function sanitizePathSegment(name) {
    return String(name || '测试')
        .trim()
        .replace(/[\\\\/:*?"<>|]/g, '_')
        .replace(/\\s+/g, '_')
        .replace(/^\\.+/, '')
        .slice(0, 80) || '测试';
}

function getAutoEditorOutputDir(taskName) {
    return '/storage/emulated/0/auto-editor/local/' + sanitizePathSegment(taskName) + '/assets';
}

function browserDownloadHtml(html, fileName) {
    var blob = new Blob([html], { type: 'text/html;charset=utf-8' });
    var url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    var a = document.createElement('a');
    a.href = url;
    a.download = fileName;
    document.body.appendChild(a);
    a.click();
    document.body.removeChild(a);
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
    showToast('✅ 浏览器下载成功: ' + fileName);
}

async function writeHtmlToAutoEditor(html, rawName, taskName) {
    var fileName = sanitizeHtmlFileName(rawName);
    var outputDir = getAutoEditorOutputDir(taskName || _taskName);
    var fullPath = outputDir + '/' + fileName;
    var code = 'file.write(' + JSON.stringify(fullPath) + ',' + JSON.stringify(html) + ');';

    try {
        if (window.autoBridge && typeof window.autoBridge.eval === 'function') {
            await window.autoBridge.eval(code);
            showToast('✅ 已写入自动化编辑器: ' + fileName);
            return true;
        }
        if (window.file && typeof window.file.write === 'function') {
            window.file.write(fullPath, html);
            showToast('✅ 已写入自动化编辑器: ' + fileName);
            return true;
        }
    } catch (err) {
        console.warn('自动化编辑器写入失败，改用浏览器下载', err);
        showToast('⚠️ 自动化写入失败，改用浏览器下载');
    }

    browserDownloadHtml(html, fileName);
    return false;
}

function collectAutoConfig() {
    var configs = {};
    document.querySelectorAll('.auto-config-field').forEach(function(el) {
        var name = el.dataset.configName;
        if (!name) return;
        var type = el.dataset.controlType || el.type || el.tagName.toLowerCase();
        if (type === 'radio') {
            var checked = el.querySelector('input[type="radio"]:checked');
            configs[name] = checked ? checked.value : '';
        } else if (type === 'checkbox') {
            configs[name] = Array.from(el.querySelectorAll('input[type="checkbox"]:checked')).map(function(item) {
                return item.value;
            }).join('|');
        } else if (type === 'switch') {
            configs[name] = el.dataset.value || el.dataset.off || '关';
        } else if (type === 'select') {
            configs[name] = el.dataset.value || el.value || '';
        } else if (el.tagName.toLowerCase() === 'select') {
            configs[name] = el.value || '';
        } else {
            configs[name] = el.value || '';
        }
    });
    return configs;
}

function importRuntimeDataFromText(text) {
    try {
        var data = JSON.parse(text);
        var count = 0;
        Object.keys(data || {}).forEach(function(id) {
            var input = document.querySelector('.input-field[data-id="' + id + '"]');
            if (input) {
                input.value = data[id];
                _inputValues[id] = data[id];
                count++;
            }
        });
        showToast('✅ 已导入 ' + count + ' 个输入框数据');
    } catch (err) {
        showToast('❌ 数据格式错误: ' + err.message);
    }
}

function importRuntimeDataByPath(path) {
    var filePath = String(path || '').trim();
    if (!filePath) { showToast('请输入文件路径'); return; }
    try {
        if (window.file && typeof window.file.read === 'function') {
            var content = window.file.read(filePath);
            if (content === null || content === undefined || content === '') {
                showToast('❌ 读取失败：文件不存在、无权限或内容为空');
                return;
            }
            importRuntimeDataFromText(content);
            return;
        }
        showToast('当前环境不支持 file.read');
    } catch (err) {
        showToast('❌ 读取失败: ' + err.message);
    }
}

function showRuntimeImportPathDialog() {
    openDialog({
        title: '📂 导入数据',
        content: {
            type: 'input',
            inputType: 'text',
            placeholder: '/storage/emulated/0/Download/input_data.json',
            value: '/storage/emulated/0/Download/input_data.json'
        }
    }).then(function(result) {
        if (result && result.input !== undefined) {
            importRuntimeDataByPath(result.input);
        }
    });
}

function buildAutoConfigPayload(taskName, loopMode) {
    return {
        task_config: [{
            task_name: taskName || _taskName || '第一个任务',
            configs: collectAutoConfig()
        }],
        loop_mode: String(loopMode === undefined ? '1' : loopMode)
    };
}

function callAutoApi(name, args) {
    args = args || [];
    try {
        if (window.auto && typeof window.auto[name] === 'function') {
            return window.auto[name].apply(window.auto, args);
        }
        if (typeof window[name] === 'function') {
            return window[name].apply(window, args);
        }
        showToast('⚠️ 当前不在自动化编辑器自定义UI环境');
        return null;
    } catch (err) {
        console.error('自动化API调用失败:', name, err);
        showToast('❌ 自动化API调用失败: ' + err.message);
        return null;
    }
}

function updateTaskStatusText(text) {
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-role="task-status"]').forEach(function(el) {
        var span = el.querySelector('span');
        if (span) span.textContent = text;
    });
}

window.onTaskState = function(state) {
    var map = { 0: '未运行', 1: '运行中', 2: '已暂停' };
    updateTaskStatusText(map[state] || String(state));
};

// 弹窗
function openDialog(config) {
    return new Promise(function(resolve) {
        var overlay = document.createElement('div');
        overlay.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;inset:0;background:rgba(0,0,0,0.45);z-index:10000;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;backdrop-filter:blur(2px);padding:20px;';
        var box = document.createElement('div');
        box.style.cssText = 'background:#fff;border-radius:10px;max-width:460px;width:100%;max-height:80vh;overflow-y:auto;padding:20px 24px;box-shadow:0 20px 60px rgba(0,0,0,0.3);';
        var title = document.createElement('h2');
        title.textContent = config.title || '提示';
        title.style.cssText = 'font-size:17px;font-weight:600;margin-bottom:10px;';
        box.appendChild(title);
        var body = document.createElement('div');
        body.style.cssText = 'margin-bottom:14px;font-size:13px;color:#5f6368;line-height:1.6;';
        var content = config.content || '';
        if (typeof content === 'string') {
            body.innerHTML = content;
        } else if (Array.isArray(content)) {
            var type = config.type || 'radio';
            var name = 'dialog_option_' + Date.now();
            content.forEach(function(opt, idx) {
                var div = document.createElement('div');
                div.style.cssText = 'display:flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;padding:3px 0;cursor:pointer;';
                var input = document.createElement('input');
                input.type = type;
                input.name = name;
                input.value = opt.value || opt;
                input.id = 'opt_' + idx;
                var label = document.createElement('label');
                label.htmlFor = 'opt_' + idx;
                label.textContent = opt.label || opt;
                div.appendChild(input);
                div.appendChild(label);
                body.appendChild(div);
            });
        } else if (content.type === 'input') {
            var inp = document.createElement('input');
            inp.type = content.inputType || 'text';
            inp.placeholder = content.placeholder || '';
            inp.style.cssText = 'width:100%;padding:5px 10px;border:1px solid #dadce0;border-radius:5px;font-size:13px;margin-top:4px;font-family:inherit;';
            if (content.value) inp.value = content.value;
            body.appendChild(inp);
            body._input = inp;
        }
        box.appendChild(body);
        var actions = document.createElement('div');
        actions.style.cssText = 'display:flex;gap:6px;justify-content:flex-end;border-top:1px solid #e4e7ed;padding-top:12px;';
        var cancelBtn = document.createElement('button');
        cancelBtn.textContent = '取消';
        cancelBtn.style.cssText = 'padding:5px 18px;border:none;border-radius:5px;font-size:13px;font-weight:500;cursor:pointer;background:#f1f3f4;color:#202124;';
        var confirmBtn = document.createElement('button');
        confirmBtn.textContent = '确定';
        confirmBtn.style.cssText = 'padding:5px 18px;border:none;border-radius:5px;font-size:13px;font-weight:500;cursor:pointer;background:#FF6B00;color:#fff;';
        actions.appendChild(cancelBtn);
        actions.appendChild(confirmBtn);
        box.appendChild(actions);
        overlay.appendChild(box);
        document.body.appendChild(overlay);

        var resolveResult = function(result) {
            if (overlay.parentNode) overlay.parentNode.removeChild(overlay);
            resolve(result);
            if (result !== null) {
                if (config.onConfirm && typeof config.onConfirm === 'function') {
                    config.onConfirm(result);
                }
                if (config._nextAction && config._nextAction !== 'none') {
                    executeAction(config._nextAction, config._nextParams || {});
                }
            }
        };
        
        cancelBtn.addEventListener('click', function() { 
            resolveResult(null); 
        });
        confirmBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
            var result = {};
            var checked = body.querySelectorAll('input:checked');
            if (checked.length > 0) {
                result.values = Array.from(checked).map(function(el) { return el.value; });
                if (checked.length === 1) result.value = checked[0].value;
            }
            var inputEl = body._input || body.querySelector('.dialog-input');
            if (inputEl) result.input = inputEl.value;
            resolveResult(result);
        });
        var keyHandler = function(e) {
            if (e.key === 'Escape') { 
                resolveResult(null);
                document.removeEventListener('keydown', keyHandler); 
            }
            if (e.key === 'Enter') {
                var inp2 = body.querySelector('.dialog-input');
                if (inp2 && document.activeElement === inp2) {
                    var result = {};
                    var inputEl = body._input || body.querySelector('.dialog-input');
                    if (inputEl) result.input = inputEl.value;
                    resolveResult(result);
                    document.removeEventListener('keydown', keyHandler);
                }
            }
        };
        document.addEventListener('keydown', keyHandler);
    });
}

// ============================================================
// 动作执行器
// ============================================================
function executeAction(actionType, params) {
    params = params || {};
    console.log('运行时执行动作:', actionType, params);

    switch (actionType) {
        case 'dialog': {
            var title = params.title || '提示';
            var content = params.content || '这是弹窗内容';
            var options = params.options ? params.options.split(',').map(function(s) { return s.trim(); }).filter(function(s) { return s; }) : [];
            var dialogConfig = { 
                title: title, 
                content: content, 
                type: 'custom' 
            };
            if (options.length > 0) {
                dialogConfig.content = options.map(function(opt) { return { label: opt, value: opt }; });
                dialogConfig.type = options.length > 2 ? 'checkbox' : 'radio';
            }
            if (params.nextAction && params.nextAction !== 'none') {
                var nextType = params.nextAction;
                var nextParams = params.nextParams || {};
                dialogConfig.onConfirm = function(result) {
                    if (result === null) return;
                    if (result.value === '取消' || result.value === 'cancel' || result.value === 'Cancel') {
                        showToast('已取消');
                        return;
                    }
                    if (result.values && Array.isArray(result.values)) {
                        for (var i = 0; i < result.values.length; i++) {
                            if (result.values[i] === '取消' || result.values[i] === 'cancel' || result.values[i] === 'Cancel') {
                                showToast('已取消');
                                return;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    executeAction(nextType, nextParams);
                };
                dialogConfig._nextAction = nextType;
                dialogConfig._nextParams = nextParams;
            }
            openDialog(dialogConfig).then(function(result) {
                if (result) {
                    var msg = '📋 结果: ';
                    if (result.value) msg += result.value;
                    else if (result.values) msg += result.values.join(', ');
                    else if (result.input) msg += result.input;
                    else msg += JSON.stringify(result);
                    showToast(msg);
                } else {
                    showToast('已取消');
                }
            });
            break;
        }
        case 'save': {
            var msg = params.message || '✅ 已保存';
            var dur = (params.duration || 1) * 1000;
            var inputs = document.querySelectorAll('.input-field');
            inputs.forEach(function(inp) {
                var id = inp.dataset.id;
                if (id) {
                    _inputValues[id] = inp.value;
                }
            });
            try {
                localStorage.setItem('wochuangye_runtime_inputs', JSON.stringify(_inputValues));
                localStorage.setItem('wochuangye_runtime_auto_config', JSON.stringify(collectAutoConfig()));
            } catch(e) {}
            showToast('💾 ' + msg, dur);
            break;
        }
        case 'download': {
            showToast('📥 正在生成 HTML...');
            document.querySelectorAll('.input-field').forEach(function(inp) {
                var id = inp.dataset.id;
                if (id) {
                    _inputValues[id] = inp.value;
                }
            });
            var html = document.documentElement.outerHTML;
            var tempDoc = document.implementation.createHTMLDocument();
            tempDoc.documentElement.innerHTML = html;
            var found = false;
            var scripts = tempDoc.querySelectorAll('script');
            for (var i = 0; i < scripts.length; i++) {
                if (scripts[i].textContent.includes('_inputValues')) {
                    scripts[i].textContent = 'var _inputValues = ' + JSON.stringify(_inputValues) + ';' + scripts[i].textContent;
                    found = true;
                    break;
                }
            }
            if (!found) {
                var s = tempDoc.createElement('script');
                s.textContent = 'var _inputValues = ' + JSON.stringify(_inputValues) + ';';
                tempDoc.head.appendChild(s);
            }
            var finalHtml = '<!DOCTYPE html>\\n' + tempDoc.documentElement.outerHTML;
            writeHtmlToAutoEditor(finalHtml, _pageName, _taskName);
            break;
        }
        case 'message': {
            var msg2 = params.text || '📢 这是一条消息';
            var dur2 = (params.duration || 1.5) * 1000;
            showToast(msg2, dur2);
            break;
        }
        case 'switchPage': {
            var targetName = params.targetPage || '';
            if (!targetName) { showToast('请指定目标页面名称'); break; }
            var targetPage = _pages.find(function(p) { return p.name === targetName; });
            if (!targetPage) { showToast('未找到页面: ' + targetName); break; }
            showToast('📄 切换到: ' + targetPage.name + ' (运行时预览)');
            break;
        }
        case 'triggerButton': {
            var targetId = params.targetId || '';
            if (!targetId) { showToast('请指定目标按钮ID'); break; }
            var targetBtn = document.querySelector('.runtime-component[data-id="' + targetId + '"] .btn');
            if (targetBtn) {
                targetBtn.click();
                showToast('⚡ 已触发按钮');
            } else {
                showToast('未找到目标按钮');
            }
            break;
        }
        case 'navigate': {
            var url = params.url || '';
            if (!url) { showToast('请输入链接地址'); break; }
            var target = params.target || '_blank';
            window.open(url, target);
            showToast('🔗 跳转: ' + url);
            break;
        }
        case 'runPreview': {
            showToast('▶ 预览运行');
            break;
        }
        case 'importData': {
            showRuntimeImportPathDialog();
            break;
        }
        case 'exportData': {
            var data = {};
            document.querySelectorAll('.input-field').forEach(function(el) {
                var id = el.dataset.id;
                if (id) {
                    data[id] = el.value;
                }
            });
            if (Object.keys(data).length === 0) {
                showToast('⚠️ 没有输入框数据可导出');
                break;
            }
            var json = JSON.stringify(data, null, 2);
            var blob = new Blob([json], { type: 'application/json' });
            var url2 = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
            var a = document.createElement('a');
            a.href = url2;
            a.download = 'input_data_' + new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10) + '.json';
            document.body.appendChild(a);
            a.click();
            document.body.removeChild(a);
            URL.revokeObjectURL(url2);
            showToast('✅ 数据已导出 (' + Object.keys(data).length + ' 项)');
            break;
        }
        case 'toggleState': {
            var btn = document.activeElement;
            if (btn && btn.classList.contains('btn')) {
                var statesStr = btn.dataset.toggleStates;
                if (statesStr) {
                    try {
                        var states = JSON.parse(statesStr);
                        var current = parseInt(btn.dataset.toggleCurrent) || 0;
                        var next = (current + 1) % states.length;
                        btn.dataset.toggleCurrent = next;
                        btn.textContent = states[next];
                        var colors = ['#34a853', '#fbbc04', '#1a73e8', '#ea4335'];
                        btn.className = 'btn ' + ['btn-state-1','btn-state-2','btn-state-3','btn-state-4'][next % 4];
                        btn.style.backgroundColor = colors[next % 4];
                        showToast('🔄 状态: ' + states[next]);
                    } catch(e) {}
                }
            }
            break;
        }
        case 'hideShow': {
            var targetId = params.targetId || '';
            if (!targetId) { showToast('请指定目标组件ID'); break; }
            var targetEl = document.querySelector('.runtime-component[data-id="' + targetId + '"]');
            if (!targetEl) { showToast('未找到目标组件'); break; }
            var isHidden = targetEl.style.display === 'none';
            targetEl.style.display = isHidden ? '' : 'none';
            showToast(isHidden ? '👁️ 显示' : '🙈 隐藏');
            break;
        }
        case 'disableEnable': {
            var targetId = params.targetId || '';
            if (!targetId) { showToast('请指定目标组件ID'); break; }
            var targetEl = document.querySelector('.runtime-component[data-id="' + targetId + '"]');
            if (!targetEl) { showToast('未找到目标组件'); break; }
            var isDisabled = targetEl.dataset.disabled === 'true';
            var newState = isDisabled ? 'false' : 'true';
            targetEl.dataset.disabled = newState;
            var clickables = targetEl.querySelectorAll('.btn, .msg-box, .img-display, [data-type="custom"]');
            clickables.forEach(function(btn) {
                btn.style.pointerEvents = isDisabled ? 'auto' : 'none';
                btn.style.opacity = isDisabled ? '1' : '0.5';
            });
            showToast(isDisabled ? '✅ 允许点击' : '🚫 禁止点击');
            break;
        }
        case 'replaceComponent': {
            var targetId = params.targetId || '';
            if (!targetId) { showToast('请指定目标组件ID'); break; }
            var targetEl = document.querySelector('.runtime-component[data-id="' + targetId + '"]');
            if (!targetEl) { showToast('未找到目标组件'); break; }
            var replaceType = params.replaceType || 'button';
            var typeMap = {
                'button': '🔘 按钮',
                'input': '📝 输入框',
                'message': '💬 消息',
                'image': '🖼️ 图片'
            };
            showToast('🔄 运行时预览: 将替换为 ' + (typeMap[replaceType] || replaceType));
            break;
        }
        case 'autoConfigFinish': {
            var taskName = params.taskName || _taskName || '第一个任务';
            var loopMode = params.loopMode || '1';
            var payload = buildAutoConfigPayload(taskName, loopMode);
            callAutoApi('config_finish', [JSON.stringify(payload)]);
            showToast('🚀 已提交配置: ' + taskName);
            break;
        }
        case 'autoSaveTaskConfig': {
            var taskName2 = params.taskName || _taskName || '第一个任务';
            var configs = collectAutoConfig();
            callAutoApi('save_task_config', [taskName2, JSON.stringify(configs)]);
            showToast('💾 已保存任务配置: ' + taskName2);
            break;
        }
        case 'autoStartTask': {
            var taskName3 = params.taskName || _taskName || '第一个任务';
            var loop = String(params.loopMode || '1') === '1';
            callAutoApi('start_task', [JSON.stringify([{ name: taskName3 }]), JSON.stringify([]), loop]);
            updateTaskStatusText('运行中');
            showToast('▶ 已请求启动任务: ' + taskName3);
            break;
        }
        case 'autoStopTask': {
            callAutoApi('stop_task', []);
            updateTaskStatusText('未运行');
            showToast('⏹ 已请求停止任务');
            break;
        }
        // ============================================================
        // 【关键修复】交卷 - 完整实现
        // ============================================================
        case 'quiz_submit': {
    var quizEl = document.querySelector('.quiz-container');
    if (!quizEl) {
        showToast('❌ 未找到答题组件');
        break;
    }
    
    // 如果已经提交，只显示结果（查看答案）
    if (quizEl.dataset.submitted === 'true') {
        var data = quizEl.dataset.resultData;
        if (data) {
            try {
                var parsed = JSON.parse(data);
                var content = parsed.details || '无答案';
                openDialog({
                    title: '📝 交卷结果',
                    content: '<div style="font-size:16px;font-weight:600;text-align:center;padding:8px 0;">🎯 得分: ' + parsed.totalCorrect + ' / ' + parsed.totalQ + '</div><div style="max-height:300px;overflow:auto;border-top:1px solid #e0e0e0;padding-top:8px;">' + content + '</div>',
                    type: 'custom'
                });
            } catch(e) {}
        }
        break;
    }
    
    var qBlocks = quizEl.querySelectorAll('.quiz-question');
    if (qBlocks.length === 0) {
        showToast('❌ 没有题目');
        break;
    }
    
    var hasAnyAnswer = false;
    var totalCorrect = 0;
    var details = '';
    
    qBlocks.forEach(function(qBlock, idx) {
        var qTextEl = qBlock.querySelector('.q-text');
        var text = '题目';
        if (qTextEl) {
            text = qTextEl.textContent || '题目';
            text = text.replace(/^\d+\.\s*/, '').trim();
        }
        
        var checkedInputs = qBlock.querySelectorAll('.q-opt input:checked');
        var userAns = [];
        checkedInputs.forEach(function(cb) {
            var val = parseInt(cb.value);
            if (!isNaN(val)) {
                userAns.push(val);
            }
        });
        userAns.sort(function(a, b) { return a - b; });
        
        if (userAns.length > 0) hasAnyAnswer = true;
        
        var opts = qBlock.querySelectorAll('.q-opt');
        var corrects = [];
        opts.forEach(function(opt, oi) {
            var isCorrect = opt.getAttribute('data-correct') === 'true';
            if (isCorrect) {
                corrects.push(oi);
            }
        });
        corrects.sort(function(a, b) { return a - b; });
        
        var isCorrect = false;
        if (userAns.length === corrects.length && userAns.length > 0) {
            isCorrect = true;
            for (var i = 0; i < userAns.length; i++) {
                if (userAns[i] !== corrects[i]) {
                    isCorrect = false;
                    break;
                }
            }
        } else if (userAns.length === 0 && corrects.length === 0) {
            isCorrect = true;
        }
        
        if (isCorrect) totalCorrect++;
        
        var correctText = corrects.length > 0 ? 
            corrects.map(function(i) { return String.fromCharCode(65 + i); }).join(', ') : 
            '无';
        var userText = userAns.length > 0 ? 
            userAns.map(function(i) { return String.fromCharCode(65 + i); }).join(', ') : 
            '未作答';
        
        var statusIcon = '';
        var statusColor = '';
        if (userAns.length === 0) {
            statusIcon = '⚠️';
            statusColor = '#fbbc04';
        } else if (isCorrect) {
            statusIcon = '✅';
            statusColor = '#34a853';
        } else {
            statusIcon = '❌';
            statusColor = '#ea4335';
        }
        
        details += '<div style="padding:4px 0;border-bottom:1px solid #f0f0f0;font-size:13px;">';
        details += '<strong>' + (idx+1) + '.</strong> ' + escapeHtml(text);
        details += '<br><span style="color:' + statusColor + ';">';
        details += statusIcon + ' 你的答案: ' + userText;
        details += '</span>';
        details += '<br><span style="color:#1a73e8;">✅ 正确答案: ' + correctText + '</span>';
        if (userAns.length > 0) {
            details += '<br><span style="font-size:12px;color:' + (isCorrect ? '#34a853' : '#ea4335') + ';">';
            details += isCorrect ? '✓ 回答正确' : '✗ 回答错误';
            details += '</span>';
        }
        details += '</div>';
    });
    
    if (!hasAnyAnswer) {
        showToast('⚠️ 请先作答再交卷');
        break;
    }
    
    var totalQ = qBlocks.length;
    var score = Math.round((totalCorrect / totalQ) * 100);
    
    quizEl.dataset.submitted = 'true';
    
    var titleEl = quizEl.querySelector('.quiz-title');
    var title = titleEl ? titleEl.textContent : '选择题';
    
    var resultContent = '';
    resultContent += '<div style="font-size:16px;font-weight:600;text-align:center;padding:8px 0;">';
    resultContent += '🎯 得分: ' + totalCorrect + ' / ' + totalQ + ' (' + score + '分)';
    resultContent += '</div>';
    resultContent += '<div style="max-height:300px;overflow:auto;border-top:1px solid #e0e0e0;padding-top:8px;">';
    resultContent += details;
    resultContent += '</div>';
    resultContent += '<div style="text-align:center;margin-top:12px;font-size:12px;color:#5f6368;">' + escapeHtml(title) + '</div>';
    resultContent += '<div style="text-align:center;margin-top:8px;">';
    resultContent += '<button id="closeResultBtn" style="padding:4px 16px;border:none;border-radius:4px;background:#f1f3f4;cursor:pointer;font-size:13px;">关闭</button>';
    resultContent += '</div>';
    
    // 显示结果弹窗
    openDialog({
        title: '📝 交卷结果',
        content: resultContent,
        type: 'custom'
    }).then(function() {
        // 在界面上标记正确答案（绿色✅）和错误选中（红色✖️）
        quizEl.querySelectorAll('.q-opt').forEach(function(opt) {
            var mark = opt.querySelector('.q-mark');
            if (!mark) {
                mark = document.createElement('span');
                mark.className = 'q-mark';
                opt.appendChild(mark);
            }
            mark.style.display = 'inline';
            var isCorrect = opt.getAttribute('data-correct') === 'true';
            var cb = opt.querySelector('input');
            var isSelected = cb && cb.checked;
            
            if (isCorrect) {
                // 正确答案：始终显示绿色对勾
                mark.textContent = '✅';
                mark.style.color = '#34a853';
                opt.style.background = '#e6f4ea';
            } else if (!isCorrect && isSelected) {
                // 错误选中的选项：显示红色叉号
                mark.textContent = '✖️';
                mark.style.color = '#ea4335';
                opt.style.background = '#fce8e6';
            } else {
                mark.style.display = 'none';
                opt.style.background = '';
            }
            // 禁用所有输入
            opt.querySelectorAll('input').forEach(function(inp) { inp.disabled = true; });
        });
        
        // 修改按钮：隐藏提交按钮，显示“重置”和“查看答案”
        var submitBtn = quizEl.querySelector('.quiz-submit-btn');
        if (submitBtn) {
            submitBtn.style.display = 'none';
            var btnContainer = document.createElement('div');
            btnContainer.style.cssText = 'display:flex;gap:8px;align-self:center;position:sticky;bottom:0;';
            
            var resetBtn = document.createElement('button');
            resetBtn.textContent = '🔄 重置';
            resetBtn.className = 'quiz-submit-btn';
            resetBtn.style.cssText = 'padding:6px 20px;background:#1a73e8;color:#fff;border:none;border-radius:5px;font-size:14px;font-weight:600;cursor:pointer;';
            
            var viewBtn = document.createElement('button');
            viewBtn.textContent = '👁️ 查看答案';
            viewBtn.className = 'quiz-submit-btn';
            viewBtn.style.cssText = 'padding:6px 20px;background:#34a853;color:#fff;border:none;border-radius:5px;font-size:14px;font-weight:600;cursor:pointer;';
            
            btnContainer.appendChild(resetBtn);
            btnContainer.appendChild(viewBtn);
            submitBtn.parentNode.insertBefore(btnContainer, submitBtn);
            
            // 存储结果数据供查看答案使用
            var resultData = { totalCorrect: totalCorrect, totalQ: totalQ, details: details, title: title };
            quizEl.dataset.resultData = JSON.stringify(resultData);
            
            // 重置功能
            resetBtn.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
                e.stopPropagation();
                // 重置所有
                quizEl.dataset.submitted = 'false';
                quizEl.querySelectorAll('.q-opt input').forEach(function(inp) {
                    inp.checked = false;
                    inp.disabled = false;
                });
                quizEl.querySelectorAll('.q-opt').forEach(function(opt) {
                    opt.style.background = '';
                    var mark = opt.querySelector('.q-mark');
                    if (mark) {
                        mark.style.display = 'none';
                        mark.textContent = '';
                    }
                });
                // 移除结果按钮，恢复提交按钮
                btnContainer.remove();
                submitBtn.style.display = '';
                submitBtn.disabled = false;
                delete quizEl.dataset.resultData;
                showToast('🔄 已重置，可以重新答题');
            });
            
            // 查看答案功能
            viewBtn.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
                e.stopPropagation();
                var data = quizEl.dataset.resultData;
                if (data) {
                    try {
                        var parsed = JSON.parse(data);
                        var content = parsed.details || '无答案';
                        openDialog({
                            title: '📝 交卷结果',
                            content: '<div style="font-size:16px;font-weight:600;text-align:center;padding:8px 0;">🎯 得分: ' + parsed.totalCorrect + ' / ' + parsed.totalQ + '</div><div style="max-height:300px;overflow:auto;border-top:1px solid #e0e0e0;padding-top:8px;">' + content + '</div>',
                            type: 'custom'
                        });
                    } catch(e) {}
                } else {
                    showToast('没有结果数据');
                }
            });
        }
        
        // 关闭弹窗时的额外处理（已包含在 openDialog 中）
        var closeBtn = document.getElementById('closeResultBtn');
        if (closeBtn) {
            closeBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
                var overlay = document.getElementById('dialog-overlay');
                if (overlay) overlay.classList.remove('active');
            });
        }
        
        showToast('📝 交卷完成！得分: ' + totalCorrect + '/' + totalQ);
    });
    
    break;
}
        default:
            showToast('未知动作: ' + actionType);
    }
}

function executeCustomJs(compId, event) {
    var el = document.querySelector('.runtime-component[data-id="' + compId + '"]');
    if (!el) return;
    var js = el.dataset.customJs;
    if (js && js.trim()) {
        try {
            var fn = new Function('comp', 'event', 'showToast', 'openDialog', 'executeAction', js);
            fn({ id: compId, element: el }, event, showToast, openDialog, executeAction);
        } catch(e) {
            showToast('❌ 自定义JS错误: ' + e.message);
            console.error('自定义JS错误:', e);
        }
    }
}

if (_globalScripts && _globalScripts.trim()) {
    try {
        new Function(_globalScripts)();
    } catch(e) {
        console.warn('全局脚本执行错误:', e);
    }
}

document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
    try {

        var tabs = pager.querySelectorAll('.pager-tab');
        var pages = pager.querySelectorAll('.pager-page');
        var activeColor = pager.querySelector('.pager-tab.active');
        var tabColor = activeColor ? activeColor.style.color : '#FF6B00';
        var tabBg = pager.querySelector('.pager-tab:not(.active)');
        var pageBg = tabBg ? pager.style.background : '#fff';
        tabs.forEach(function(tab) {
            tab.addEventListener('click', function() {
                var idx = tab.dataset.pagerIdx;
                tabs.forEach(function(t) {
                    t.classList.remove('active');
                    t.style.color = tabColor;
                    t.style.borderBottom = '2.5px solid transparent';
                    t.style.background = 'transparent';
                });
                tab.classList.add('active');
                tab.style.color = (pager.querySelector('.pager-tab.active') || tab).style.color;
                tab.style.borderBottom = '2.5px solid ' + tabColor;
                tab.style.background = pageBg;
                pages.forEach(function(pg) {
                    pg.style.display = pg.dataset.pagerPage === idx ? 'block' : 'none';
                });
            });
        });
    });

    document.querySelectorAll('.custom-select[data-control-type="select"]').forEach(function(sel) {

                e.stopPropagation();

                val++;
                display.textContent = val;
                var customJs = container.dataset.customJs;

    document.querySelectorAll('.btn-state-1, .btn-state-2, .btn-state-3, .btn-state-4').forEach(function(btn) {
        var toggleStates = btn.dataset.toggleStates;

            try {
                var states = JSON.parse(toggleStates);
                var currentIdx = parseInt(btn.dataset.toggleCurrent) || parseInt(btn.dataset.toggle) || 0;
                btn.dataset.toggleCurrent = currentIdx;
                if (currentIdx < states.length) {
                    btn.textContent = states[currentIdx];
                }
                btn.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
                    e.stopPropagation();
                    var actionType = this.dataset.action;
                    if (actionType === 'toggleState') {
                        executeAction('toggleState', {});
                    } else {
                        var states2 = JSON.parse(this.dataset.toggleStates);
                        var current2 = parseInt(this.dataset.toggleCurrent) || 0;
                        var next2 = (current2 + 1) % states2.length;
                        this.dataset.toggleCurrent = next2;
                        this.textContent = states2[next2];
                        var colors2 = ['#34a853', '#fbbc04', '#1a73e8', '#ea4335'];
                        this.className = 'btn ' + ['btn-state-1','btn-state-2','btn-state-3','btn-state-4'][next2 % 4];
                        this.style.backgroundColor = colors2[next2 % 4];
                        showToast('🔄 状态: ' + states2[next2]);
                    }
                });

    document.querySelectorAll('.img-display').forEach(function(img) {

            opt.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
                if (e.target.closest('input')) return;
                if (quizEl.dataset.submitted === 'true') return;
                var cb = this.querySelector('input');
                if (cb) {
                    var question = this.closest('.quiz-question');
                    var type = question?.querySelector('.q-type')?.textContent === '多选题' ? 'multi' : 'single';
                    if (type === 'single') {
                        var siblings = question.querySelectorAll('.q-opt input');
                        siblings.forEach(function(s) { if (s !== cb) s.checked = false; });
                        cb.checked = true;
                    } else {
                        cb.checked = !cb.checked;
                    }
                    cb.dispatchEvent(new Event('change'));
                }
            });
        });
        
        // 交卷按钮 - 触发交卷动作
        var submitBtn = quizEl.querySelector('.quiz-submit-btn');
        if (submitBtn) {
            submitBtn.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
                e.stopPropagation();
