                    showToast('📝 已交卷');
                    return;
                }
                // 执行交卷动作
                executeAction('quiz_submit', {});
            });
        }
    });

    console.log('✅ 运行时已初始化 (v4 - 容器/计数器/答题)');
    console.log('可用函数: showToast, openDialog, executeAction, executeCustomJs');
});
<\/script>`;

    // 页面背景
    const pageBgStyle = [];
    if (page.bgImage) {
        pageBgStyle.push('background-image:url(' + page.bgImage + ')');
        pageBgStyle.push('background-size:' + (page.bgFit || 'cover'));
        pageBgStyle.push('background-position:center');
        pageBgStyle.push('background-repeat:no-repeat');
        if (page.bgBlur) {
            pageBgStyle.push('position:relative');
        }
    }
    const pageBgInline = pageBgStyle.length ? pageBgStyle.join(';') : '';

    let bodyHTML = '';
    if (page.bgImage && page.bgBlur) {
        bodyHTML += '<div style="position:relative;">';
        bodyHTML += '<div style="position:absolute;inset:0;overflow:hidden;z-index:0;">';
        bodyHTML += '<img src="' + page.bgImage + '" style="width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:' + (page.bgFit||'cover') + ';filter:blur(' + page.bgBlur + 'px);transform:scale(' + (1 + (page.bgBlur||0) * 0.03) + ');" onerror="this.parentElement.style.display=\'none\'" />';
        bodyHTML += '</div>';
    }

    bodyHTML += '<div class="page" style="min-height:600px;position:relative;' + pageBgInline + (page.bgBlur ? '' : '') + '">';
    const topLevel = page.components.filter(c => !c.parentId);
    for (const comp of topLevel) {
        const htmlContent = getComponentHTML(comp);
        bodyHTML += wrapRuntimeComponent(comp, htmlContent);
    }
    bodyHTML += '\n</div>';

    // 有虚化时关闭外层容器
    if (page.bgImage && page.bgBlur) {
        bodyHTML += '\n</div>';
    }

    return `<!DOCTYPE html>
        <html lang="zh-CN">
        <head><meta charset="UTF-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1"><title>${page.name}</title>
        <script src="http://www.autoeditor.cn/autoeditor/auto.ui.1.1.js"><\/script>
        <style>${styles}</style>
        ${script}
        </head><body>${bodyHTML}</body></html>`;
}

        // ================================================================
        //  代码面板
        // ================================================================
        function updateCodePanel() {
            const page = getCurrentPage();
            if (!page) {
                codeArea.style.display = 'none';
                codeEmpty.style.display = 'flex';
                codePanelTitle.textContent = '📄 无页面';
                return;
            }

            if (state.showGlobalCode) {
                codeArea.style.display = 'block';
                codeEmpty.style.display = 'none';
                const globalCode =
                    `/* ===== 全局样式 ===== */\n${state.globalStyles || DEFAULT_GLOBAL_STYLES}\n\n/* ===== 全局脚本 ===== */\n${state.globalScripts || DEFAULT_GLOBAL_SCRIPTS}`;
                codeArea.value = globalCode;
                codePanelTitle.textContent = '🌐 全局代码 (CSS + JS)';
                codeGlobalBtn.textContent = '📄 组件代码';
                codeGlobalBtn.classList.add('active');
                return;
            }

            codeGlobalBtn.textContent = '🌐 全局代码';
            codeGlobalBtn.classList.remove('active');

            let comp = page.components.find(c => c.id === state.selectedId);
            if (!comp) {
                for (const c of page.components) {
                    if (c.type === 'container' && c.children) {
                        const child = c.children.find(ch => ch.id === state.selectedId);
                        if (child) { comp = child; break; }
                    }
                    if (c.type === 'pager' && c.props.pages) {
                        let found = false;
                        for (const pg of c.props.pages) {
                            if (pg.children) {
                                const child = pg.children.find(ch => ch.id === state.selectedId);
                                if (child) { comp = child; found = true; break; }
                            }
                        }
                        if (found) break;
                    }
                }
            }
            if (comp) {
                codeArea.style.display = 'block';
                codeEmpty.style.display = 'none';
                codeArea.value = getComponentHTML(comp);
                codePanelTitle.textContent = `📄 组件代码: ${getTypeLabel(comp.type, comp.subtype)} (ID:${comp.id})`;
            } else {
                codeArea.style.display = 'none';
                codeEmpty.style.display = 'flex';
                codePanelTitle.textContent = '📄 点击组件查看代码';
            }
        }

        // ================================================================
        //  应用代码
        // ================================================================
        function applyCodeFromPanel() {
            const page = getCurrentPage();
            if (!page) { showToast('没有页面'); return; }

            if (state.showGlobalCode) {
                const code = codeArea.value;
                const stylesMatch = code.match(/\/\* ===== 全局样式 ===== \*\/\s*([\s\S]*?)(?=\/\* ===== 全局脚本 ===== \*\/|$)/);
                const scriptsMatch = code.match(/\/\* ===== 全局脚本 ===== \*\/\s*([\s\S]*?)$/);
                if (stylesMatch) {
                    const newStyles = stylesMatch[1].trim();
                    state.globalStyles = newStyles === DEFAULT_GLOBAL_STYLES || !newStyles ? '' : newStyles;
                } else {
                    state.globalStyles = '';
                }
                if (scriptsMatch) {
                    const newScripts = scriptsMatch[1].trim();
                    state.globalScripts = newScripts === DEFAULT_GLOBAL_SCRIPTS || !newScripts ? '' : newScripts;
                } else {
                    state.globalScripts = '';
                }
                if (state.globalStyles === DEFAULT_GLOBAL_STYLES) state.globalStyles = '';
                if (state.globalScripts === DEFAULT_GLOBAL_SCRIPTS) state.globalScripts = '';
                autoSave();
                showToast('✅ 全局代码已应用');
                return;
            }

            let comp = page.components.find(c => c.id === state.selectedId);
            if (!comp) {
                for (const c of page.components) {
                    if (c.type === 'container' && c.children) {
                        const child = c.children.find(ch => ch.id === state.selectedId);
                        if (child) { comp = child; break; }
                    }
                    if (c.type === 'pager' && c.props.pages) {
                        let found = false;
                        for (const pg of c.props.pages) {
                            if (pg.children) {
                                const child = pg.children.find(ch => ch.id === state.selectedId);
                                if (child) { comp = child; found = true; break; }
                            }
                        }
                        if (found) break;
                    }
                }
            }
            if (!comp) { showToast('请先选中一个组件'); return; }

            const code = codeArea.value;
            if (!code || code.trim() === '') {
                showToast('⚠️ 代码为空');
                return;
            }

            if (comp.type === 'container') {
                const wrapper = document.createElement('div');
                wrapper.innerHTML = code;
                const containerEl = wrapper.querySelector('.container-wrapper');
                if (containerEl) {
                    const titleEl = containerEl.querySelector('.container-title');
                    if (titleEl) comp.props.text = titleEl.textContent.trim() || '容器';
                    const bgColor = containerEl.style.backgroundColor;
                    if (bgColor) comp.props.bgColor = bgColor;
                }
                refreshComponent(comp);
                selectComponent(comp.id);
                updateCodePanel();
                autoSave();
                showToast('✅ 容器已更新');
                return;
            }

            if (comp.type === 'counter') {
                const wrapper = document.createElement('div');
                wrapper.innerHTML = code;
                const display = wrapper.querySelector('.counter-display');
                if (display) {
                    const val = parseInt(display.textContent) || 0;
                    comp.props.value = val;
                }
                refreshComponent(comp);
                selectComponent(comp.id);
                updateCodePanel();
                autoSave();
                showToast('✅ 计数器已更新');
                return;
            }

            if (comp.type === 'quiz') {
                // 答题组件暂不支持代码编辑，使用编辑器
                showToast('📝 请使用答题编辑器修改');
                return;
            }

            const div = document.createElement('div');
            div.innerHTML = code;
            const children = div.children;
            if (children.length === 0) {
                showToast('⚠️ 代码无效，请包含有效的 HTML 元素');
                return;
            }
            const el = children[0];
            const tag = el.tagName.toLowerCase();
            const classes = el.className || '';
            const style = el.style;
            const text = el.textContent || '';

            const actionType = el.dataset.action || 'none';
            let actionParams = {};
            try { actionParams = JSON.parse(el.dataset.actionParams || '{}'); } catch (e) {}

            const dataType = el.dataset.type || comp.type;
            const dataSubtype = el.dataset.subtype || comp.subtype;
            const dataToggle = parseInt(el.dataset.toggleCurrent) || parseInt(el.dataset.toggle) || 0;

            comp.customCss = el.dataset.customCss || '';
            comp.customJs = el.dataset.customJs || '';

            if (comp.type === 'button') {
                comp.props.text = text.trim() || '按钮';
                if (dataSubtype && dataSubtype !== 'undefined') {
                    comp.subtype = dataSubtype;
                } else {
                    if (classes.includes('btn-success')) comp.subtype = 'success';
                    else if (classes.includes('btn-danger')) comp.subtype = 'danger';
                    else if (classes.includes('btn-warning')) comp.subtype = 'warning';
                    else if (classes.includes('btn-outline')) comp.subtype = 'outline';
                    else if (classes.includes('btn-state-1') || classes.includes('btn-state-2') ||
                        classes.includes('btn-state-3') || classes.includes('btn-state-4')) {
                        comp.subtype = 'toggle';
                        comp.toggleState = dataToggle;
                        const statesData = el.dataset.toggleStates;
                        if (statesData) {
                            try {
                                const states = JSON.parse(statesData);
                                comp.props.toggleStates = states;
                                if (dataToggle < states.length) comp.props.text = states[dataToggle];
                            } catch (e) {}
                        }
                    } else comp.subtype = 'default';
                }
                if (style.backgroundColor) comp.props.bgColor = style.backgroundColor;
                if (style.color) comp.props.color = style.color;
                if (style.fontSize) comp.props.fontSize = parseInt(style.fontSize) || 14;
                if (actionType !== 'none') comp.action = { type: actionType, params: actionParams };
                if (comp.subtype === 'toggle') {
                    const states = comp.props.toggleStates || ['编辑', '完成', '已保存', '发布'];
                    if (dataToggle < states.length) {
                        comp.toggleState = dataToggle;
                        comp.props.text = states[dataToggle];
                    }
                }
            } else if (comp.type === 'input') {
                comp.props.text = el.value || '';
                comp.props.placeholder = el.placeholder || '';
                if (style.color) comp.props.color = style.color;
                if (style.backgroundColor) comp.props.bgColor = style.backgroundColor;
                if (style.fontSize) comp.props.fontSize = parseInt(style.fontSize) || 14;
                state.inputValues[comp.id] = el.value || '';
            } else if (comp.type === 'message') {
                comp.props.text = text.trim() || '消息';
                if (style.color) comp.props.color = style.color;
                if (style.backgroundColor) comp.props.bgColor = style.backgroundColor;
                if (style.fontSize) comp.props.fontSize = parseInt(style.fontSize) || 14;
                if (actionType !== 'none') comp.action = { type: actionType, params: actionParams };
            } else if (comp.type === 'effect') {
                comp.props.text = text.trim() || '效果';
                if (classes.includes('anim-slide')) comp.subtype = 'slide';
                else if (classes.includes('anim-bounce')) comp.subtype = 'bounce';
                else if (classes.includes('anim-rotate')) comp.subtype = 'rotate';
                else comp.subtype = 'fade';
                if (style.color) comp.props.color = style.color;
                if (style.backgroundColor) comp.props.bgColor = style.backgroundColor;
                if (style.fontSize) comp.props.fontSize = parseInt(style.fontSize) || 14;
                if (actionType !== 'none') comp.action = { type: actionType, params: actionParams };
            } else if (comp.type === 'appbar') {
                comp.props.text = text.trim() || '应用栏';
                if (style.color) comp.props.color = style.color;
                if (style.backgroundColor) comp.props.bgColor = style.backgroundColor;
                if (style.fontSize) comp.props.fontSize = parseInt(style.fontSize) || 14;
                if (actionType !== 'none') comp.action = { type: actionType, params: actionParams };
            } else if (comp.type === 'image') {
                comp.props.src = el.src || '';
                comp.props.alt = el.alt || '图片';
                if (style.color) comp.props.color = style.color;
                if (style.backgroundColor) comp.props.bgColor = style.backgroundColor;
                if (actionType !== 'none') comp.action = { type: actionType, params: actionParams };
            } else if (comp.type === 'fileinput' || comp.type === 'filedownload') {
                comp.props.text = text.trim() || (comp.type === 'fileinput' ? '📂 导入数据' : '💾 下载数据');
                if (style.color) comp.props.color = style.color;
                if (style.backgroundColor) comp.props.bgColor = style.backgroundColor;
                if (style.fontSize) comp.props.fontSize = parseInt(style.fontSize) || 14;
                if (actionType !== 'none') comp.action = { type: actionType, params: actionParams };
            } else if (comp.type === 'custom') {
                comp.props.text = text.trim() || '🧩 自定义';
                if (style.color) comp.props.color = style.color;
                if (style.backgroundColor) comp.props.bgColor = style.backgroundColor;
                if (style.fontSize) comp.props.fontSize = parseInt(style.fontSize) || 14;
                if (actionType !== 'none') comp.action = { type: actionType, params: actionParams };
            }

            if (el.dataset.x !== undefined) comp.x = parseFloat(el.dataset.x) || comp.x;
            if (el.dataset.y !== undefined) comp.y = parseFloat(el.dataset.y) || comp.y;
            if (el.dataset.width !== undefined) comp.width = el.dataset.width;
            if (el.dataset.height !== undefined) comp.height = el.dataset.height;

            refreshComponent(comp);
            selectComponent(comp.id);
            updateCodePanel();
            autoSave();
            ensureCanvasSize();
            showToast('✅ 代码已应用，组件已更新');
        }

        // ================================================================
        //  预览 & 下载
        // ================================================================
        function runPreview() {
            const allPages = state.pages;
            if (allPages.length <= 1) {
                const html = generateHTML();
                const overlay = document.getElementById('previewOverlay');
                const frame = document.getElementById('previewFrame');
                if (!overlay || !frame) { showToast('预览容器不存在'); return; }
                frame.srcdoc = html;
                overlay.classList.add('active');
                return;
            }
            const currentPg = getCurrentPage();
            let allHtml = '<style>';
            allHtml += '*{margin:0;padding:0;box-sizing:border-box;}';
            allHtml += 'body{font-family:"PingFang SC","Hiragino Sans GB","Microsoft YaHei",sans-serif;background:#f5f5f5;padding:12px;}';
            allHtml += '.ppt{padding:8px 16px;border-radius:6px 6px 0 0;cursor:pointer;font-size:14px;font-weight:500;user-select:none;transition:all .2s;background:#f1f3f4;color:#5f6368;border:1px solid #dadce0;border-bottom:1px solid #dadce0;margin-bottom:-1px;white-space:nowrap;}';
            allHtml += '.ppt.act{background:#FF6B00;color:#fff;border-color:#FF6B00;border-bottom:2px solid #FF6B00;}';
            allHtml += '.pp{display:none;background:#fff;border-radius:0 10px 10px 10px;padding:24px 28px;box-shadow:0 4px 20px rgba(0,0,0,.06);min-height:500px;position:relative;}';
            allHtml += '.pp.act{display:block;}';
            allHtml += '</style>';
            allHtml += '<div style="display:flex;gap:2px;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:flex-end;margin-bottom:0;">';
            allPages.forEach(pg => {
                const isA = pg.id === currentPg.id;
                allHtml += '<div class="ppt' + (isA?' act':'') + '" data-pid="' + pg.id + '">' + escapeHtml(pg.name) + '</div>';
            });
            allHtml += '</div>';
            allPages.forEach(pg => {
                allHtml += '<div class="pp" data-pid="' + pg.id + '">';
                const tmp = state.currentPageId;
                state.currentPageId = pg.id;
                const h = generateHTML();
                state.currentPageId = tmp;
                const m = h.match(/<body[^>]*>([\s\S]*?)<\/body>/i);
                if (m) {
                    // 过滤掉 body 中的运行时脚本，避免重复执行
                    allHtml += m[1].replace(/<script>[\s\S]*?<\/script>/gi, '');
                }
                allHtml += '</div>';
            });
            // 统一添加一次运行时脚本（用当前页面的）
            const tmp = state.currentPageId;
            state.currentPageId = currentPg.id;
            const currentHtml = generateHTML();
            state.currentPageId = tmp;
            const scriptMatch = currentHtml.match(/<body[\s\S]*?<script>([\s\S]*?)<\/script>\s*<\/body>/i);
            if (scriptMatch) {
                allHtml += '<script>' + scriptMatch[1] + '<\/script>';
            }
            // 页面切换脚本
            allHtml += '<script>document.querySelectorAll(".ppt").forEach(function(t){t.addEventListener("click",function(){var pid=this.dataset.pid;document.querySelectorAll(".ppt").forEach(function(x){x.classList.remove("act");});this.classList.add("act");document.querySelectorAll(".pp").forEach(function(x){x.classList.remove("act");});document.querySelector(\'.pp[data-pid="\'+pid+\'"]\').classList.add("act");});});<\/script>';
            const overlay = document.getElementById('previewOverlay');
            const frame = document.getElementById('previewFrame');
            if (!overlay || !frame) { showToast('预览容器不存在'); return; }
            frame.srcdoc = allHtml;
            overlay.classList.add('active');
        }

        function sanitizeHtmlFileName(name) {
            const safeName = String(name || '页面')
                .trim()
                .replace(/[\\/:*?"<>|]/g, '_')
                .replace(/\s+/g, '_')
                .replace(/^\.+/, '')
                .slice(0, 80) || '页面';
            return /\.(html?|HTML?)$/.test(safeName) ? safeName : safeName + '.htm';
        }

        function sanitizePathSegment(name) {
            return String(name || '测试')
                .trim()
                .replace(/[\\/:*?"<>|]/g, '_')
                .replace(/\s+/g, '_')
                .replace(/^\.+/, '')
                .slice(0, 80) || '测试';
        }

        function getAutoEditorOutputDir(taskName) {
            return '/storage/emulated/0/auto-editor/local/' + sanitizePathSegment(taskName) + '/assets';
        }

        function browserDownloadHtml(html, fileName) {
            const blob = new Blob([html], { type: 'text/html;charset=utf-8' });
            const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
            const a = document.createElement('a');
            a.href = url;
            a.download = fileName;
            document.body.appendChild(a);
            a.click();
            document.body.removeChild(a);
            URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
            showToast('✅ 浏览器下载成功: ' + fileName);
        }

        async function writeHtmlToAutoEditor(html, rawName, taskName) {
            const fileName = sanitizeHtmlFileName(rawName);
            const outputDir = getAutoEditorOutputDir(taskName || state.taskName);
            const fullPath = outputDir + '/' + fileName;
            const code = 'file.write(' + JSON.stringify(fullPath) + ',' + JSON.stringify(html) + ');';

            try {
                if (window.autoBridge && typeof window.autoBridge.eval === 'function') {
                    await window.autoBridge.eval(code);
                    showToast('✅ 已写入自动化编辑器: ' + fileName);
                    return true;
                }
                if (window.file && typeof window.file.write === 'function') {
                    window.file.write(fullPath, html);
                    showToast('✅ 已写入自动化编辑器: ' + fileName);
                    return true;
                }
            } catch (err) {
                console.warn('自动化编辑器写入失败，改用浏览器下载', err);
                showToast('⚠️ 自动化写入失败，改用浏览器下载');
            }

            browserDownloadHtml(html, fileName);
            return false;
        }

        async function downloadCurrentHTML() {
            const allPages = state.pages;
            if (allPages.length <= 1) {
                const page = getCurrentPage();
                if (!page) { showToast('没有页面可下载'); return; }
                syncInputValues(page);
                const html = generateHTML();
                await writeHtmlToAutoEditor(html, state.pageName || page.name || '页面', state.taskName || '测试');
                return;
            }
            // 多页面导出：所有页面打包到一个HTML
            syncInputValues(getCurrentPage());
            let fullHtml = generateAllPagesHTML();
            await writeHtmlToAutoEditor(fullHtml, (state.taskName || '测试') + '_全部页面', state.taskName || '测试');
        }

        function syncInputValues(page) {
            if (!page) return;
            page.components.forEach(c => {
                if (c.type === 'input' && c.element) {
                    const el = c.element.querySelector('.input-field');
                    if (el) {
                        state.inputValues[c.id] = el.value;
                        c.props.defaultValue = el.value;
                    }
                }
                if (c.type === 'container' && c.children) {
                    for (const child of c.children) {
                        if (child.type === 'input' && child.element) {
                            const el = child.element.querySelector('.input-field');
                            if (el) {
                                state.inputValues[child.id] = el.value;
                                child.props.defaultValue = el.value;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
                if (c.type === 'pager' && c.props.pages) {
                    for (const pg of c.props.pages) {
                        if (pg.children) {
                            for (const child of pg.children) {
                                if (child.type === 'input' && child.element) {
                                    const el = child.element.querySelector('.input-field');
                                    if (el) {
                                        state.inputValues[child.id] = el.value;
                                        child.props.defaultValue = el.value;
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            });
        }

        function generateAllPagesHTML() {
            const allPages = state.pages;
            const currentPg = getCurrentPage();
            let styles = '', script = '';
            let pagesContent = [];
            allPages.forEach(pg => {
                const tempCurrent = state.currentPageId;
                state.currentPageId = pg.id;
                const html = generateHTML();
                state.currentPageId = tempCurrent;
                const bodyMatch = html.match(/<body[^>]*>([\s\S]*?)<\/body>/i);
                if (bodyMatch) pagesContent.push({ name: pg.name, id: pg.id, body: bodyMatch[1] });
                const styleMatch = html.match(/<style>([\s\S]*?)<\/style>/i);
                if (styleMatch && !styles) styles = styleMatch[1];
                const scriptMatch = html.match(/<script[^>]*>([\s\S]*?)<\/script>/i);
                if (scriptMatch && !script) script = scriptMatch[1];
            });
            let body = '<div style="margin-bottom:0;display:flex;gap:2px;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:flex-end;">';
            pagesContent.forEach((pc, idx) => {
                const isActive = pc.id === currentPg.id;
                body += '<div class="all-page-tab' + (isActive ? ' active' : '') + '" data-pid="' + pc.id + '" style="padding:8px 16px;border-radius:6px 6px 0 0;cursor:pointer;font-size:14px;font-weight:500;user-select:none;transition:all 0.2s;background:' + (isActive ? '#FF6B00' : '#f1f3f4') + ';color:' + (isActive ? '#fff' : '#5f6368') + ';border:1px solid ' + (isActive ? '#FF6B00' : '#dadce0') + ';border-bottom:' + (isActive ? '2px solid #FF6B00' : '1px solid #dadce0') + ';margin-bottom:-1px;white-space:nowrap;">' + escapeHtml(pc.name) + '</div>';
            });
            body += '</div>';
            pagesContent.forEach(pc => {
                const isActive = pc.id === currentPg.id;
                body += '<div class="all-page-content" data-pid="' + pc.id + '" style="display:' + (isActive ? 'block' : 'none') + ';background:#fff;border-radius:0 10px 10px 10px;padding:24px 28px;box-shadow:0 4px 20px rgba(0,0,0,0.06);min-height:500px;position:relative;">' + pc.body + '</div>';
            });
            // 页面切换脚本
            const tabScript = '<script>document.querySelectorAll(".all-page-tab").forEach(function(tab){tab.addEventListener("click",function(){var pid=this.dataset.pid;document.querySelectorAll(".all-page-tab").forEach(function(t){t.style.background="#f1f3f4";t.style.color="#5f6368";t.style.borderColor="#dadce0";t.style.borderBottom="1px solid #dadce0";t.style.marginBottom="-1px";});this.style.background="#FF6B00";this.style.color="#fff";this.style.borderColor="#FF6B00";this.style.borderBottom="2px solid #FF6B00";document.querySelectorAll(".all-page-content").forEach(function(p){p.style.display=p.dataset.pid===pid?"block":"none";});});});<\/script>';
            return '<!DOCTYPE html><html lang="zh-CN"><head><meta charset="UTF-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1"><title>' + escapeHtml(state.taskName || '测试') + '</title><script src="http://www.autoeditor.cn/autoeditor/auto.ui.1.1.js"><\/script><style>' + styles + '</style></head><body>' + body + tabScript + '<script>' + script + '<\/script></body></html>';
        }

        // ================================================================
        //  名称修改
        // ================================================================
        function showNameEditor() {
            openDialog({
                title: '📝 修改名称',
                content: {
                    type: 'nameSettings',
                    taskName: state.taskName || '测试',
                    fileName: state.pageName || '我的主页'
                }
            }).then(result => {
                if (result && result.taskName !== undefined && result.fileName !== undefined) {
                    const taskName = result.taskName.trim();
                    const fileName = result.fileName.trim();
                    if (!taskName) {
                        showToast('任务名称不能为空');
                        return;
                    }
                    if (!fileName) {
                        showToast('文件名称不能为空');
                        return;
                    }
                    state.taskName = taskName;
                    state.pageName = fileName;
                    autoSave();
                    showToast('✅ 名称已更新: ' + state.taskName + ' / ' + state.pageName);
                }
            });
        }

        // ================================================================
        //  导入HTML项目
        // ================================================================
        function importHTMLProject() {
            fileInput.click();
        }

        fileInput.addEventListener('change', function(e) {
            const file = this.files[0];
            if (!file) return;
            const reader = new FileReader();
            reader.onload = function(ev) {
                try {
                    const content = ev.target.result;
                    const parser = new DOMParser();
                    const doc = parser.parseFromString(content, 'text/html');

                    const title = doc.querySelector('title')?.textContent || file.name.replace(/\.[^/.]+$/, '');
                    const pageName = title || '导入页面';

                    let importedInputValues = {};
                    const scripts = doc.querySelectorAll('script');
                    for (const script of scripts) {
                        const text = script.textContent;
                        const match = text.match(/_inputValues\s*=\s*({[\s\S]*?});/);
                        if (match) {
                            try {
                                const data = eval('(' + match[1] + ')');
                                if (data && typeof data === 'object') {
                                    importedInputValues = { ...importedInputValues, ...data };
                                }
                            } catch (e) {}
                        }
                        const match2 = text.match(/var\s+_inputValues\s*=\s*({[\s\S]*?});/);
                        if (match2) {
                            try {
                                const data = eval('(' + match2[1] + ')');
                                if (data && typeof data === 'object') {
                                    importedInputValues = { ...importedInputValues, ...data };
                                }
                            } catch (e) {}
                        }
                    }

                    const page = createPage(pageName);

                    const wrappers = doc.querySelectorAll('.runtime-component');
                    let targetElements = wrappers;
                    if (targetElements.length === 0) {
                        const pageDiv = doc.querySelector('.page');
                        if (pageDiv) {
                            targetElements = pageDiv.children;
                        }
                    }

                    for (const wrapper of targetElements) {
                        let inner = wrapper;
                        if (wrapper.classList && wrapper.classList.contains('runtime-component')) {
                            inner = wrapper.firstElementChild;
                            if (!inner) continue;
                        }

                        const x = parseFloat(inner.dataset.x) || 50;
                        const y = parseFloat(inner.dataset.y) || 50;
                        const width = inner.dataset.width || 'auto';
                        const height = inner.dataset.height || 'auto';
                        const actionType = inner.dataset.action || 'none';
                        let actionParams = {};
                        try { actionParams = JSON.parse(inner.dataset.actionParams || '{}'); } catch (e) {}
                        const compId = parseInt(inner.dataset.id) || uid();
                        const dataType = inner.dataset.type || '';
                        const dataSubtype = inner.dataset.subtype || 'default';
                        const dataToggle = parseInt(inner.dataset.toggleCurrent) || parseInt(inner.dataset.toggle) || 0;
                        const customCss = inner.dataset.customCss || '';
                        const customJs = inner.dataset.customJs || '';

                        const tag = inner.tagName.toLowerCase();
                        const classes = inner.className || '';
                        const style = inner.style;
                        const text = inner.textContent || '';

                        let type = dataType || 'button',
                            subtype = dataSubtype,
                            props = { text: text.trim() || '组件' };

                        if (!dataType) {
                            if (tag === 'button') {
                                type = 'button';
                                if (classes.includes('btn-success')) subtype = 'success';
                                else if (classes.includes('btn-danger')) subtype = 'danger';
                                else if (classes.includes('btn-warning')) subtype = 'warning';
                                else if (classes.includes('btn-outline')) subtype = 'outline';
                                else if (classes.includes('btn-state-1') || classes.includes('btn-state-2') ||
                                    classes.includes('btn-state-3') || classes.includes('btn-state-4')) {
                                    type = 'button';
                                    subtype = 'toggle';
                                    const statesData = inner.dataset.toggleStates;
                                    if (statesData) {
                                        try { props.toggleStates = JSON.parse(statesData); } catch (e) {}
                                    } else { props.toggleStates = ['编辑', '完成', '已保存', '发布']; }
                                } else subtype = 'default';
                                props.text = text.trim() || '按钮';
                            } else if (tag === 'input') {
                                type = 'input';
                                subtype = 'text';
                                props.text = inner.value || '';
                                props.placeholder = inner.placeholder || '输入内容';
                            } else if (classes.includes('msg-box')) {
                                type = 'message';
                                subtype = 'info';
                                props.text = text.trim() || '消息';
                            } else if (classes.includes('effect-demo')) {
                                type = 'effect';
                                subtype = 'fade';
                                if (classes.includes('anim-slide')) subtype = 'slide';
                                else if (classes.includes('anim-bounce')) subtype = 'bounce';
                                else if (classes.includes('anim-rotate')) subtype = 'rotate';
                                props.text = text.trim() || '效果';
                            } else if (classes.includes('app-bar')) {
                                type = 'appbar';
                                subtype = 'top';
                                props.text = text.trim() || '应用栏';
                            } else if (tag === 'img' || classes.includes('img-display')) {
                                type = 'image';
                                subtype = 'default';
                                props.src = inner.src || '';
                                props.alt = inner.alt || '图片';
                            } else if (classes.includes('btn-success') && text.includes('导入')) {
                                type = 'fileinput';
                                subtype = 'default';
                                props.text = text.trim() || '📂 导入数据';
                            } else if (classes.includes('btn-warning') && text.includes('下载')) {
                                type = 'filedownload';
                                subtype = 'default';
                                props.text = text.trim() || '💾 下载数据';
                            } else if (classes.includes('custom') || (style && style.backgroundColor && style
                                    .backgroundColor === '#f0f0f0')) {
                                type = 'custom';
                                subtype = 'default';
                                props.text = text.trim() || '🧩 自定义';
                            } else if (classes.includes('container-wrapper')) {
                                type = 'container';
                                subtype = 'default';
                                props.text = text.trim() || '容器';
                                props.bgColor = style.backgroundColor || '#f5f0e8';
                            } else if (classes.includes('counter-display') || inner.closest('.counter-display')) {
                                type = 'counter';
                                subtype = 'default';
                                const display = inner.closest('.counter-display') || inner;
                                props.value = parseInt(display.textContent) || 0;
                                props.text = '计数';
                            } else if (classes.includes('quiz-container')) {
                                type = 'quiz';
                                subtype = 'default';
                                const titleEl = inner.querySelector('.quiz-title');
                                if (titleEl) props.title = titleEl.textContent.trim() || '选择题';
                                // 尝试解析题目
                                const questions = [];
                                const qBlocks = inner.querySelectorAll('.quiz-question');
                                qBlocks.forEach((qBlock, idx) => {
                                    const qTextEl = qBlock.querySelector('.q-text');
                                    const qText = qTextEl ? qTextEl.textContent.replace(/^\d+\.\s*/, '').trim() :
                                        `题目 ${idx+1}`;
                                    const typeSpan = qBlock.querySelector('.q-type');
                                    const qType = typeSpan && typeSpan.textContent === '多选题' ? 'multi' :
                                        'single';
                                    const opts = [];
                                    const corrects = [];
                                    const optEls = qBlock.querySelectorAll('.q-opt');
                                    optEls.forEach((optEl, oi) => {
                                        const label = optEl.querySelector('label');
                                        if (label) opts.push(label.textContent.replace(/^[A-Z]\.\s*/, '')
                                            .trim());
                                        const mark = optEl.querySelector('.q-mark');
                                        if (mark && mark.textContent === '✅') corrects.push(oi);
                                    });
                                    if (corrects.length === 0 && qType === 'single') corrects.push(0);
                                    questions.push({
                                        id: idx + 1,
                                        text: qText,
                                        options: opts.length > 0 ? opts : ['选项A', '选项B', '选项C',
                                        '选项D'],
                                        correct: corrects.length > 0 ? corrects : [0],
                                        type: qType
                                    });
                                });
                                if (questions.length > 0) props.questions = questions;
                            }
                        }

                        const comp = createComponentData(type, subtype, props);
                        comp.x = x;
                        comp.y = y;
                        comp.width = width;
                        comp.height = height;
                        comp.toggleState = dataToggle;
                        comp.id = compId;
                        comp.customCss = customCss;
                        comp.customJs = customJs;
                        comp.userAnswers = {};

                        if (style.color) comp.props.color = style.color;
                        if (style.backgroundColor) comp.props.bgColor = style.backgroundColor;
                        if (style.fontSize) comp.props.fontSize = parseInt(style.fontSize) || 14;
                        if (style.width && style.width !== 'auto') comp.props.width = style.width;
                        if (text.trim()) comp.props.text = text.trim();

                        if (type === 'input') {
                            if (inner.value !== undefined && inner.value !== '') {
                                comp.props.text = inner.value;
                                state.inputValues[comp.id] = inner.value;
                            }
                            if (inner.placeholder) comp.props.placeholder = inner.placeholder;
                        }
                        if (type === 'image') {
                            if (inner.src) comp.props.src = inner.src;
                            if (inner.alt) comp.props.alt = inner.alt;
                        }
                        if (type === 'button' && subtype === 'toggle') {
                            const statesData = inner.dataset.toggleStates;
                            if (statesData) {
                                try { comp.props.toggleStates = JSON.parse(statesData); } catch (e) {}
                            }
                            comp.toggleState = dataToggle;
                            const states = comp.props.toggleStates || ['编辑', '完成', '已保存', '发布'];
                            if (dataToggle < states.length) comp.props.text = states[dataToggle];
                            if (actionType && actionType !== 'none') {
                                comp.action = { type: actionType, params: actionParams };
                            } else {
                                comp.action = { type: 'toggleState', params: {} };
                            }
                        } else if (actionType && actionType !== 'none') {
                            comp.action = { type: actionType, params: actionParams };
                        }
                        if (actionType === 'none') comp.action = { type: 'none', params: {} };

                        page.components.push(comp);
                    }

                    if (Object.keys(importedInputValues).length > 0) {
                        state.inputValues = { ...state.inputValues, ...importedInputValues };
                    }

                    switchPage(page.id);
                    autoSave();
                    ensureCanvasSize();
                    showToast('✅ 导入成功: ' + page.name + ' (共 ' + page.components.length + ' 个组件)');
                } catch (err) {
                    showToast('❌ 导入失败: ' + err.message);
                    console.error(err);
                }
            };
            reader.readAsText(file);
            this.value = '';
        });

        // ================================================================
        //  数据导入导出
        // ================================================================
        dataFileInput.addEventListener('change', function(e) {
            const file = this.files[0];
            if (!file) return;
            importInputData(file);
            this.value = '';
        });

        // ================================================================
        //  清空全部
        // ================================================================
        function clearAll() {
            openDialog({
                title: '🗑 清空全部',
                content: '确定要删除所有页面和内容吗？此操作不可撤销！',
                type: 'custom'
            }).then(result => {
                if (result !== null) {
                    state.pages = [];
                    state.inputValues = {};
                    state.selectedId = null;
                    const newPage = createPage('首页');
                    state.currentPageId = newPage.id;
                    renderPage(newPage);
                    updatePageTabs();
                    updatePropertiesPanel();
                    updateCodePanel();
                    autoSave();
                    showToast('✅ 已清空全部页面');
                }
            });
        }

        // ================================================================
        //  保存条件
        // ================================================================
        function handleSaveCondition(triggerId) {
            if (state.saveConditions.trigger === triggerId) {
                const msg = state.saveConditions.message || '已保存';
                const dur = state.saveConditions.duration || 1000;
                showToast('💾 ' + msg, dur);
                return true;
            }
            return false;
        }

        // ================================================================
        //  拖拽添加
        // ================================================================
        function initDragFromPanel() {
            const items = document.querySelectorAll('.comp-item');
            items.forEach(item => {
                item.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
                    if (e.button !== 0) return;
                    startDragFromPanel(e.clientX, e.clientY, item);
                });
                item.addEventListener('touchstart', (e) => {
                    const t = e.touches[0];
                    startDragFromPanel(t.clientX, t.clientY, item);
                    e.preventDefault();
                }, { passive: false });
            });
        }

        function startDragFromPanel(clientX, clientY, item) {
            const type = item.dataset.type;
            const subtype = item.dataset.subtype;
            const label = item.querySelector('.label')?.textContent || type;
            const clone = document.createElement('div');
            clone.className = 'drag-clone';
            clone.textContent = label;
            document.body.appendChild(clone);
            clone.style.left = (clientX - 30) + 'px';
            clone.style.top = (clientY - 18) + 'px';

            const onMove = (ev) => {
                const cx = ev.clientX || ev.touches?.[0]?.clientX || clientX;
                const cy = ev.clientY || ev.touches?.[0]?.clientY || clientY;
                clone.style.left = (cx - 30) + 'px';
                clone.style.top = (cy - 18) + 'px';
                const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
                clone.style.borderColor = (cx >= rect.left && cx <= rect.right && cy >= rect.top && cy <= rect
                    .bottom) ? '#34a853' : 'var(--primary)';
                clone.style.opacity = (cx >= rect.left && cx <= rect.right && cy >= rect.top && cy <= rect.bottom) ?
                    '0.9' : '0.7';
            };

            const onUp = (ev) => {
                const cx = ev.clientX || ev.changedTouches?.[0]?.clientX || clientX;
                const cy = ev.clientY || ev.changedTouches?.[0]?.clientY || clientY;
                document.removeEventListener('mousemove', onMove);
                document.removeEventListener('mouseup', onUp);
                document.removeEventListener('touchmove', onMove);
                document.removeEventListener('touchend', onUp);
                clone.remove();
                const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
                if (cx >= rect.left && cx <= rect.right && cy >= rect.top && cy <= rect.bottom) {
                    // 检查是否拖到了分页组件的页面区域
                    const targetEl = document.elementFromPoint(cx, cy);
                    const pagerArea = targetEl?.closest('.pager-page-area');
                    const containerInner = targetEl?.closest('.container-inner');
                    if (pagerArea) {
                        const pagerComp = pagerArea.closest('.canvas-component');
                        if (pagerComp) {
                            const pagerId = parseInt(pagerComp.dataset.id);
                            const pagerData = getComponent(pagerId);
                            if (pagerData && pagerData.type === 'pager') {
                                const pages = pagerData.props.pages || [];
                                const activeIdx = pagerData.props.activeIndex || 0;
                                if (!pages[activeIdx].children) pages[activeIdx].children = [];
                                const newComp = createComponentData(type, subtype);
                                newComp.x = 5;
                                newComp.y = 5;
                                newComp.parentId = pagerId;
                                pages[activeIdx].children.push(newComp);
                                refreshComponent(pagerData);
                                selectComponent(newComp.id);
                                autoSave();
                                showToast('✅ 已添加到分页: ' + (pages[activeIdx].name || ''));
                                return;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    if (containerInner) {
                        const containerComp = containerInner.closest('.canvas-component');
                        if (containerComp) {
                            const containerId = parseInt(containerComp.dataset.id);
                            const containerData = getComponent(containerId);
                            if (containerData && containerData.type === 'container') {
                                if (!containerData.children) containerData.children = [];
                                const newComp = createComponentData(type, subtype);
                                newComp.x = 5;
                                newComp.y = 5;
                                newComp.parentId = containerId;
                                containerData.children.push(newComp);
                                refreshComponent(containerData);
                                selectComponent(newComp.id);
                                autoSave();
                                showToast('✅ 已添加到容器');
                                return;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    if (item.id === 'customCompBtn') {
                        createCustomComponent();
                    } else {
                        addComponent(type, subtype);
                    }
                }
            };

            document.addEventListener('mousemove', onMove);
            document.addEventListener('mouseup', onUp);
            document.addEventListener('touchmove', onMove, { passive: true });
            document.addEventListener('touchend', onUp, { passive: true });
        }

        // ================================================================
        //  初始化
        // ================================================================
        function init() {
            state.defaultGlobalStyles = DEFAULT_GLOBAL_STYLES;
            state.defaultGlobalScripts = DEFAULT_GLOBAL_SCRIPTS;

            loadTemplatesFromStorage();

            document.getElementById('customCompBtn').addEventListener('click', createCustomComponent);

            const loaded = loadStateFromStorage();

            if (loaded && state.pages.length > 0) {
                if (!getPage(state.currentPageId)) {
                    state.currentPageId = state.pages[0].id;
                }
                const page = getCurrentPage();
                renderPage(page);
                updatePageTabs();
                updatePropertiesPanel();
                updateCodePanel();
                ensureCanvasSize();
                showToast('📂 已恢复上次编辑');
            } else {
                const defaultPage = createPage('首页');
                state.currentPageId = defaultPage.id;

                // 添加示例组件
                const id1 = addComponent('button', 'default', { text: '🎯 弹窗演示' });
                const id2 = addComponent('button', 'default', { text: '💾 保存数据' });
                const id3 = addComponent('message', 'info', { text: '👋 点击我弹出消息' });
                const id4 = addComponent('input', 'text', { placeholder: '输入框内容' });
                const id5 = addComponent('button', 'default', { text: '📄 切换页面' });
                const id6 = addComponent('image', 'default', { src: 'https://via.placeholder.com/300x200/FF6B00/fff?text=示例图片',
                    alt: '示例图片' });
                const id7 = addComponent('fileinput', 'default', { text: '📂 导入数据' });
                const id8 = addComponent('filedownload', 'default', { text: '💾 下载数据' });
                const id9 = addComponent('custom', 'default', {
                    text: '🧩 自定义示例',
                    customCss: 'background: #e8f0fe; border-radius: 8px; padding: 6px 12px;',
                    customJs: 'showToast("🧩 自定义组件被点击了！");'
                });
                const id10 = addComponent('container', 'default', {});
                const id11 = addComponent('counter', 'default', { value: 5 });
                const id12 = addComponent('quiz', 'default', {});

                const comp1 = getComponent(id1);
                if (comp1) {
                    comp1.action = {
                        type: 'dialog',
                        params: {
                            title: '🎯 弹窗演示',
                            content: '这是一个自定义弹窗！点击确定后弹出消息。',
                            options: '确定, 取消',
                            nextAction: 'message',
                            nextParams: { text: '✅ 你点击了确定！', duration: 2 }
                        }
                    };
                    comp1.x = 60;
                    comp1.y = 30;
                }
                const comp2 = getComponent(id2);
                if (comp2) {
                    comp2.action = { type: 'save', params: { message: '✅ 数据已保存', duration: 1.2 } };
                    comp2.x = 60;
                    comp2.y = 90;
                }
                const comp3 = getComponent(id3);
                if (comp3) {
                    comp3.action = { type: 'message', params: { text: '👋 这是一条消息！', duration: 2 } };
                    comp3.x = 60;
                    comp3.y = 150;
                }
                const comp4 = getComponent(id4);
                if (comp4) { comp4.x = 60;
                    comp4.y = 210; }
                const comp5 = getComponent(id5);
                if (comp5) {
                    comp5.x = 60;
                    comp5.y = 270;
                    comp5.action = {
                        type: 'switchPage',
                        params: { targetPage: state.pages.length > 1 ? state.pages[1].name : '' }
                    };
                }
                const comp6 = getComponent(id6);
                if (comp6) { comp6.x = 300;
                    comp6.y = 30; }
                const comp7 = getComponent(id7);
                if (comp7) { comp7.x = 60;
                    comp7.y = 330; }
                const comp8 = getComponent(id8);
                if (comp8) { comp8.x = 220;
                    comp8.y = 330; }
                const comp9 = getComponent(id9);
                if (comp9) { comp9.x = 300;
                    comp9.y = 200; }
                const comp10 = getComponent(id10);
                if (comp10) { comp10.x = 30;
                    comp10.y = 400;
                    comp10.props.text = '容器 1';
                    comp10.width = 300;
                    comp10.height = 200; }
                const comp11 = getComponent(id11);
                if (comp11) { comp11.x = 350;
                    comp11.y = 400;
                    comp11.props.value = 5;
                    comp11.customJs = 'showToast("计数器值变化: " + comp.props.value);'; }
                const comp12 = getComponent(id12);
                if (comp12) { comp12.x = 500;
                    comp12.y = 30;
                    comp12.width = 320;
                    comp12.height = 280; }

                const container = getComponent(id10);
                if (container) {
                    const child1 = createComponentData('button', 'default', { text: '容器内按钮' });
                    child1.x = 10;
                    child1.y = 30;
                    child1.parentId = container.id;
                    child1.action = { type: 'message', params: { text: '容器内按钮被点击！' } };
                    container.children.push(child1);
                    const child2 = createComponentData('input', 'text', { placeholder: '容器内输入' });
                    child2.x = 10;
                    child2.y = 80;
                    child2.parentId = container.id;
                    container.children.push(child2);
                    refreshComponent(container);
                }

                state.selectedId = null;
                renderPage(defaultPage);
                updatePropertiesPanel();
                updatePageTabs();
                updateCodePanel();
                ensureCanvasSize();
                autoSave();
            }

            // 事件
            document.getElementById('btnRun').addEventListener('click', runPreview);
            document.getElementById('previewBackBtn').addEventListener('click', () => {
                const overlay = document.getElementById('previewOverlay');
                const frame = document.getElementById('previewFrame');
                if (frame) frame.srcdoc = '';
                if (overlay) overlay.classList.remove('active');
            });
            document.getElementById('btnDownload').addEventListener('click', downloadCurrentHTML);
            document.getElementById('btnName').addEventListener('click', showNameEditor);
            document.getElementById('btnImport').addEventListener('click', importHTMLProject);
            document.getElementById('btnClear').addEventListener('click', clearAll);

            document.getElementById('btnArrange').addEventListener('click', arrangeComponents);

            document.getElementById('btnTemplate').addEventListener('click', showTemplateDialog);

            document.getElementById('btnToggleCode').addEventListener('click', () => {
                state.codeOpen = !state.codeOpen;
                codePanel.classList.toggle('open', state.codeOpen);
                document.getElementById('btnToggleCode').textContent = state.codeOpen ? '📄 隐藏' : '📄 代码';
                if (state.codeOpen) updateCodePanel();
            });

            codeApplyBtn.addEventListener('click', applyCodeFromPanel);

            codeGlobalBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
                state.showGlobalCode = !state.showGlobalCode;
                updateCodePanel();
                if (state.showGlobalCode && !state.codeOpen) {
                    state.codeOpen = true;
                    codePanel.classList.add('open');
                    document.getElementById('btnToggleCode').textContent = '📄 隐藏';
                }
            });

            addPageBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
                const name = prompt('请输入页面名称：', '页面 ' + (state.pages.length + 1));
                if (name !== null && name.trim()) {
                    const page = createPage(name.trim());
                    switchPage(page.id);
                    showToast('✅ 已创建: ' + page.name);
                } else if (name !== null) {
                    const page = createPage();
                    switchPage(page.id);
                }
            });

            document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
                if ((e.key === 'Delete' || e.key === 'Backspace') && state.selectedId !== null && !e.target
                    .closest('input, textarea, select')) {
                    deleteComponent(state.selectedId);
                    e.preventDefault();
                }
                if (e.key === 'Escape' && state.selectedId !== null) {
                    const page = getCurrentPage();
                    if (page) {
                        const old = page.components.find(c => c.id === state.selectedId);
                        if (old && old.element) old.element.classList.remove('selected');
                        for (const c of page.components) {
                            if (c.type === 'container' && c.children) {
                                const child = c.children.find(ch => ch.id === state.selectedId);
                                if (child && child.element) child.element.classList.remove('selected');
                            }
                        }
                        state.selectedId = null;
                        updatePropertiesPanel();
                        updateCodePanel();
                    }
                }
                if (e.ctrlKey && e.key === 's') { e.preventDefault();
                    downloadCurrentHTML(); }
                if (e.ctrlKey && e.key === 'Enter') { e.preventDefault();
                    runPreview(); }
            });

            initDragFromPanel();

            canvas.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                const btn = e.target.closest('.canvas-component .btn');
                if (btn) {
                    const compEl = btn.closest('.canvas-component');
                    if (compEl) {
                        const id = parseInt(compEl.dataset.id);
                        if (!isNaN(id)) {
                            const comp = getComponent(id);
                            if (comp && (!comp.action || comp.action.type === 'none') && !comp.customJs) {
                                handleSaveCondition(id);
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            });

            canvas.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
                if (e.target === canvas || e.target.classList.contains('empty-hint')) {
                    if (state.selectedId !== null) {
                        const page = getCurrentPage();
                        if (page) {
                            const old = page.components.find(c => c.id === state.selectedId);
                            if (old && old.element) old.element.classList.remove('selected');
                            for (const c of page.components) {
                                if (c.type === 'container' && c.children) {
                                    const child = c.children.find(ch => ch.id === state.selectedId);
                                    if (child && child.element) child.element.classList.remove('selected');
                                }
                            }
                            state.selectedId = null;
                            updatePropertiesPanel();
                            updateCodePanel();
                        }
                    }
                }
            });

            console.log('🧩 逆风UI · 无限画布已启动 (v4)');
            console.log('✅ 容器组件: 移入/移出/滚动');
            console.log('✅ 计数器组件: 默认值/代码');
            console.log('✅ 答题组件: 编辑题目/选项/交卷评分');
            console.log('✅ 模板应用: ID映射修复');
            console.log('✅ 新动作: 隐藏/显示, 禁止/允许点击, 替换组件');
        }

        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', init);
    </script>
</body>
</html>
